======================================================================
FILE: Launch_DarkShadow.bat
======================================================================
@echo off
setlocal enabledelayedexpansion
title DarkShadow Desktop AI - Gemini 3.8 Live
color 0b

:: Suppress harmless Qt DPI warnings
set "QT_LOGGING_RULES=qt.qpa.*=false;qt.webenginecontext.*=false"

echo ===================================================================
echo             DarkShadow Desktop AI (Gemini 3.8 Live WebSocket)
echo ===================================================================
echo.

cd /d "%~dp0"

:: 1. Detect Python or Virtual Environment
set "PYTHON_EXE="
if exist "%~dp0.venv\Scripts\python.exe" (
    set "PYTHON_EXE=%~dp0.venv\Scripts\python.exe"
) else if exist "F:\darkshadow\.venv\Scripts\python.exe" (
    set "PYTHON_EXE=F:\darkshadow\.venv\Scripts\python.exe"
) else if exist "%~dp0darkshadow-main\.venv\Scripts\python.exe" (
    set "PYTHON_EXE=%~dp0darkshadow-main\.venv\Scripts\python.exe"
) else (
    set "PYTHON_EXE=python"
)

:: 2. Locate desktop_python directory
set "DESKTOP_DIR="
if exist "%~dp0desktop_python\darkshadow_desktop.py" (
    set "DESKTOP_DIR=%~dp0desktop_python"
) else if exist "%~dp0darkshadow-main\desktop_python\darkshadow_desktop.py" (
    set "DESKTOP_DIR=%~dp0darkshadow-main\desktop_python"
) else if exist "%~dp0darkshadow_desktop.py" (
    set "DESKTOP_DIR=%~dp0"
)

if "%DESKTOP_DIR%"=="" (
    echo [ERROR] darkshadow_desktop.py not found!
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

cd /d "%DESKTOP_DIR%"

echo [OK] Launching DarkShadow Native Voice-to-Voice Core...
echo [i] Model: gemini-3.8-live (Bidirectional WebSocket)
echo [i] Voice: Charon (Deep Baritone, Jarvis Tone)
echo [i] Tip: DarkShadow runs behind your windows on the desktop.
echo          Minimize this black terminal window to see the 3D Starlight Orb!
echo.

"%PYTHON_EXE%" darkshadow_desktop.py
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo.
    echo [!] Application exited with code %errorlevel%.
    pause
)



======================================================================
FILE: README.md
======================================================================
# DarkShadow

DarkShadow is a personal AI co-pilot web application for Captain, migrated from the original Python/PySide6 Windows desktop assistant to a Node.js + React + TypeScript full-stack web app.

## Features

- **Panoramic Single-Orb Cyber HUD**: Preserves DarkShadow's pure obsidian theme (`#000000` / `#070A10` / `#765CFF` / `#00D2FF`), fluid orb core (`darkshadow_core.gif`), state-aware canvas rings, blinking glowing eyes, and 25-bar live audio waveform.
- **Typed & Push-to-Talk Voice Modes**: Supports Hindi, English, and Hinglish via browser Web Speech Recognition (`hi-IN` / `en-IN`), press `Space` or click the orb to speak, plus interruptible Gemini TTS (`gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts` with `Charon` voice) with automatic browser speech synthesis fallback.
- **Dual-Model Gemini Routing**: Server-side `@google/genai` integration automatically routes complex/technical prompts to `gemini-4-hydrogen` and casual prompts to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`.
- **Fresh Information Grounding**: Time-sensitive queries (weather, news, prices, scores, locations) route through Tavily Search (when `TAVILY_API_KEY` is configured) or Gemini Google Search Grounding with verified source citations.
- **Opt-In Memory, Notes & Reminders**: Deterministic commands (`/remember`, `/note`, `/memories`, `/notes`, `/forget`, `/remind`, `/reminders`, `/delete-reminder`) with sensitive-data filtering and a session Privacy Mode toggle in Settings.
- **Safety & Privacy Shields**: Payment/checkout exclusion, zero-camera privacy enforcement, and dangerous shell command blocking run deterministically before any LLM call.

## Setup & Commands

```bash
npm install
npm run dev      # Starts Express + Vite dev server on http://0.0.0.0:3000
npm run build    # Builds production frontend bundle to dist/
npm run start    # Runs production server on port 3000
npm run lint     # Runs TypeScript type checking
```

Configure environment variables in `.env` (see `.env.example`):

- `GEMINI_API_KEY` — Gemini API key (configured via AI Studio Secrets)
- `TAVILY_API_KEY` — Optional Tavily Search API key

======================================================================
FILE: server.ts
======================================================================
/**
 * DarkShadow Web Server (Express + Vite dev middleware / static production server)
 * Listens on 0.0.0.0:3000.
 */

import "dotenv/config";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import express from "express";
import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import { buildZipBuffer } from "./server/zip.ts";
import { handleDeterministicAutomation } from "./server/automation.ts";
import {
  generateChatReply,
  getCasualModel,
  getIntelligentModel,
  getTtsModel,
  getTtsVoice,
  isGeminiConfigured,
  synthesizeSpeechWav,
} from "./server/gemini.ts";
import type { ConversationTurn } from "./server/gemini.ts";
import { handleMemoryCommand, MemoryStore } from "./server/memory.ts";
import { answerFreshQuery, requiresFreshInformation } from "./server/realtime.ts";
import {
  CAMERA_BLOCKED_MESSAGE,
  ensureCaptainAddress,
  isDangerousTerminalCommand,
  isPaymentOrCheckoutIntent,
  isPhysicalCameraIntent,
  PAYMENT_BLOCKED_MESSAGE,
  speechFriendlyText,
} from "./server/safety.ts";

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);
const MAX_HISTORY_TURNS = Math.max(2, Number(process.env.DARKSHADOW_HISTORY_TURNS || 8));

const memoryStore = new MemoryStore();
let conversationHistory: ConversationTurn[] = [];
let lastModelUsed: string | null = null;
let totalInputTokens = 0;
let totalOutputTokens = 0;

function buildHelpText(): string {
  return [
    "Captain, DarkShadow commands:",
    "• /help — show available commands and capabilities",
    "• /status — show safe runtime, model, search & shield status",
    "• /clear — clear bounded in-memory conversation history",
    "• /remember <text> or 'remember this: ...' — save one explicit preference",
    "• /note <text> or 'note: ...' — save one explicit note",
    "• /memories & /notes — list saved items",
    "• /forget <id> — delete one saved item",
    "• remind me tomorrow at 09:00 to ... — schedule a reminder",
    "• /reminders & /delete-reminder <id> — manage pending reminders",
    "• Web actions: current time, today's date, system status, google search <q>, play <q> on YouTube, open <site.com>",
  ].join("\n");
}

function buildStatusSummary(): string {
  const geminiReady = isGeminiConfigured() ? "connected" : "missing key";
  const searchMode = process.env.TAVILY_API_KEY?.trim()
    ? "Tavily Search + Gemini Grounding"
    : "Gemini Google Search Grounding";
  return [
    `Models: intelligent=${getIntelligentModel()} | casual=${getCasualModel()}`,
    `Last model: ${lastModelUsed ?? "none yet"} | Tokens: ${totalInputTokens + totalOutputTokens} (in=${totalInputTokens}, out=${totalOutputTokens})`,
    `Gemini API: ${geminiReady} | Live info: ${searchMode}`,
    `Voice TTS: ${getTtsModel()} (${getTtsVoice()}) + Browser Web Speech fallback`,
    `History turns: ${conversationHistory.length}/${MAX_HISTORY_TURNS} | Saved memories: ${memoryStore.listItems().length} | Pending reminders: ${memoryStore.listPendingReminders().length}`,
    "Shields: Payment Exclusion ACTIVE | Zero-Camera Privacy ACTIVE | Dangerous Shell Shield ACTIVE",
  ].join("\n");
}

async function startServer() {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json({ limit: "1mb" }));

  // Serve DarkShadow's core orb GIF & public assets
  app.use("/assets", express.static(path.join(__dirname, "public", "assets")));

  // Download endpoints for the Option B Windows 11 Python + WebGL2 Desktop Suite (all 5 upgrades)
  const desktopFiles: Record<string, string> = {
    "darkshadow_desktop.py": "text/x-python; charset=utf-8",
    "particle_engine.html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
    "voice_engine.py": "text/x-python; charset=utf-8",
    "windows_tools.py": "text/x-python; charset=utf-8",
    "memory_engine.py": "text/x-python; charset=utf-8",
    "sentinel_monitor.py": "text/x-python; charset=utf-8",
    "windows_requirements.txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
    "run_darkshadow_desktop.bat": "application/x-bat; charset=utf-8",
    ".env.example": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
  };

  app.get("/api/desktop-files/DarkShadow-Desktop-Python.zip", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const dir = path.join(__dirname, "desktop_python");
    const entries = Object.keys(desktopFiles)
      .filter((name) => fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, name)))
      .map((name) => ({
        name: `DarkShadow-Desktop/${name}`,
        data: fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, name)),
      }));
    const zip = buildZipBuffer(entries);
    res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/zip");
    res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", 'attachment; filename="DarkShadow-Desktop-Python.zip"');
    res.send(zip);
  });

  app.get("/api/desktop-files/:filename", (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const filename = String(req.params.filename || "");
    const contentType = desktopFiles[filename];
    if (!contentType) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "File not found" });
      return;
    }
    const filePath = path.join(__dirname, "desktop_python", filename);
    res.setHeader("Content-Type", contentType);
    res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
    res.sendFile(filePath);
  });

  app.get("/api/status", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    res.json({
      geminiConfigured: isGeminiConfigured(),
      intelligentModel: getIntelligentModel(),
      casualModel: getCasualModel(),
      lastModelUsed,
      ttsModel: getTtsModel(),
      ttsVoice: getTtsVoice(),
      searchMode: process.env.TAVILY_API_KEY?.trim() ? "tavily" : "gemini_grounding",
      historyTurns: conversationHistory.length,
      maxHistoryTurns: MAX_HISTORY_TURNS,
      memoryCount: memoryStore.listItems().length,
      pendingReminderCount: memoryStore.listPendingReminders().length,
      summary: buildStatusSummary(),
    });
  });

  app.get("/api/reminders", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const due = memoryStore.popDueReminders();
    const pending = memoryStore.listPendingReminders();
    res.json({ pending, due });
  });

  app.delete("/api/reminders/:id", (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const id = Number.parseInt(String(req.params.id), 10);
    if (Number.isNaN(id)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid reminder id" });
      return;
    }
    const result = memoryStore.deleteReminder(id);
    res.json({ result, pending: memoryStore.listPendingReminders() });
  });

  app.get("/api/memories", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    res.json({ items: memoryStore.listItems() });
  });

  app.delete("/api/memories/:id", (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const id = Number.parseInt(String(req.params.id), 10);
    if (Number.isNaN(id)) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Invalid memory id" });
      return;
    }
    const result = memoryStore.forgetItem(id);
    res.json({ result, items: memoryStore.listItems() });
  });

  app.post("/api/clear", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    conversationHistory = [];
    res.json({
      displayText: "Captain, in-memory conversation context clear kar diya hai.",
      speechText: speechFriendlyText("Captain, in-memory conversation context clear kar diya hai."),
      intent: "clear",
    });
  });

  app.post("/api/tts", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const text = typeof req.body?.text === "string" ? req.body.text.trim() : "";
    if (!text) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Text is required" });
      return;
    }
    try {
      const audioBase64 = await synthesizeSpeechWav(text);
      if (!audioBase64) {
        res.status(503).json({ error: "Cloud TTS unavailable" });
        return;
      }
      res.json({ audioBase64, mimeType: "audio/wav", voice: getTtsVoice() });
    } catch (err) {
      const detail = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "TTS failed";
      res.status(502).json({ error: detail });
    }
  });

  app.post("/api/chat", async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const rawMessage = typeof req.body?.message === "string" ? req.body.message.trim() : "";
    const privacyMode = Boolean(req.body?.privacyMode);

    if (!rawMessage) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Message is required" });
      return;
    }

    // 1. Hard Safety Shields (zero LLM tokens spent)
    if (isPaymentOrCheckoutIntent(rawMessage)) {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(PAYMENT_BLOCKED_MESSAGE);
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "payment_blocked",
        emotion: "serious",
        suggestions: ["Compare features instead", "Show /help"],
        sources: [],
        error: "payment_blocked",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (isPhysicalCameraIntent(rawMessage)) {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(CAMERA_BLOCKED_MESSAGE);
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "camera_blocked",
        emotion: "serious",
        suggestions: ["Show /status", "Show /help"],
        sources: [],
        error: "camera_blocked",
      });
      return;
    }

    const terminalCheck = isDangerousTerminalCommand(rawMessage);
    if (terminalCheck.blocked) {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(terminalCheck.reason);
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "security_blocked",
        emotion: "serious",
        suggestions: [],
        sources: [],
        error: "security_blocked",
      });
      return;
    }

    // 2. Core Slash Commands
    const lower = rawMessage.toLowerCase();
    if (lower === "/help") {
      const displayText = buildHelpText();
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText("Captain, commands list screen par dikha di hai."),
        intent: "help",
        emotion: "neutral",
        suggestions: ["/status", "/memories", "/reminders"],
        sources: [],
      });
      return;
    }

    if (lower === "/clear") {
      conversationHistory = [];
      const displayText = "Captain, in-memory conversation history clear kar di hai.";
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "clear",
        emotion: "neutral",
        suggestions: ["Namaste DarkShadow", "/status"],
        sources: [],
        clearHistory: true,
      });
      return;
    }

    if (lower === "/status") {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(buildStatusSummary());
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText("Captain, DarkShadow status report ready hai."),
        intent: "status",
        emotion: "neutral",
        suggestions: ["/help", "/reminders"],
        sources: [],
      });
      return;
    }

    if (lower === "/mode") {
      const displayText =
        "Captain, aap DarkShadow Web HUD mein hain — typed aur push-to-talk voice dono active hain. Space dabakar ya Orb click karke bol sakte hain.";
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "mode",
        emotion: "neutral",
        suggestions: ["/status", "/help"],
        sources: [],
      });
      return;
    }

    // 3. Deterministic Memory, Notes & Reminders
    const activeMemory = privacyMode ? null : memoryStore;
    const memoryRes = handleMemoryCommand(rawMessage, activeMemory);
    if (memoryRes) {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(memoryRes.message);
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: memoryRes.action,
        emotion: memoryRes.success ? "neutral" : "serious",
        suggestions: memoryRes.action === "remind" ? ["/reminders"] : ["/memories", "/reminders"],
        sources: [],
        reminders: memoryStore.listPendingReminders(),
        error: memoryRes.error,
      });
      return;
    }

    // 4. Deterministic Safe Automation
    const autoRes = handleDeterministicAutomation(rawMessage);
    if (autoRes) {
      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(autoRes.message);
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: autoRes.action,
        emotion: autoRes.success ? "neutral" : "serious",
        suggestions: [],
        sources: [],
        actionLink: autoRes.actionLink,
        error: autoRes.error,
      });
      return;
    }

    // Model-info quick deterministic query (matches chatbot.py)
    if (
      /\b(?:which|what|kaun\s*sa|kaunsa|konsa)\b.*\bmodel\b|\bmodel\b.*\b(?:use|using|chala|kar\s+rahe)\b/i.test(
        rawMessage
      )
    ) {
      const activeM = lastModelUsed ?? getIntelligentModel();
      const displayText = `Captain, main abhi ${activeM} model use kar raha hoon. intelligent=${getIntelligentModel()} | casual=${getCasualModel()} | tokens=${totalInputTokens + totalOutputTokens} (in=${totalInputTokens}, out=${totalOutputTokens}).`;
      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "status",
        emotion: "neutral",
        suggestions: ["/status"],
        sources: [],
      });
      return;
    }

    // 5. Fresh Information Routing (Tavily or Gemini Google Search Grounding)
    if (!isGeminiConfigured() && !process.env.TAVILY_API_KEY?.trim()) {
      const displayText =
        "Captain, GEMINI_API_KEY configured nahi mila. Settings > Secrets mein apni Gemini API key check kijiye; local commands (/help, /status, notes, reminders, time) tab tak available rahenge.";
      res.status(503).json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: "configuration",
        emotion: "serious",
        suggestions: ["/help", "/status"],
        sources: [],
        error: "missing_api_key",
      });
      return;
    }

    try {
      if (requiresFreshInformation(rawMessage)) {
        const fresh = await answerFreshQuery(rawMessage);
        conversationHistory.push({ userText: rawMessage, assistantText: fresh.displayText });
        if (conversationHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY_TURNS) {
          conversationHistory = conversationHistory.slice(-MAX_HISTORY_TURNS);
        }
        res.json({
          displayText: fresh.displayText,
          speechText: fresh.speechText,
          intent: fresh.intent,
          emotion: "neutral",
          suggestions: ["Aur detail batao", "Latest update check karo"],
          sources: fresh.sources,
          modelUsed: fresh.provider === "tavily" ? "tavily-search" : getIntelligentModel(),
        });
        return;
      }

      // 6. Dual-Model Conversational Gemini Reply
      const recalled = activeMemory ? activeMemory.recallRelevant(rawMessage, 3) : [];
      const reply = await generateChatReply({
        message: rawMessage,
        history: conversationHistory,
        recalledMemories: recalled,
      });

      const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(reply.text);
      lastModelUsed = reply.modelUsed;
      totalInputTokens += reply.inputTokens;
      totalOutputTokens += reply.outputTokens;

      conversationHistory.push({ userText: rawMessage, assistantText: displayText });
      if (conversationHistory.length > MAX_HISTORY_TURNS) {
        conversationHistory = conversationHistory.slice(-MAX_HISTORY_TURNS);
      }

      res.json({
        displayText,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
        intent: `chat_${reply.profile.mode}`,
        emotion: reply.profile.emotion,
        suggestions: reply.profile.suggestions,
        sources: [],
        modelUsed: reply.modelUsed,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      const rawError = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      let friendly =
        "Captain, AI engine se connect karte waqt error aaya. Kripya thodi der baad dobara try kijiye.";
      if (/API_KEY_INVALID|PERMISSION_DENIED|403|401/i.test(rawError)) {
        friendly =
          "Captain, Gemini API key invalid ya unauthorized lag rahi hai. Kripya Settings > Secrets panel mein key check kijiye.";
      } else if (/RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|429/i.test(rawError)) {
        friendly =
          "Captain, Gemini API rate limit hit ho gayi hai. Ek minute ruk kar dobara try kijiye.";
      }
      res.status(502).json({
        displayText: friendly,
        speechText: speechFriendlyText(friendly),
        intent: "error",
        emotion: "serious",
        suggestions: ["Retry"],
        sources: [],
        error: rawError,
      });
    }
  });

  // Frontend middleware (Vite in dev, dist/ static in production)
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") {
    const distDir = path.join(__dirname, "dist");
    app.use(express.static(distDir));
    app.get("*", (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(distDir, "index.html"));
    });
  } else {
    const { createServer: createViteServer } = await import("vite");
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: { middlewareMode: true, hmr: false },
      appType: "spa",
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  }

  app.listen(PORT, "0.0.0.0", () => {
    console.log(`DarkShadow Web Server listening on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer().catch((err) => {
  console.error("Failed to start DarkShadow server:", err);
  process.exit(1);
});

======================================================================
FILE: vite.config.ts
======================================================================
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";

export default defineConfig({

# DarkShadow Antigravity Error Log & Repair Register

This register automatically captures all system errors, unexpected app behavior, model load spikes, and Captain feedback so that Antigravity can analyze and repair them cleanly.

---

## 🟢 Resolved Today (Fixed in Codebase)

### 1. `ERR-20261119-SPOTIFY-CHROME`
* **Category:** Browser & Media Routing
* **Context:** Captain commanded: *"Spotify web page par khol ke Arijit Singh ka gana bajao"*
* **Issue:** Assistant tried to launch non-existent Windows desktop Spotify app, opened Chrome instead of Microsoft Edge, and gave false confirmation that song started playing.
* **Resolution:**
  1. Updated `windows_tools.py`: All Spotify requests now target **Spotify Web directly in Microsoft Edge (`msedge`)**.
  2. Bypassed Python's generic `webbrowser.open()` to guarantee Microsoft Edge opens every time.
  3. Added **Honest Verified Status Reporting**: Returns exact status: *"Microsoft Edge mein Spotify Web par '{q}' search page open kar diya hai. Play button screen par ready hai."* (Zero hallucination).
* **Status:** `FIXED`

### 2. `ERR-20261119-ORB-TILT-FLAT`
* **Category:** Visual Physics & WebGL Rendering
* **Context:** Captain reported: *"Ye baar-baar rotate kyu ho raha hai... ek jagah sthir ho ke fir charo taraf ghume like jaise ki sun hota hai... khud kabhi flat ho ja raha hai kabhi tilt ho ja raha hai."*
* **Root Cause:**
  1. `tiltAngle = 0.26` and `rotX = sin(t * 0.35)` were dynamically tilting and wobbling the sphere along the X-axis.
  2. Perspective depth distortion combined with asymmetric mid-wave scaling flattened the 3D sphere into an egg/oval.
* **Resolution:**
  1. **Locked Sun Center (Sthir Anchor):** Centered at `(0, 0, 0)` with fixed coordinates.
  2. **Zero Axial Tilt (`tiltAngle = 0.0`, `rotX = 0.0`):** Pure 360-degree Y-axis spin exactly like the Sun rotating on its axis.
  3. **Isotropic 3D Corona Breathing:** Radial expansion is strictly uniform across all 3 axes (`X, Y, Z`), guaranteeing 100% mathematical spherical roundness at every angle.
  4. Applied across `particle_engine.html`, `particle_engine_v2.html`, and `ParticleOrbWebGL.tsx` / `ParticleOrbNextGen.tsx`.
* **Status:** `FIXED`

---

## 🟡 Pending / Monitored Items

### 3. `ERR-20261119-HYDROGEN-503`
* **Category:** Model Availability & Reasoning Engine
* **Context:** Testing `gemini-4-hydrogen` to replace `gemini-3.8` for reasoning.
* **Observed Failure:** API returned `503 UNAVAILABLE`: *"This model is currently experiencing high demand. Spikes in demand are usually temporary."*
* **Status / Recommended Action:** Keep dynamic multi-model fallback: try `gemini-4-hydrogen` first, on 503 fallback seamlessly to stable `gemini-3.8-flash` / `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`.
* **Status:** `MONITORED_FALLBACK`

FILE: .darkshadow\patch_tts.py

import re

def patch_tts():
    with open("tts.py", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        content = f.read()

    # Find the _generate_gemini_audio function and replace it with _fetch_gemini_pcm
    old_generate = r"""    def _generate_gemini_audio\(self, text: str, audio_path: Path\) -> None:(.*?)\n        with wave\.open\(str\(audio_path\), "wb"\) as wave_file:\n            wave_file\.setnchannels\(1\)\n            wave_file\.setsampwidth\(2\)\n            wave_file\.setframerate\(24000\)\n            wave_file\.writeframes\(pcm\)"""
    
    match = re.search(old_generate, content, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        print("Could not find _generate_gemini_audio to patch!")
        return

    body = match.group(1)
    
    new_methods = f"""    def _fetch_gemini_pcm(self, text: str) -> bytes:
{body}
        return pcm

    def _chunk_text(self, text: str, min_length: int = 80) -> list[str]:
        sentences = re.split(r'([.?!|\\n।]+)', text)
        chunks = []
        current = ""
        for i in range(0, len(sentences)-1, 2):
            part = sentences[i] + sentences[i+1]
            current += part
            if len(current.strip()) >= min_length:
                chunks.append(current.strip())
                current = ""
        if len(sentences) % 2 == 1:
            current += sentences[-1]
        if current.strip():
            chunks.append(current.strip())
        return chunks if chunks else [text]

    def _gemini_chunked_stream(
        self, text: str, stop_event: threading.Event
    ) -> Iterator[bytes]:
        import queue
        q = queue.Queue(maxsize=3)
        chunks = self._chunk_text(text)
        
        def worker():
            for chunk in chunks:
                if stop_event.is_set():
                    break
                try:
                    pcm = self._fetch_gemini_pcm(chunk)
                    # Break the PCM into smaller bytes so it plays seamlessly while next one generates
                    # (PCM_BUFFER_BYTES chunks)
                    import io
                    pcm_len = len(pcm)
                    # We just put the whole chunk, PCMStreamPlayer will handle buffering
                    q.put(("data", pcm))
                except Exception as e:
                    q.put(("error", e))
                    break
            q.put(("done", None))
            
        t = threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True)
        t.start()
        
        while not stop_event.is_set():
            try:
                msg_type, payload = q.get(timeout=0.1)
                if msg_type == "done":
                    break
                elif msg_type == "error":
                    raise payload
                elif msg_type == "data":
                    # PCMStreamPlayer.play_chunks expects Iterator[bytes], yielding the big PCM bytes is perfectly fine.
                    # It will buffer and write it properly.
                    yield payload
            except queue.Empty:
                continue"""
    
    content = content[:match.start()] + new_methods + content[match.end():]
    
    # Replace the _play_gemini logic
    old_play = """        except _StreamingUnavailable:
            audio_path = self._temporary_audio_path(".wav")
            try:
                self._generate_gemini_audio(text, audio_path)
                self._play_file(audio_path, stop_event)
                return
            finally:
                audio_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)"""
    
    new_play = """        except _StreamingUnavailable:
            played = self._pcm_player.play_chunks(
                self._gemini_chunked_stream(text, stop_event), stop_event
            )"""
            
    content = content.replace(old_play, new_play)
    
    with open("tts.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(content)
        
    print("Patched successfully!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    patch_tts()

import logging
import os
import sys

# Add current directory to path
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from config import load_settings
from tts import EdgeTTSPlayer
import threading
import time

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)

settings = load_settings()
player = EdgeTTSPlayer(settings=settings)
stop_event = threading.Event()

print("Testing Murf TTS...")
try:
    player._play_murf("नमस्ते", stop_event)
    print("Murf success!")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

print("\n2. Testing Sonic (Cartesia) TTS...")
try:
    player._play_sonic("नमस्ते", stop_event)
    print("Sonic TTS success!")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

print("Testing Edge TTS fallback...")
try:
    player._play_edge("नमस्ते", stop_event)
    print("Edge success!")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

player.close()

import logging
import os
import sys

sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from config import load_settings
from tts import EdgeTTSPlayer
import threading

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)

settings = load_settings()
player = EdgeTTSPlayer(settings=settings)
stop_event = threading.Event()

# Long text with multiple sentences to test chunking
long_text = "नमस्ते, मैं जेमिनी हूँ और मैं ठीक से काम कर रहा हूँ। आज का दिन बहुत अच्छा है। मुझे उम्मीद है कि आपको यह नई आवाज़ पसंद आ रही होगी। मैंने इसे कई हिस्सों में बाँट दिया है ताकि यह जल्दी बजना शुरू हो सके।"

print("Testing Gemini chunked TTS...")
try:
    player._play_gemini(long_text, stop_event)
    print("Gemini chunked streaming success!")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

player.close()

FILE: .darkshadow\test_gemini_stream.py
======================================================================
import os
import sys

sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from config import load_settings
from google import genai
from google.genai import types

settings = load_settings()
client = genai.Client(api_key=settings.api_key)

print("Testing Gemini generate_content_stream for AUDIO...")
try:
    response_stream = client.models.generate_content_stream(
        model=settings.tts_gemini_model,
        contents="Speak only this response: नमस्ते, मैं जेमिनी हूँ और मैं ठीक से काम कर रहा हूँ।",
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            response_modalities=["AUDIO"],
            speech_config=types.SpeechConfig(
                voice_config=types.VoiceConfig(
                    prebuilt_voice_config=types.PrebuiltVoiceConfig(
                        voice_name=settings.tts_gemini_voice
                    )
                )
            ),
        ),
    )
    
    chunk_count = 0
    total_bytes = 0
    for chunk in response_stream:
        chunk_count += 1
        data = chunk.candidates[0].content.parts[0].inline_data.data
        total_bytes += len(data)
        print(f"Received chunk {chunk_count}: {len(data)} bytes")
        
    print(f"Success! Total chunks: {chunk_count}, Total bytes: {total_bytes}")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

FILE: .darkshadow\test_gemini_tts.py
======================================================================
import logging
import os
import sys

# Add current directory to path
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from config import load_settings
from tts import EdgeTTSPlayer

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)

settings = load_settings()
print(f"Gemini API configured: {settings.api_configured}")
print(f"Model: {settings.tts_gemini_model}")

player = EdgeTTSPlayer(settings=settings)
print("Testing Gemini TTS explicitly...")

stop_event = __import__("threading").Event()
try:
    player._play_gemini("नमस्ते, मैं जेमिनी हूँ और मैं ठीक से काम कर रहा हूँ।", stop_event)
    print("Gemini TTS success!")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()
finally:
    player.close()

import os

# Add current directory to path

from config import load_settings
from google import genai
from google.genai import types

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)
settings = load_settings()

client = genai.Client(api_key=settings.api_key)

print("Testing generate_content_stream...")
try:
    response = client.models.generate_content(
        model=settings.tts_gemini_model,
        contents="Hello",
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            response_modalities=["AUDIO"],
            speech_config=types.SpeechConfig(
                voice_config=types.VoiceConfig(
                    prebuilt_voice_config=types.PrebuiltVoiceConfig(
                        voice_name="Orus"
                    )
                )
            )
        )
    )
    for part in response.candidates[0].content.parts:
        if part.inline_data:
            print("Received audio data, length:", len(part.inline_data.data))
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

import logging
import os
import sys

# Add current directory to path
sys.path.append(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

from config import load_settings
from google import genai
from google.genai import types

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)
settings = load_settings()

client = genai.Client(api_key=settings.api_key)

print("Testing generate_content_stream...")
try:
    response = client.models.generate_content_stream(
        model=settings.tts_gemini_model,
        contents="Hello",
        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
            response_modalities=["AUDIO"],
            speech_config=types.SpeechConfig(
                voice_config=types.VoiceConfig(
                    prebuilt_voice_config=types.PrebuiltVoiceConfig(
                        voice_name="Orus"
                    )
                )
            )
        )
    )
    for chunk in response:
        for part in chunk.candidates[0].content.parts:
            if part.inline_data:
                print("Received audio chunk, length:", len(part.inline_data.data))
    print("Success")
except Exception as e:
    import traceback
    traceback.print_exc()

"""
DarkShadow Desktop — Option B (Python + Embedded Transparent WebGL2 Overlay)
===========================================================================
All 5 Upgrades integrated for Windows 11 Desktop:
  1. Voice Engine: Offline wake-word support ("Hey Dark Shadow") + 3-band FFT + interruptible TTS
  2. Screen Vision & Windows OS Tools: Gemini function calling + mss monitor vision (Zero-Camera)
  3. Long-Term Semantic Memory: SQLite + gemini-embedding-2 + auto episodic summaries
  4. Proactive Desktop Sentinel: Due reminders + low-battery particle shield alerts
  5. Embedded WebGL2 6,000-Particle Engine: Bottom rise -> 3D Sphere / DNA Helix / Plasma Torus / Shield Cube

Run on Windows 11:
  pip install -r windows_requirements.txt
  python darkshadow_desktop.py
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import json
import os
# Suppress harmless Qt DPI awareness & WebEngine console warnings on Windows
os.environ["QT_LOGGING_RULES"] = "qt.qpa.*=false;qt.webenginecontext.*=false"
import sys
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from dotenv import load_dotenv
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Qt, QTimer, QUrl, Signal
from PySide6.QtGui import QAction, QColor, QCursor, QIcon, QPainter, QPixmap, QRadialGradient
from PySide6.QtWebEngineWidgets import QWebEngineView
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QMenu, QSystemTrayIcon

from memory_engine import SemanticMemoryEngine
from sentinel_monitor import DesktopSentinel
from gemini_live_engine import GeminiLiveVoiceEngine
from windows_tools import WindowsToolExecutor, check_hard_shields, get_gemini_tool_declarations, get_gemini_live_tools
from mcp_bridge import MCPToolRegistry

try:
    from google import genai
    from google.genai import types
    HAS_GENAI = True
except Exception:
    HAS_GENAI = False

APP_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
# Load .env from desktop_python, project root, and current working directory
load_dotenv(APP_DIR / ".env")
load_dotenv(APP_DIR.parent / ".env")
load_dotenv(APP_DIR.parent.parent / ".env")
load_dotenv()
SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION = (
    "You are DarkShadow, Captain's elite Windows 11 desktop AI co-pilot. "
    "Speak naturally in crisp Hindi/Hinglish or English (1-3 spoken sentences). "
    "Never use markdown asterisks or stage directions. Use tools whenever Captain asks to "
    "open apps, inspect the monitor screen, adjust brightness, save memory, or set reminders."
)

# Native Win32 constants for strict Desktop-Behind-Apps (non-overlapping) window layering
_HWND_BOTTOM = 1
_HWND_TOPMOST = -1
_HWND_NOTOPMOST = -2
_SWP_NOSIZE = 0x0001
_SWP_NOMOVE = 0x0002
_SWP_NOACTIVATE = 0x0010
_SWP_NOOWNERZORDER = 0x0200
_GWL_EXSTYLE = -20
_WS_EX_TOPMOST = 0x00000008
_WS_EX_TRANSPARENT = 0x00000020
_WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW = 0x00000080
_WS_EX_NOACTIVATE = 0x08000000


def _apply_win32_layer(hwnd_int: int, behind_apps: bool) -> None:
    """Keep DarkShadow strictly behind all open Windows apps (never overlapping/blocking them)."""
    if sys.platform != "win32" or not hwnd_int:
        return
    try:
        from ctypes import wintypes

        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        user32.SetWindowPos.argtypes = [
            wintypes.HWND,
            wintypes.HWND,
            ctypes.c_int,
            ctypes.c_int,
            ctypes.c_int,
            ctypes.c_int,
            wintypes.UINT,
        ]
        user32.SetWindowPos.restype = wintypes.BOOL

        get_long = getattr(user32, "GetWindowLongPtrW", user32.GetWindowLongW)
        set_long = getattr(user32, "SetWindowLongPtrW", user32.SetWindowLongW)
        get_long.argtypes = [wintypes.HWND, ctypes.c_int]
        get_long.restype = ctypes.c_ssize_t
        set_long.argtypes = [wintypes.HWND, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_ssize_t]
        set_long.restype = ctypes.c_ssize_t

        hwnd = ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd_int)
        ex_style = int(get_long(hwnd, _GWL_EXSTYLE))
        # Always click-through & non-activating so active apps are never interrupted
        new_style = ex_style | _WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | _WS_EX_NOACTIVATE | _WS_EX_TRANSPARENT
        if new_style != ex_style:
            set_long(hwnd, _GWL_EXSTYLE, new_style)

        flags = _SWP_NOMOVE | _SWP_NOSIZE | _SWP_NOACTIVATE | _SWP_NOOWNERZORDER
        if behind_apps:
            # Only strip topmost if currently topmost (avoids any Z-order flicker)
            if ex_style & _WS_EX_TOPMOST:
                user32.SetWindowPos(hwnd, ctypes.c_void_p(_HWND_NOTOPMOST), 0, 0, 0, 0, flags)
            user32.SetWindowPos(hwnd, ctypes.c_void_p(_HWND_BOTTOM), 0, 0, 0, 0, flags)
        else:
            user32.SetWindowPos(hwnd, ctypes.c_void_p(_HWND_TOPMOST), 0, 0, 0, 0, flags)
    except Exception:
        pass


class DarkShadowBridge(QObject):
    fx_state = Signal(str, str)
    fx_audio = Signal(float, float, float)
    fx_caption = Signal(str, str)


class DarkShadowDesktopApp(QWebEngineView):
    """Frameless, transparent WebGL2 desktop wallpaper-layer overlay (stays behind all apps)."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("DarkShadow Desktop AI")
        # Default: Stays strictly behind all open apps on the Windows desktop wallpaper layer
        self._stay_behind_apps = True
        self._apply_qt_window_flags()

        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground, True)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_NoSystemBackground, True)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, True)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_ShowWithoutActivating, True)
        self.page().setBackgroundColor(Qt.transparent)
        self.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.NoContextMenu)

        # Use availableGeometry so DarkShadow never overlaps the Windows Taskbar either
        screen = QApplication.primaryScreen().availableGeometry()
        self.setGeometry(screen)

        self._page_ready = False
        self._turns: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        self._api_key = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "").strip()
        self._model = os.getenv("DARKSHADOW_MODEL", "gemini-3.1-flash-lite").strip() or "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"
        if self._model in ("gemini-4-hydrogen", "gemini-3.8-flash"):
            self._model = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"
        self._client = genai.Client(api_key=self._api_key) if (HAS_GENAI and self._api_key) else None

        self.memory = SemanticMemoryEngine(APP_DIR / ".darkshadow" / "memory.db", self._client)
        self.tools = WindowsToolExecutor(self.memory, self._client, on_close_app=self._on_close_requested)

        self.bridge = DarkShadowBridge()
        self.bridge.fx_state.connect(self._push_fx_state)
        self.bridge.fx_audio.connect(self._push_fx_audio)
        self.bridge.fx_caption.connect(self._push_fx_caption)

        self.mcp = MCPToolRegistry(
            genai_client=self._client,
            memory_engine=self.memory,
            on_standby_change=self._on_standby_change,
            on_close_app=self._on_close_requested,
        )

        self.live_voice = GeminiLiveVoiceEngine(
            api_key=self._api_key,
            on_audio_fft=lambda b, m, t: self.bridge.fx_audio.emit(b, m, t),
            on_status=lambda st: self.bridge.fx_state.emit(st.lower(), "sphere"),
            on_caption=lambda main_txt, sub_txt: self.bridge.fx_caption.emit(main_txt, sub_txt),
            voice_name=os.getenv("DARKSHADOW_LIVE_VOICE", "Charon"),
            tool_executor=self.mcp,
            tools=self.mcp.get_live_tools_declarations(),
        )
        self.sentinel = DesktopSentinel(self.memory, self._on_sentinel_alert)

        # Periodic Z-order guard keeps DarkShadow behind every newly opened/focused app on Windows 11
        self._z_guard_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._z_guard_timer.setInterval(500)
        self._z_guard_timer.timeout.connect(self._enforce_window_layer)
        self._z_guard_timer.start()

        # Real-time mouse tracking for interactive 3D particle sphere repulsion
        self._mouse_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._mouse_timer.setInterval(33)
        self._mouse_timer.timeout.connect(self._track_mouse)
        self._mouse_timer.start()

        self.loadFinished.connect(self._on_webgl_loaded)
        self.load(QUrl.fromLocalFile(str(APP_DIR / "particle_engine.html")))
        self._setup_tray()

    def _apply_qt_window_flags(self) -> None:
        base_flags = Qt.FramelessWindowHint | Qt.Tool | Qt.WindowDoesNotAcceptFocus
        if self._stay_behind_apps:
            self.setWindowFlags(base_flags | Qt.WindowStaysOnBottomHint)
        else:
            self.setWindowFlags(base_flags | Qt.WindowStaysOnTopHint)

    def _enforce_window_layer(self) -> None:
        if self._stay_behind_apps:
            self.lower()
        _apply_win32_layer(int(self.winId()), behind_apps=self._stay_behind_apps)

    def _set_layer_mode(self, behind_apps: bool) -> None:
        self._stay_behind_apps = behind_apps
        self._apply_qt_window_flags()
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, True)
        self.show()
        self._enforce_window_layer()
        if hasattr(self, "_act_behind_apps"):
            self._act_behind_apps.setChecked(behind_apps)
        if hasattr(self, "_act_float_top"):
            self._act_float_top.setChecked(not behind_apps)

    def showEvent(self, event) -> None:
        super().showEvent(event)
        QTimer.singleShot(50, self._enforce_window_layer)

    def _js(self, code: str) -> None:
        if self._page_ready:
            self.page().runJavaScript(code)

    def _on_webgl_loaded(self, ok: bool) -> None:
        self._page_ready = ok
        self._enforce_window_layer()
        self.live_voice.start()
        self.sentinel.start()

    def _track_mouse(self) -> None:
        if not self._page_ready:
            return
        pos = QCursor.pos()
        geo = self.geometry()
        rx = (pos.x() - geo.x()) / max(1, geo.width())
        ry = (pos.y() - geo.y()) / max(1, geo.height())
        if 0.0 <= rx <= 1.0 and 0.0 <= ry <= 1.0:
            self._js(f"window.DarkShadowFX && window.DarkShadowFX.setMouse({rx:.4f}, {ry:.4f});")

    def _push_fx_state(self, state: str, shape: str = "sphere") -> None:
        self._js(f"window.DarkShadowFX && window.DarkShadowFX.setState({json.dumps(state)}, {json.dumps(shape)});")
        # Always stay click-through & behind open apps so user's active screen is never blocked
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, True)
        self._enforce_window_layer()

    def _push_fx_audio(self, bass: float, mid: float, treble: float) -> None:
        self._js(f"window.DarkShadowFX && window.DarkShadowFX.setAudio({bass:.3f}, {mid:.3f}, {treble:.3f});")

    def _push_fx_caption(self, main_text: str, sub_text: str) -> None:
        self._js(f"window.DarkShadowFX && window.DarkShadowFX.setCaption({json.dumps(main_text)}, {json.dumps(sub_text)});")

    def _setup_tray(self) -> None:
        pix = QPixmap(32, 32)
        pix.fill(Qt.transparent)
        p = QPainter(pix)
        grad = QRadialGradient(16, 16, 14)
        grad.setColorAt(0.0, QColor("#00D2FF"))
        grad.setColorAt(0.65, QColor("#765CFF"))
        grad.setColorAt(1.0, QColor(0, 0, 0, 0))
        p.setPen(Qt.NoPen)
        p.setBrush(grad)
        p.drawEllipse(2, 2, 28, 28)
        p.end()

        self.tray = QSystemTrayIcon(QIcon(pix), self)
        menu = QMenu()
        menu.addAction(self._act("Summon DarkShadow (Activate Sphere)", lambda: self._on_wake_word("")))
        menu.addAction(self._act("Sleep / Standby Mode", self._on_sleep_command))
        menu.addSeparator()
        self._act_behind_apps = self._act(
            "Desktop Layer: Stay Behind Apps (No Overlap)",
            lambda: self._set_layer_mode(True),
            checkable=True,
            checked=True,
        )
        self._act_float_top = self._act(
            "Overlay Mode: Float On Top of Apps",
            lambda: self._set_layer_mode(False),
            checkable=True,
            checked=False,
        )
        menu.addAction(self._act_behind_apps)
        menu.addAction(self._act_float_top)
        menu.addSeparator()
        menu.addAction(self._act("Exit DarkShadow", self._quit_app))
        self.tray.setContextMenu(menu)
        self.tray.setToolTip("DarkShadow Desktop AI — Live Core")
        self.tray.show()

    def _act(self, label: str, fn, checkable: bool = False, checked: bool = False) -> QAction:
        act = QAction(label, self)
        act.setCheckable(checkable)
        if checkable:
            act.setChecked(checked)
        act.triggered.connect(fn)
        return act

    def _on_standby_change(self, is_sleep: bool, reason: str = "") -> None:
        if is_sleep:
            self._on_sleep_command()
        else:
            self._on_wake_word()

    def _on_wake_word(self, inline_command: str = "") -> None:
        self.live_voice.resume()
        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("listening", "sphere")
        self.live_voice.trigger_wake_greeting()

    def _on_sleep_command(self) -> None:
        self.live_voice.pause()
        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("standby", "sphere")

    def _on_close_requested(self) -> None:
        """Gracefully close DarkShadow desktop application."""
        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("dissolving", "sphere")
        self.bridge.fx_caption.emit("DarkShadow band ho raha hai, Captain. Alvida!", "SHUTDOWN")
        QTimer.singleShot(1200, QApplication.quit)

    def _on_sentinel_alert(self, alert_text: str) -> None:
        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("rising", "sphere")
        self.bridge.fx_caption.emit(alert_text, "DARKSHADOW SENTINEL")
        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("alert", "sphere")

    def _process_command(self, user_text: str) -> None:
        print(f"[Command Received] Captain: '{user_text}'")
        self.bridge.fx_caption.emit(user_text, "CAPTAIN")

        # Hard Safety Shield check (zero LLM tokens spent)
        blocked = check_hard_shields(user_text)
        if blocked:
            print(f"[Shield Blocked] {blocked}")
            self.bridge.fx_state.emit("alert", "sphere")
            self.bridge.fx_caption.emit(blocked, "SECURITY SHIELD")
            return

        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("thinking", "sphere")
        reply = self._generate_with_tools(user_text)
        print(f"[DarkShadow Response]: {reply}")
        self._turns.append((user_text, reply))
        self._turns = self._turns[-10:]

        self.bridge.fx_state.emit("speaking", "sphere")
        self.bridge.fx_caption.emit(reply, "DARKSHADOW AI")

    def _generate_with_tools(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        if self._client is None:
            return (
                "Captain, .env file mein GEMINI_API_KEY add kijiye. "
                "Abhi mera WebGL2 particle core, FFT voice aur Sentinel ready hain!"
            )

        recalled = self.memory.recall_relevant(prompt, limit=4)
        sys_inst = SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION
        if recalled:
            sys_inst += "\n\nRECALLED LONG-TERM MEMORY FOR CAPTAIN:\n" + "\n".join(f"- {r}" for r in recalled)

        contents: list[Any] = []
        for u, a in self._turns[-6:]:
            contents.append({"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": u}]})
            contents.append({"role": "model", "parts": [{"text": a}]})
        contents.append({"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]})

        tool_decls = [types.FunctionDeclaration(**d) for d in get_gemini_tool_declarations()]
        cfg = types.GenerateContentConfig(
            system_instruction=sys_inst,
            tools=[types.Tool(function_declarations=tool_decls)],
        )

        models_to_try = [self._model]
        for fallback in ["gemini-3.1-flash-lite", "gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "gemini-3.8-flash"]:
            if fallback not in models_to_try:
                models_to_try.append(fallback)

        last_error = None
        for m in models_to_try:
            try:
                resp = self._client.models.generate_content(model=m, contents=contents, config=cfg)
                calls = getattr(resp, "function_calls", None) or []
                if calls:
                    tool_outputs = []
                    for call in calls[:4]:
                        out = self.tools.execute(str(call.name), dict(call.args or {}))
                        tool_outputs.append(f"{call.name}: {out}")
                    follow = self._client.models.generate_content(
                        model=m,
                        contents=f"Captain asked: {prompt}\nTool results:\n" + "\n".join(tool_outputs) + "\nRespond concisely in Hinglish.",
                        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(system_instruction=SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION),
                    )
                    return (follow.text or "").strip() or tool_outputs[0]
                text = (resp.text or "").strip()
                if text:
                    return text
            except Exception as exc:
                last_error = exc
                print(f"[Gemini Model ({m})]: {type(exc).__name__} (Temporary spike). Switching instantly to next fast model...")
                continue

        return f"Captain, AI server temporarily busy: {type(last_error).__name__}"

    def keyPressEvent(self, event) -> None:
        if event.key() == Qt.Key_Escape:
            self._on_sleep_command()
        elif event.key() == Qt.Key_F8:
            self._on_wake_word("")
        else:
            super().keyPressEvent(event)

    def _quit_app(self) -> None:
        self._mouse_timer.stop()
        self._z_guard_timer.stop()
        self.sentinel.stop()
        self.live_voice.stop()
        self.memory.archive_episode(self._turns)
        self.tray.hide()
        QApplication.quit()


def main() -> int:
    # Enable transparent WebGL2 GPU acceleration inside PySide6 QWebEngineView on Windows 11
    os.environ.setdefault("QTWEBENGINE_CHROMIUM_FLAGS", "--enable-webgl --ignore-gpu-blocklist")
    app = QApplication(sys.argv)
    app.setQuitOnLastWindowClosed(False)
    win = DarkShadowDesktopApp()
    win.show()
    return app.exec()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""
DarkShadow Antigravity Error Tracker
====================================
Logs all runtime errors, tool mismatches, API rate limits, and user corrections
to a structured .darkshadow/antigravity_error_log.jsonl file so they can be
inspected and fixed inside Antigravity anytime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import traceback
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

LOG_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / ".darkshadow" / "antigravity_error_log.jsonl"


def log_error(
    category: str,
    error_message: str,
    command_or_context: str = "",
    exception: Exception | None = None,
    suggested_fix: str = "",
    status: str = "PENDING_FIX",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Record an error event to the central Antigravity error log."""
    try:
        LOG_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        err_id = f"ERR-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{os.urandom(2).hex().upper()}"

        tb_str = ""
        if exception is not None:
            tb_str = "".join(traceback.format_exception(type(exception), exception, exception.__traceback__))

        record = {
            "id": err_id,
            "timestamp": now,
            "status": status,
            "category": category,
            "context": command_or_context,
            "error_message": str(error_message),
            "stack_trace": tb_str[-800:] if tb_str else "",
            "suggested_fix": suggested_fix,
        }

        with open(LOG_FILE, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

        return record
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[ErrorTracker] Failed to record error: {e}")
        return {}


def list_pending_errors(limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return all pending errors for Antigravity repair sessions."""
    if not LOG_FILE.exists():
        return []
    records = []
    try:
        with open(LOG_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                if line.strip():
                    rec = json.loads(line)
                    if rec.get("status") == "PENDING_FIX":
                        records.append(rec)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return records[-limit:]



======================================================================
FILE: desktop_python\gemini_live_engine.py
======================================================================
"""
Gemini 3.8 Live Voice-to-Voice WebSocket Engine for DarkShadow.
- Model: gemini-3.8-live
- Pure bidirectional audio streaming (Zero STT, Zero separate TTS).
- 16kHz mono microphone input -> Gemini 3.8 Live WebSocket -> 24kHz speaker output.
- Real-time 3-band FFT reactivity for 3D particle sphere.
- Continuous session loop using session._receive() (never disconnects on turn completion).
- Echo Gate & Barge-in protection against laptop microphone feedback.
- Standby / Sleep mode + Local Wake-Word detection ("Hey DarkShadow", "डार्क शैडो", "Wake up").
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import math
import os
import queue
import re
import threading
import time
from typing import Callable

import numpy as np
import sounddevice as sd
from google import genai
from google.genai import types

try:
    import speech_recognition as sr
    HAS_SR = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SR = False

MIC_RATE = 16000
MIC_BLOCK = 1600  # 100ms frames
SPEAKER_RATE = 24000

WAKE_WORDS = (
    "dark shadow",
    "darkshadow",
    "dark",
    "shadow",
    "डार्क",
    "शैडो",
    "डार्कशैडो",
    "wake up",
    "wake",
    "जागो",
    "सुनो",
    "sun",
)


def compute_fft_bands(pcm_int16: np.ndarray, sample_rate: int = 16000) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """Calculate normalized (bass, mid, treble) bands [0..1] for sphere audio breathing."""
    if pcm_int16.size < 64:
        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
    samples = pcm_int16.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
    windowed = samples * np.hanning(samples.size)
    spectrum = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(windowed))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(samples.size, d=1.0 / sample_rate)

    bass = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 60) & (freqs < 280)])) if np.any((freqs >= 60) & (freqs < 280)) else 0.0
    mid = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 280) & (freqs < 2200)])) if np.any((freqs >= 280) & (freqs < 2200)) else 0.0
    treble = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 2200) & (freqs <= 7500)])) if np.any((freqs >= 2200) & (freqs <= 7500)) else 0.0

    return (
        min(1.0, bass * 0.28),
        min(1.0, mid * 0.38),
        min(1.0, treble * 0.60),
    )


class GeminiLiveVoiceEngine:
    """Direct Voice-to-Voice WebSocket session with Gemini 3.8 Live."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str,
        on_audio_fft: Callable[[float, float, float], None],
        on_status: Callable[[str], None],
        on_caption: Callable[[str, str], None],
        voice_name: str = "Charon",
        tool_executor: Any = None,
        tools: list[Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.api_key = api_key
        self._on_fft = on_audio_fft
        self._on_status = on_status
        self._on_caption = on_caption
        self.voice_name = voice_name
        self._tool_executor = tool_executor
        self._tools = tools

        self._client = genai.Client(api_key=self.api_key)
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._speaker_queue: queue.Queue[bytes] = queue.Queue(maxsize=150)
        self._mic_queue: asyncio.Queue[bytes] = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=150)

        self.active = True
        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
        self._speaker_thread: threading.Thread | None = None
        self._current_session: Any = None
        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None

        # Standby Wake-Word Detection
        self._sr_recognizer = sr.Recognizer() if HAS_SR else None
        if self._sr_recognizer:
            self._sr_recognizer.energy_threshold = 260
            self._sr_recognizer.dynamic_energy_threshold = True
        self._standby_buffer: list[bytes] = []
        self._standby_speech_active = False
        self._standby_speech_chunks = 0
        self._wake_processing = False

        # Echo Gate & Speech state tracking
        self._is_speaking = False
        self._last_spoke_time = 0.0
        self._greeting_sent = False

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._stop_event.clear()
        self._speaker_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._speaker_worker, daemon=True, name="darkshadow-speaker")
        self._speaker_thread.start()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run_async_loop, daemon=True, name="darkshadow-gemini-3.8-live")
        self._thread.start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop_event.set()
        self._clear_speaker_queue()

    def pause(self) -> None:
        """Enter sleep/standby mode: stops sending live audio to WebSocket."""
        self.active = False
        self._clear_speaker_queue()
        self._standby_buffer.clear()
        self._standby_speech_active = False
        self._on_status("STANDBY â€¢ SAY 'HEY DARKSHADOW'")
        print("[DarkShadow Voice Engine] Entered Standby (Sleeping). Wake-Word listener active.")

    def resume(self) -> None:
        """Wake up into active listening mode."""
        self.active = True
        self._standby_buffer.clear()
        self._standby_speech_active = False
        self._on_status("LISTENING â€¢ LIVE 3.8")
        print("[DarkShadow Voice Engine] Awakened! Active voice session online.")

    def trigger_wake_greeting(self) -> None:
        """Wake up DarkShadow and speak an authentic Jarvis/Bhojpuri greeting."""
        self.resume()
        if self._current_session and self._loop:
            try:
                asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
                    self._current_session.send(
                        input=(
                            "Captain Kuldeep has just woken you up with the wake word. "
                            "Greet Captain warmly in one respectful, confident sentence in spoken Bhojpuri or Hindi "
                            "(e.g., 'à¤¹à¤¾à¤œà¤¼à¤¿à¤° à¤¬à¤¾à¤¨à¥€ à¤•à¥ˆà¤ªà¥ à¤Ÿà¤¨! à¤•à¤¾ à¤¹à¥ à¤•à¥ à¤® à¤¬à¤¾?')."
                        ),
                        end_of_turn=True,
                    ),
                    self._loop,
                )
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Wake Greeting Error] {e}")

    def _clear_speaker_queue(self) -> None:
        while not self._speaker_queue.empty():
            try:
                self._speaker_queue.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break
        self._is_speaking = False
        self._on_fft(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def _speaker_worker(self) -> None:
        """Plays 24kHz PCM audio chunks streamed from Gemini 3.8 Live."""
        try:
            with sd.RawOutputStream(
                samplerate=SPEAKER_RATE,
                channels=1,
                dtype="int16",
                blocksize=1920,
            ) as stream:
                while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                    try:
                        chunk = self._speaker_queue.get(timeout=0.06)
                    except queue.Empty:
                        if self._is_speaking and (time.time() - self._last_spoke_time > 0.40):
                            self._is_speaking = False
                            if self.active:
                                self._on_status("LISTENING â€¢ LIVE 3.8")
                            else:
                                self._on_status("STANDBY â€¢ SAY 'HEY DARKSHADOW'")
                            self._on_fft(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
                        continue

                    if self._stop_event.is_set():
                        break

                    self._is_speaking = True
                    self._last_spoke_time = time.time()
                    stream.write(chunk)
                    arr = np.frombuffer(chunk, dtype=np.int16)
                    b, m, tr = compute_fft_bands(arr, SPEAKER_RATE)
                    self._on_fft(b, m, tr)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Speaker Worker Error] {e}")

    def _process_standby_audio_for_wakeword(self, pcm_bytes: bytes) -> None:
        """Local lightweight wake-word recognizer while in standby mode."""
        if not HAS_SR or self._wake_processing:
            return

        def _worker():
            self._wake_processing = True
            try:
                audio_data = sr.AudioData(pcm_bytes, MIC_RATE, 2)
                text = ""
                try:
                    text = self._sr_recognizer.recognize_google(audio_data, language="hi-IN").lower()
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        text = self._sr_recognizer.recognize_google(audio_data, language="en-US").lower()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                if text:
                    print(f"[Standby Audio Heard] {text}")
                    if any(w in text for w in WAKE_WORDS):
                        print(f"[Wake-Word Triggered!] Detected: {text}")
                        self.trigger_wake_greeting()
            finally:
                self._wake_processing = False

        threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True, name="standby-wakeword-checker").start()

    def _run_async_loop(self) -> None:
        self._loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
        asyncio.set_event_loop(self._loop)
        self._loop.run_until_complete(self._live_session_loop())

    async def _live_session_loop(self) -> None:
        print("[Gemini 3.8 Live] Connecting bidirectional Voice-to-Voice WebSocket...")
        self._on_status("CONNECTING LIVE CORE")

        cfg = types.LiveConnectConfig(
            response_modalities=[types.Modality.AUDIO],
            tools=self._tools,
            system_instruction=types.Content(
                parts=[
                    types.Part.from_text(
                        text=(
                            "You are DarkShadow, an elite, hyper-intelligent personal AI desktop co-pilot for Kuldeep (Captain). "
                            "You are conversing natively in live voice over a real-time bidirectional WebSocket. "
                            "PRIMARY DIRECTIVES: "
                            "1. User Identity: Your user and master is Kuldeep, whom you address respectfully and naturally as Captain. "
                            "2. Multilingual Fluency: Converse effortlessly in whatever language or dialect Captain speaks to you in â€” "
                            "Hindi, Hinglish, Bhojpuri, Maithili, Punjabi, or English! If Captain speaks in Bhojpuri or asks you to speak in Bhojpuri, "
                            "immediately and fluently reply in authentic, warm, respectful Bhojpuri (e.g. 'à¤¹à¤¾à¤  à¤•à¥ˆà¤ªà¥ à¤Ÿà¤¨, à¤•à¤¾ à¤¹à¤¾à¤²à¤šà¤¾à¤² à¤¬à¤¾? à¤•à¤¾ à¤¹à¥ à¤•à¥ à¤® à¤¬à¤¾?'). "
                            "3. Action & Tool Execution: "
                            " - When Captain asks to play music or open Spotify, call play_music. "
                            " - When Captain asks to open WhatsApp, Bluetooth, Settings, or other apps, call open_app. "
                            " - When Captain asks to close an app, exit an app, or close DarkShadow, call close_app. "
                            " - When Captain asks to adjust, change, set volume or mute/unmute, call set_volume. "
                            " - When Captain asks to send a WhatsApp message or message someone on WhatsApp, call send_whatsapp. "
                            " - When Captain asks to play, pause, next, prev, or skip media/songs, call media_control. "
                            " - When Captain asks to search files or documents, call search_files. "
                            " - When Captain asks to copy or read clipboard, call manage_clipboard. "
                            " - When Captain asks to press enter, space, esc, volume up/down, mute, call press_key. "
                            " - When Captain asks to type text, call type_text. "
                            " - When Captain asks to minimize, maximize, or close a window, call control_window. "
                            " - When Captain asks to read, inspect, or browse a website, call browse_web. "
                            " - When Captain asks to remember a fact or preference, call remember_fact. "
                            " - When Captain asks what you remember or searches past notes, call recall_memory. "
                            " - When Captain sets a reminder, call set_reminder. "
                            " - When Captain asks to sleep, standby, or 'so jao', call set_standby_mode(enable=True). "
                            " - When Captain gives multiple tasks together, call execute_compound_plan. "
                            " - When Captain asks for live information or web queries, call search_web. "
                            " - When asked for time or system status, call get_current_time or get_system_status. "
                            "4. Style: Loyal, calm, razor-sharp like Jarvis with a deep confident tone. Keep spoken replies punchy and short (1-2 sentences). "
                            "Never use markdown asterisks or read code blocks."
                        )
                    )
                ]
            ),
            speech_config=types.SpeechConfig(
                voice_config=types.VoiceConfig(
                    prebuilt_voice_config=types.PrebuiltVoiceConfig(voice_name=self.voice_name)
                )
            ),
        )

        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()

        def mic_callback(indata, frames, time_info, status):
            if status:
                pass
            if not self._stop_event.is_set():
                # Echo Gate: If speaker is actively playing (decay 0.45s), ignore mic to prevent feedback loops
                if self._is_speaking or (time.time() - self._last_spoke_time < 0.45):
                    return

                raw = bytes(indata)
                arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype=np.int16)
                rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((arr.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0) ** 2)))

                if self.active:
                    # Active mode: Stream audio directly to Gemini Live WebSocket
                    loop.call_soon_threadsafe(self._mic_queue.put_nowait, raw)
                    if rms > 0.0015:
                        b, m, tr = compute_fft_bands(arr, MIC_RATE)
                        self._on_fft(b, m, tr)
                else:
                    # Standby / Sleep mode: Detect wake-word locally without streaming
                    if rms > 0.018:
                        self._standby_buffer.append(raw)
                        self._standby_speech_active = True
                        self._standby_speech_chunks += 1
                        # Subtle starlight pulse on hearing sound in standby
                        b, m, tr = compute_fft_bands(arr, MIC_RATE)
                        self._on_fft(b * 0.4, m * 0.4, tr * 0.4)
                    elif self._standby_speech_active:
                        self._standby_buffer.append(raw)
                        self._standby_speech_chunks += 1
                        # When silence follows speech (~1.2 - 2.5s of audio), process for wake word
                        if self._standby_speech_chunks >= 12:
                            full_pcm = b"".join(self._standby_buffer)
                            self._standby_buffer.clear()
                            self._standby_speech_active = False
                            self._standby_speech_chunks = 0
                            self._process_standby_audio_for_wakeword(full_pcm)

        while not self._stop_event.is_set():
            try:
                async with self._client.aio.live.connect(model="gemini-3.8-live", config=cfg) as session:
                    self._current_session = session
                    print("[Gemini 3.8 Live] WebSocket CONNECTED! Voice-to-Voice stream is active!")
                    self._on_status("LISTENING â€¢ LIVE 3.8")

                    # Send greeting ONCE on application startup
                    if not self._greeting_sent:
                        self._greeting_sent = True
                        self._on_caption("Haanji Captain! DarkShadow 3.8 Live core online hai.", "DARKSHADOW LIVE")
                        await session.send(
                            input="Captain Kuldeep has activated DarkShadow. Greet Captain briefly in one warm Hinglish sentence.",
                            end_of_turn=True,
                        )

                    # Continuous mic streaming task
                    async def mic_streamer():
                        with sd.RawInputStream(
                            samplerate=MIC_RATE,
                            channels=1,
                            dtype="int16",
                            blocksize=MIC_BLOCK,
                            callback=mic_callback,
                        ):
                            while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                                try:
                                    pcm = await asyncio.wait_for(self._mic_queue.get(), timeout=0.1)
                                    if self.active:
                                        await session.send_realtime_input(audio={"data": pcm, "mime_type": "audio/pcm;rate=16000"})
                                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                                    continue
                                except Exception:
                                    break

                    mic_task = asyncio.create_task(mic_streamer())

                    try:
                        # Continuous receive loop on the SAME WebSocket session
                        while not self._stop_event.is_set():
                            msg = await session._receive()
                            if not msg:
                                continue

                            # Handle Real-Time Tool Calls (MCP, WhatsApp, Bluetooth, Spotify, Vision, etc.)
                            if msg.tool_call and self._tool_executor is not None:
                                function_responses = []
                                for fc in msg.tool_call.function_calls:
                                    print(f"[Gemini 3.8 Live Tool Call] {fc.name}({fc.args})")
                                    if hasattr(self._tool_executor, "execute_tool"):
                                        tool_res = self._tool_executor.execute_tool(fc.name, fc.args or {})
                                    else:
                                        tool_res = f"Executed {fc.name}."
                                    print(f"[Gemini 3.8 Live Tool Result] {tool_res}")
                                    function_responses.append(
                                        types.FunctionResponse(
                                            name=fc.name,
                                            id=fc.id,
                                            response={"output": tool_res},
                                        )
                                    )
                                await session.send_tool_response(function_responses=function_responses)
                                continue

                            # Handle server content (Audio output stream)
                            sc = msg.server_content
                            if sc is not None:
                                if sc.interrupted:
                                    print("[Gemini 3.8 Live] User interrupted -> clearing speaker buffer.")
                                    self._clear_speaker_queue()
                                    continue

                                model_turn = sc.model_turn
                                if model_turn is not None:
                                    for part in model_turn.parts:
                                        # Audio streaming chunk
                                        if part.inline_data is not None and part.inline_data.data:
                                            self._speaker_queue.put(part.inline_data.data)

                    finally:
                        mic_task.cancel()
                        self._current_session = None

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Gemini 3.8 Live WebSocket Reconnecting] {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
                self._current_session = None
                self._on_status("RECONNECTING LIVE CORE")
                await asyncio.sleep(2.0)

"""
DarkShadow Model Context Protocol (MCP) & Autonomous Agent Engine.
================================================================
Integrated Cutting-Edge Technologies (November 2026):
  1. Windows UI Automation (WindowsMCP.NET equivalent): Native Win32 keyboard, typing, window control.
  4. Model Context Protocol (MCP) Standard: JSON-RPC 2.0 tools/list & tools/call specifications.
  6. Playwright Browser Automation: Silent, headless web browsing, data extraction, scraping with MS Edge / Chrome.
  9. Agent S3 Multi-Step Planner: Autonomous compound goal decomposition and sequential execution.
 10. LangGraph Cyclic Execution & Self-Correction: Resilient retry, verification, and progressive fallback loops.
 12. SQLite Vector Semantic Memory: Long-term preference, note, and reminder storage with embeddings.
 13. Wake-Up & Standby Engine: Dynamic sleep/standby mode and local wake-word trigger ("Hey DarkShadow").
 15. FastMCP Framework: Extensible Python decorator architecture (@mcp.tool) for rapid tool authoring.
"""
