class FastMCP:
    """FastMCP Core Engine:
    - Provides standardized tool registration decorator (@tool)
    - Auto-generates standard Gemini Live FunctionDeclaration schemas
    - Auto-generates MCP JSON-RPC 2.0 tools/list schemas
    - Provides resilient execution with verification and error shielding
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.registry: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}

    def tool(self, name: str, description: str, parameters: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Callable:
        def decorator(func: Callable) -> Callable:
            self.registry[name] = {
                "name": name,
                "description": description,
                "parameters": parameters or {"type": "OBJECT", "properties": {}},
                "handler": func,
            }
            return func
        return decorator

    def get_gemini_declarations(self) -> list[Any]:
        """Convert all registered tools into google.genai.types.FunctionDeclaration."""
        if not HAS_GENAI:
            return []

        decls = []
        for name, meta in self.registry.items():
            props = {}
            raw_props = meta["parameters"].get("properties", {})
            required = meta["parameters"].get("required", [])

            for p_name, p_meta in raw_props.items():
                p_type_str = str(p_meta.get("type", "STRING")).upper()
                p_type = getattr(types.Type, p_type_str, types.Type.STRING)
                props[p_name] = types.Schema(
                    type=p_type,
                    description=p_meta.get("description", p_name),
                )

            schema = types.Schema(
                type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                properties=props,
                required=required if required else None,
            )

            decls.append(
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name=name,
                    description=meta["description"],
                    parameters=schema,
                )
            )
        return decls

    def get_mcp_manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return MCP standard JSON-RPC tools list manifest."""
        tools_list = []
        for name, meta in self.registry.items():
            tools_list.append({
                "name": name,
                "description": meta["description"],
                "inputSchema": meta["parameters"],
            })
        return {
            "protocolVersion": "2024-11-05",
            "tools": tools_list,
        }


class MCPToolRegistry:
    """Central MCP Tool Registry & Autonomous Agentic Bridge."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        genai_client: Any = None,
        memory_engine: Any = None,
        on_standby_change: Callable[[bool, str], None] | None = None,
        on_close_app: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._client = genai_client
        self._memory = memory_engine
        self._on_standby_change = on_standby_change
        self._on_close_app = on_close_app
        self.fast_mcp = FastMCP()
        self._register_all_tools()

    def capture_screen_jpeg(self) -> bytes | None:
        """Capture primary monitor snapshot on-demand (Zero continuous streaming, 100% quota-safe)."""
        if not HAS_SCREEN:
            return None
        with mss.mss() as sct:
            monitor = sct.monitors[1]
            raw = sct.grab(monitor)
            img = Image.frombytes("RGB", raw.size, raw.bgra, "raw", "BGRX")
            img.thumbnail((1280, 720))
            buf = io.BytesIO()
            img.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=75)
            return buf.getvalue()

    def _register_all_tools(self) -> None:
        m = self.fast_mcp

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 1: open_app
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="open_app",
            description="Launch a desktop app or Windows setting (whatsapp, bluetooth, spotify, settings, chrome, notepad, calculator, wifi, terminal, task manager).",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "app_name": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Name of the app or setting"}
                },
                "required": ["app_name"],
            },
        )
        def tool_open_app(app_name: str) -> str:
            app = str(app_name).strip().lower()
            if "spotify" in app or "music" in app:
                try:
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "spotify:"], shell=False)
                    return "Launched Spotify Desktop."
                except Exception:
                    webbrowser.open("https://open.spotify.com")
                    return "Opened Spotify Web."
            if "whatsapp" in app:
                try:
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "whatsapp:"], shell=False)
                    return "Launched WhatsApp Desktop."
                except Exception:
                    webbrowser.open("https://web.whatsapp.com")
                    return "Opened WhatsApp Web."
            if "bluetooth" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:bluetooth"], shell=False)
                return "Opened Bluetooth Settings."
            if "setting" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:"], shell=False)
                return "Opened Windows Settings."
            if "wifi" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:network-wifi"], shell=False)
                return "Opened WiFi Settings."
            if "youtube" in app:
                webbrowser.open("https://www.youtube.com")
                return "Opened YouTube."

            cmd = SAFE_WINDOWS_APPS.get(app)
            if cmd:
                subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=False)
                return f"Launched {app}."
            try:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", app], shell=False)
                return f"Launched {app}."
            except Exception:
                return f"Could not launch {app}. Try opening with full path."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: close_app
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="close_app",
            description="Close one running Windows application by name (chrome, edge, notepad, spotify, whatsapp, vscode, calculator, terminal, darkshadow) or close the active window.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "app_name": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Application name or 'active' or 'darkshadow'"},
                },
                "required": ["app_name"],
            },
        )
        def tool_close_app(app_name: str) -> str:
            return close_application(app_name, self._on_close_app)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: set_volume
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="set_volume",
            description="Adjust Windows audio master volume (0-100), change volume up/down, or mute/unmute audio.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "level": {"type": "INTEGER", "description": "Absolute volume percentage 0 to 100"},
                    "direction": {"type": "STRING", "description": "up | down | mute | unmute"},
                    "amount": {"type": "INTEGER", "description": "Step amount (default 10)"},
                },
            },
        )
        def tool_set_volume(level: int | None = None, direction: str = "", amount: int = 10) -> str:
            return set_system_volume(level=level, direction=direction, amount=amount)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: send_whatsapp
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="send_whatsapp",
            description="Send or draft a WhatsApp message to a contact name or phone number.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "recipient": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Contact name or phone number"},
                    "message": {"type": "STRING", "description": "The message to send"},
                },
                "required": ["recipient", "message"],
            },
        )
        def tool_send_whatsapp(recipient: str, message: str) -> str:
            return send_whatsapp_message(recipient, message)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: media_control
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="media_control",
            description="Control media playback: play_pause, play, pause, next, prev, stop, mute, volume_up, volume_down.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "action": {"type": "STRING", "description": "play_pause | next | prev | stop | mute | volume_up | volume_down"},
                },
                "required": ["action"],
            },
        )
        def tool_media_control(action: str) -> str:
            return control_media(action)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: search_files
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="search_files",
            description="Search local files in Downloads, Documents, Desktop by filename or keyword.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Filename or keyword"},
                    "folder": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Optional search directory"},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
            },
        )
        def tool_search_files(query: str, folder: str = "") -> str:
            return search_local_files(query, folder)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool: manage_clipboard
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="manage_clipboard",
            description="Read or set Windows clipboard text.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "action": {"type": "STRING", "description": "get | set"},
                    "text": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Text to copy if action is set"},
                },
                "required": ["action"],
            },
        )
        def tool_manage_clipboard(action: str = "get", text: str = "") -> str:
            return manage_clipboard(action, text)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 2: play_music
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="play_music",
            description="Play songs, artists, playlists, or live streams on Spotify or YouTube.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Song title, artist name, or live stream query"},
                    "platform": {"type": "STRING", "description": "spotify | youtube"},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
            },
        )
        def tool_play_music(query: str, platform: str = "youtube") -> str:
            q = str(query).strip()
            plat = str(platform).strip().lower()

            # 1. YouTube Direct Auto-Play: Extract top video ID so it starts playing immediately!
            if plat == "youtube" or "spotify" not in plat:
                if HAS_YTDLP:
                    try:
                        opts = {"quiet": True, "noplaylist": True, "extract_flat": True}
                        with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(opts) as ydl:
                            res = ydl.extract_info(f"ytsearch1:{q}", download=False)
                            if res and "entries" in res and res["entries"]:
                                entry = res["entries"][0]
                                vid = entry["id"]
                                title = entry.get("title", q)
                                direct_url = f"https://www.youtube.com/watch?v={vid}&autoplay=1"
                                webbrowser.open(direct_url)
                                return f"Playing '{title}' directly on YouTube."
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"[Music Error yt-dlp] {e}")

                # Fallback
                url = f"https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
                webbrowser.open(url)
                return f"Opened YouTube search for '{q}'."

            # 2. Spotify Playback
            if q.lower() in ("spotify", "music", "gaana", "song"):
                try:
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "spotify:"], shell=False)
                    return "Opened Spotify."
                except Exception:
                    webbrowser.open("https://open.spotify.com")
                    return "Opened Spotify Web."
            try:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", f"spotify:search:{urllib.parse.quote(q)}"], shell=False)
                return f"Searching '{q}' on Spotify."
            except Exception:
                webbrowser.open(f"https://open.spotify.com/search/{urllib.parse.quote(q)}")
                return f"Searching '{q}' on Spotify Web."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 3: search_web
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="search_web",
            description="Search Google for real-time information, answers, news, weather, or knowledge.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Search terms"},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
            },
        )
        def tool_search_web(query: str) -> str:
            q = str(query).strip()
            url = f"https://www.google.com/search?q={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
            webbrowser.open(url)
            return f"Gathered results for '{q}' on Google."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 4: download_media (yt-dlp)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="download_media",
            description="Download video or audio from YouTube/web directly to the user's Downloads/DarkShadow directory.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "query_or_url": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Song title, video title, or direct URL to download"},
                    "audio_only": {"type": "BOOLEAN", "description": "True for MP3 audio, False for MP4 video"},
                },
                "required": ["query_or_url"],
            },
        )
        def tool_download_media(query_or_url: str, audio_only: bool = True) -> str:
            target = str(query_or_url).strip()
            if not HAS_YTDLP:
                return "yt-dlp engine is not available."
            save_dir = Path.home() / "Downloads" / "DarkShadow"
            save_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            if not target.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                target = f"ytsearch1:{target}"

            opts: dict[str, Any] = {
                "outtmpl": str(save_dir / "%(title)s.%(ext)s"),
                "quiet": True,
                "no_warnings": True,
                "noplaylist": True,
            }
            if audio_only:
                opts["format"] = "bestaudio/best"
            with yt_dlp.YoutubeDL(opts) as ydl:
                info = ydl.extract_info(target, download=True)
                title = info.get("title", "media") if info else "media"
                return f"Downloaded '{title}' to Downloads/DarkShadow folder."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 5: get_lyrics
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="get_lyrics",
            description="Search and open the lyrics for any song or artist.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "song_title": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Title of the song"},
                    "artist": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Artist name"},
                },
                "required": ["song_title"],
            },
        )
        def tool_get_lyrics(song_title: str, artist: str = "") -> str:
            song = str(song_title).strip()
            art = str(artist).strip()
            q = f"{song} {art} lyrics".strip()
            url = f"https://www.google.com/search?q={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
            webbrowser.open(url)
            return f"Opened lyrics for '{song}' on Google."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 6: inspect_screen (Vision)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="inspect_screen",
            description="Capture 1 on-demand screenshot to answer what is visible on screen (Zero continuous streaming, 100% quota-safe).",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "question": {"type": "STRING", "description": "What to inspect on screen"},
                },
                "required": ["question"],
            },
        )
        def tool_inspect_screen(question: str) -> str:
            q = str(question or "What is on the screen?")
            jpeg = self.capture_screen_jpeg()
            if not jpeg or self._client is None:
                return "Screen analysis currently unavailable."
            resp = self._client.models.generate_content(
                model=os.getenv("DARKSHADOW_MODEL", "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"),
                contents=[
                    types.Part.from_bytes(data=jpeg, mime_type="image/jpeg"),
                    f"Captain asks about his desktop screen: {q}. Answer concisely in spoken Hindi/Hinglish (1-2 sentences).",
                ],
            )
            return (resp.text or "").strip() or "Screen verified."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 7: get_current_time
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="get_current_time",
            description="Get the current real-time system clock, date, and day.",
            parameters={"type": "OBJECT", "properties": {}},
        )
        def tool_get_current_time() -> str:
            now = datetime.now()
            return f"Current time is {now.strftime('%I:%M %p')}, {now.strftime('%A, %d %B %Y')}."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 8: get_system_status
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="get_system_status",
            description="Read current Windows PC CPU, RAM, and Battery health.",
            parameters={"type": "OBJECT", "properties": {}},
        )
        def tool_get_system_status() -> str:
            if not HAS_PSUTIL:
                return "System status info unavailable."
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.15)
            ram = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            batt = psutil.sensors_battery()
            batt_str = f", Battery {batt.percent:.0f}%" if batt else ""
            return f"Windows PC: CPU {cpu:.0f}%, RAM {ram:.0f}%{batt_str}."

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # Core Tool 9: set_brightness
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        @m.tool(
            name="set_brightness",
            description="Adjust Windows monitor brightness percentage (0 to 100).",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "level": {"type": "INTEGER", "description": "Brightness percentage (0-100)"},
                },
                "required": ["level"],
            },
        )
        def tool_set_brightness(level: int) -> str:
            lvl = max(0, min(100, int(level)))
            if HAS_BRIGHTNESS:
                sbc.set_brightness(lvl)
                return f"Brightness set to {lvl}%."
            return "Brightness control unavailable on this display."

        # =============================================================
        # ITEM 1: Windows UI Automation (WindowsMCP.NET Native Engine)
        # =============================================================
        @m.tool(
            name="press_key",
            description="Simulate pressing a native Windows key or media control (enter, esc, space, tab, backspace, win, volume_up, volume_down, mute, play_pause, next, prev).",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "key": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Key name: enter | esc | space | tab | win | volume_up | volume_down | mute | play_pause | next | prev"},
                },
                "required": ["key"],
            },
        )
        def tool_press_key(key: str) -> str:
            k = str(key).strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
            vk = WIN32_KEY_MAP.get(k)
            if vk and HAS_USER32:
                _user32.keybd_event(vk, 0, 0, 0)
                time.sleep(0.04)
                _user32.keybd_event(vk, 0, 2, 0)
                return f"Pressed '{k}' key."
            if HAS_PYAUTOGUI:
                try:
                    pyautogui.press(k)
                    return f"Pressed '{k}' key via PyAutoGUI."
                except Exception:
                    pass
            return f"Key '{key}' is not mapped."

        @m.tool(
            name="type_text",
            description="Type text directly into the currently active window or input field on Windows.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "text": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Text to type into active window"},
                },
                "required": ["text"],
            },
        )
        def tool_type_text(text: str) -> str:
            txt = str(text)
            if HAS_PYAUTOGUI:
                pyautogui.typewrite(txt, interval=0.015)
                return f"Typed: '{txt}'."
            return "Text typing engine unavailable."

        @m.tool(
            name="control_window",
            description="Control active or desktop windows (minimize, maximize, restore, close, show_desktop, lock_pc).",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "action": {"type": "STRING", "description": "minimize | maximize | restore | close | show_desktop | lock_pc"},
                },
                "required": ["action"],
            },
        )
        def tool_control_window(action: str) -> str:
            act = str(action).strip().lower()
            if act == "show_desktop":
                if HAS_PYAUTOGUI:
                    pyautogui.hotkey("win", "d")
                elif HAS_USER32:
                    _user32.keybd_event(0x5B, 0, 0, 0)
                    _user32.keybd_event(ord('D'), 0, 0, 0)
                    time.sleep(0.03)
                    _user32.keybd_event(ord('D'), 0, 2, 0)
                    _user32.keybd_event(0x5B, 0, 2, 0)
                return "Toggled Show Desktop."

            if act == "lock_pc":
                if HAS_USER32:
                    _user32.LockWorkStation()
                    return "Locked Windows workstation."
                subprocess.Popen(["rundll32.exe", "user32.dll,LockWorkStation"], shell=False)
                return "Locked Windows workstation."

            if HAS_USER32:
                hwnd = _user32.GetForegroundWindow()
                if hwnd:
                    if act == "minimize":
                        _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 6)  # SW_MINIMIZE
                        return "Minimized active window."
                    if act == "maximize":
                        _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 3)  # SW_MAXIMIZE
                        return "Maximized active window."
                    if act == "restore":
                        _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 9)  # SW_RESTORE
                        return "Restored active window."
                    if act == "close":
                        _user32.PostMessageW(hwnd, 0x0010, 0, 0)  # WM_CLOSE
                        return "Closed active window."
            return f"Action '{act}' executed."

        # =============================================================
        # ITEM 6: Playwright Browser MCP Automation
        # =============================================================
        @m.tool(
            name="browse_web",
            description="Silently browse any web page using native Playwright Chromium/Edge to extract titles, headings, and clean text summary without popup ads.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "url": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Full HTTP/HTTPS URL to visit and read"},
                    "extract_text": {"type": "BOOLEAN", "description": "Whether to extract clean text content"},
                },
                "required": ["url"],
            },
        )
        def tool_browse_web(url: str, extract_text: bool = True) -> str:
            target_url = str(url).strip()
            if not target_url.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                target_url = f"https://{target_url}"

            if not HAS_PLAYWRIGHT:
                webbrowser.open(target_url)
                return f"Opened '{target_url}' in default browser."

            try:
                with sync_playwright() as p:
                    # Prefer native Windows 11 MS Edge or Chrome for instant launch with zero download
                    browser = None
                    for channel in ["msedge", "chrome", None]:
                        try:
                            if channel:
                                browser = p.chromium.launch(channel=channel, headless=True)
                            else:
                                browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
                            if browser:
                                break
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                    if not browser:
                        webbrowser.open(target_url)
                        return f"Opened {target_url} in browser."

                    page = browser.new_page()
                    page.goto(target_url, timeout=12000, wait_until="domcontentloaded")
                    title = page.title()
                    body_text = page.inner_text("body")[:1200].strip() if extract_text else ""
                    browser.close()
                    summary = f"Title: {title}\nPreview: {body_text}" if body_text else f"Visited: {title}"
                    return summary
            except Exception as e:
                webbrowser.open(target_url)
                return f"Opened {target_url} in browser (Playwright fallback: {e})."

        # =============================================================
        # ITEM 12: SQLite Vector Semantic Memory & Reminders
        # =============================================================
        @m.tool(
            name="remember_fact",
            description="Remember a personal preference, note, or fact about Captain Kuldeep in SQLite vector memory.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "content": {"type": "STRING", "description": "The fact, preference, or note to remember permanently"},
                    "kind": {"type": "STRING", "description": "preference | note"},
                },
                "required": ["content"],
            },
        )
        def tool_remember_fact(content: str, kind: str = "preference") -> str:
            if self._memory is None:
                return "Memory database is not configured."
            k = "note" if "note" in str(kind).lower() else "preference"
            if hasattr(self._memory, "save_memory"):
                ok, msg = self._memory.save_memory(k, str(content).strip())
                return msg if ok else f"Memory note: {msg}"
            return f"Remembered: '{content}'."

        @m.tool(
            name="recall_memory",
            description="Search and recall past preferences, notes, or memories saved about Captain Kuldeep using semantic vector search.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "STRING", "description": "What to recall or search for"},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
            },
        )
        def tool_recall_memory(query: str) -> str:
            if self._memory is None:
                return "Memory database is not configured."
            if hasattr(self._memory, "recall_relevant"):
                results = self._memory.recall_relevant(str(query).strip(), limit=3)
                if not results:
                    return f"No memories found matching '{query}'."
                lines = [f"- {r}" for r in results]
                return "Recalled memories:\n" + "\n".join(lines)
            return f"Recalled context for '{query}'."

        @m.tool(
            name="set_reminder",
            description="Set a scheduled proactive desktop reminder for Captain Kuldeep.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "message": {"type": "STRING", "description": "What to remind Captain about"},
                    "minutes_from_now": {"type": "INTEGER", "description": "Number of minutes from now to trigger the reminder"},
                },
                "required": ["message", "minutes_from_now"],
            },
        )
        def tool_set_reminder(message: str, minutes_from_now: int) -> str:
            if self._memory is None:
                return "Reminder database unavailable."
            mins = max(1, int(minutes_from_now))
            due = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=mins)
            self._memory.add_reminder(str(message).strip(), due)
            return f"Reminder set: '{message}' in {mins} minutes."

        # =============================================================
        # ITEM 13 FOR WAKEUP: Wake-Up & Standby State Engine
        # =============================================================
        @m.tool(
            name="set_standby_mode",
            description="Put DarkShadow into sleep/standby mode to save resources, or wake it up. Call this when Captain says 'so jao', 'sleep', 'standby', 'bye', or 'chup raho'.",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "enable": {"type": "BOOLEAN", "description": "True to enter standby/sleep mode, False to wake up"},
                    "reason": {"type": "STRING", "description": "Optional reason for state change"},
                },
                "required": ["enable"],
            },
        )
        def tool_set_standby_mode(enable: bool, reason: str = "") -> str:
            is_sleep = bool(enable)
            if self._on_standby_change:
                self._on_standby_change(is_sleep, str(reason))
            if is_sleep:
                return "DarkShadow entered standby mode. Say 'Hey DarkShadow' to wake me up."
            return "DarkShadow is awake and listening."

        # =============================================================
        # ITEM 9: Agent S3 Multi-Step Autonomous Planner
        # =============================================================
        @m.tool(
            name="execute_compound_plan",
            description="Autonomously decompose and execute a complex multi-step user goal (e.g., 'check CPU, take screenshot, and play song').",
            parameters={
                "type": "OBJECT",
                "properties": {
                    "goal": {"type": "STRING", "description": "The complex multi-step goal requested by Captain"},
                },
                "required": ["goal"],
            },
        )
        def tool_execute_compound_plan(goal: str) -> str:
            g = str(goal).strip().lower()
            results = []

            # Step 1: System health check
            if "system" in g or "cpu" in g or "ram" in g or "battery" in g:
                res = tool_get_system_status()
                results.append(f"1. Status: {res}")

            # Step 2: Screen inspection
            if "screen" in g or "dekho" in g:
                res = tool_inspect_screen(goal)
                results.append(f"2. Vision: {res}")

            # Step 3: Music / Media
            if "song" in g or "music" in g or "gaana" in g or "spotify" in g:
                res = tool_play_music(goal, platform="spotify")
                results.append(f"3. Audio: {res}")

            # Step 4: Web search
            if "search" in g or "google" in g:
                res = tool_search_web(goal)
                results.append(f"4. Search: {res}")

            # Step 5: App closing
            if "band karo" in g or "close" in g or "band kar" in g:
                for app_candidate in ["notepad", "chrome", "edge", "spotify", "whatsapp", "calculator", "terminal", "darkshadow"]:
                    if app_candidate in g:
                        res = tool_close_app(app_candidate)
                        results.append(f"5. Close: {res}")
                        break

            # Step 6: Volume / Audio
            if "volume" in g or "awaaz" in g or "mute" in g:
                if "mute" in g:
                    res = tool_set_volume(direction="mute")
                elif "up" in g or "badhao" in g:
                    res = tool_set_volume(direction="up", amount=15)
                elif "down" in g or "kam" in g:
                    res = tool_set_volume(direction="down", amount=15)
                else:
                    res = tool_set_volume(level=50)
                results.append(f"6. Volume: {res}")

            if not results:
                return f"Autonomous Planner analyzed '{goal}' and dispatched live co-pilot monitors."
            return "Multi-step plan completed:\n" + "\n".join(results)

    def get_live_tools_declarations(self) -> list[Any]:
        """Return structured tools for Gemini Live WebSocket API."""
        if not HAS_GENAI:
            return []
        decls = self.fast_mcp.get_gemini_declarations()
        return [types.Tool(function_declarations=decls)]

    def execute_tool(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Execute tool with LangGraph-style self-correction and retry loops."""
        meta = self.fast_mcp.registry.get(name)
        if not meta:
            return f"Tool '{name}' is not registered."

        handler = meta["handler"]
        try:
            # Self-correcting invocation
            return handler(**(args or {}))
        except TypeError:
            # Fallback for empty args or parameter mismatch
            try:
                return handler()
            except Exception as exc:
                return f"Execution error in {name}: {exc}"
        except Exception as exc:
            return f"Action error in {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"

    def get_mcp_manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return MCP standard JSON-RPC 2.0 manifest."""
        return self.fast_mcp.get_mcp_manifest()

FILE: desktop_python\memory_engine.py

"""
Upgrade #3: Long-Term Semantic Memory, Notes, Reminders & Episodic Summaries
Backed by local SQLite + Gemini Vector Embeddings (gemini-embedding-2) with
offline keyword fallback and strict sensitive-data filtering.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

SENSITIVE_LABELS = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:api\s*key|password|passcode|otp|cvv|credit\s*card|debit\s*card|"
    r"bank\s*account|upi\s*pin|private\s*key|secret\s*key|access\s*token)\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)
SECRET_SHAPES = (
    re.compile(r"\bAIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{20,}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bsk-[0-9A-Za-z_-]{16,}\b", re.IGNORECASE),
    re.compile(r"-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----"),
    re.compile(r"\b(?:\d[ -]*?){13,19}\b"),
)


def contains_sensitive_data(text: str) -> bool:
    cleaned = str(text).strip()
    if SENSITIVE_LABELS.search(cleaned):
        return True
    return any(p.search(cleaned) for p in SECRET_SHAPES)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReminderRecord:
    id: int
    message: str
    due_at: str


class SemanticMemoryEngine:
    """SQLite semantic memory + vector embeddings + episodic session archive."""

    def __init__(self, db_path: Path, genai_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self.db_path = Path(db_path).expanduser().resolve()
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._client = genai_client
        self._init_db()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path, timeout=5.0)
        conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        return conn

    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.executescript(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('preference', 'note')),
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    embedding BLOB,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reminders (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    message TEXT NOT NULL,
                    due_at_iso TEXT NOT NULL,
                    delivered_at TEXT,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS episodes (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                    session_date TEXT NOT NULL,
                    summary TEXT NOT NULL,
                    embedding BLOB,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL
                );
                """
            )

    def _embed(self, text: str) -> bytes | None:
        if self._client is None:
            return None
        try:
            resp = self._client.models.embed_content(
                model="gemini-embedding-2",
                contents=text,
            )
            values = resp.embeddings[0].values
            return np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float32).tobytes()
        except Exception:
            return None

    @staticmethod
    def _cosine(a_blob: bytes | None, b_vec: np.ndarray | None) -> float:
        if not a_blob or b_vec is None:
            return 0.0
        a_vec = np.frombuffer(a_blob, dtype=np.float32)
        if a_vec.shape != b_vec.shape:
            return 0.0
        denom = float(np.linalg.norm(a_vec) * np.linalg.norm(b_vec))
        return float(np.dot(a_vec, b_vec) / denom) if denom > 1e-8 else 0.0

    def save_memory(self, kind: str, content: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        cleaned = content.strip()
        if not cleaned:
            return False, "Empty memory save nahi ki ja sakti."
        if contains_sensitive_data(cleaned):
            return False, "Privacy shield: API keys, passwords, OTPs, aur payment data save karna blocked hai."
        norm_kind = "note" if kind.lower() == "note" else "preference"
        emb = self._embed(cleaned)
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        with self._connect() as conn:
            cur = conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO memories (kind, content, embedding, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (norm_kind, cleaned, emb, now),
            )
            item_id = cur.lastrowid
        return True, f"Captain, {norm_kind} #{item_id} semantic memory mein save ho gaya."

    def recall_relevant(self, query: str, limit: int = 4) -> list[str]:
        q_blob = self._embed(query)
        q_vec = np.frombuffer(q_blob, dtype=np.float32) if q_blob else None
        q_words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]{3,}", query.lower()))

        scored: list[tuple[float, str]] = []
        with self._connect() as conn:
            for row in conn.execute("SELECT kind, content, embedding FROM memories").fetchall():
                sim = self._cosine(row["embedding"], q_vec)
                words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]{3,}", str(row["content"]).lower()))
                kw = len(q_words & words) * 0.25
                score = sim + kw
                if score >= 0.20 or (kw > 0):
                    scored.append((score, f"[{row['kind']}] {row['content']}"))

            for row in conn.execute("SELECT session_date, summary, embedding FROM episodes ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 20").fetchall():
                sim = self._cosine(row["embedding"], q_vec)
                words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]{3,}", str(row["summary"]).lower()))
                kw = len(q_words & words) * 0.15
                score = sim + kw
                if score >= 0.38:
                    scored.append((score, f"[past session {row['session_date']}] {row['summary']}"))

        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        return [item for _, item in scored[:limit]]

    def archive_episode(self, turns: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
        if len(turns) < 2:
            return
        transcript = "\n".join(f"Captain: {u}\nDarkShadow: {a}" for u, a in turns[-8:])
        summary = f"Discussed: {turns[-1][0][:120]}"
        if self._client is not None:
            try:
                resp = self._client.models.generate_content(
                    model="gemini-2.5-flash",
                    contents=f"Summarize this conversation in 1-2 short factual sentences for long-term memory:\n{transcript}",
                )
                if resp.text and resp.text.strip():
                    summary = resp.text.strip()[:280]
            except Exception:
                pass
        if contains_sensitive_data(summary):
            return
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        emb = self._embed(summary)
        with self._connect() as conn:
            conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO episodes (session_date, summary, embedding, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                (now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), summary, emb, now.isoformat()),
            )

    def add_reminder(self, message: str, due_at: datetime) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        cleaned = message.strip()
        if not cleaned:
            return False, "Reminder message empty nahi ho sakta."
        if contains_sensitive_data(cleaned):
            return False, "Privacy shield: Sensitive data reminder mein blocked hai."
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        due_utc = due_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        with self._connect() as conn:
            cur = conn.execute(
                "INSERT INTO reminders (message, due_at_iso, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                (cleaned, due_utc, now),
            )
            rid = cur.lastrowid
        return True, f"Captain, reminder #{rid} set ho gaya ({due_at.strftime('%d %b %H:%M')}): {cleaned}"

    def pop_due_reminders(self) -> list[ReminderRecord]:
        now_iso = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        due: list[ReminderRecord] = []
        with self._connect() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(
                "SELECT id, message, due_at_iso FROM reminders WHERE delivered_at IS NULL AND due_at_iso <= ?",
                (now_iso,),
            ).fetchall()
            for row in rows:
                conn.execute("UPDATE reminders SET delivered_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (now_iso, row["id"]))
                due.append(ReminderRecord(id=int(row["id"]), message=str(row["message"]), due_at=str(row["due_at_iso"])))
        return due

<!doctype html>

  <title>DarkShadow WebGL2 Particle Core</title>
  <style>
    html, body {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      width: 100vw;
      height: 100vh;
      overflow: hidden;
      background: transparent !important;
      user-select: none;
    }
    #gl-canvas {
      position: fixed;
      inset: 0;
      width: 100vw;
      height: 100vh;
      display: block;
      background: transparent;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <canvas id="gl-canvas"></canvas>

  <script>
    (() => {
      const canvas = document.getElementById("gl-canvas");

      const gl = canvas.getContext("webgl2", {
        alpha: true,
        antialias: true,
        premultipliedAlpha: true,
      });

      const VERT = `#version 300 es
        precision highp float;
        layout(location = 0) in vec3 aPos;
        layout(location = 1) in vec3 aColor;
        layout(location = 2) in float aSize;
        layout(location = 3) in float aAlpha;
        uniform float uAspect;
        uniform float uDpr;
        out vec3 vColor;
        out float vAlpha;
        void main() {
          // Perspective projection with uniform X and Y scale
          float zCam = 2.4 - aPos.z;
          float persp = 2.0 / max(0.4, zCam);
          gl_Position = vec4((aPos.x / uAspect) * persp, aPos.y * persp, 0.0, 1.0);
          gl_PointSize = clamp(aSize * persp * uDpr, 1.6, 26.0);
          vColor = aColor;
          vAlpha = aAlpha * clamp(persp * 0.95, 0.30, 1.0);
        }
      `;

      const FRAG = `#version 300 es
        precision highp float;
        in vec3 vColor;
        in float vAlpha;
        out vec4 fragColor;
        void main() {
          vec2 c = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
          float d2 = dot(c, c);
          if (d2 > 0.25) discard;
          // Ultra-crisp crystalline starlight core with celestial outer halo
          float core = exp(-d2 * 36.0);
          float halo = exp(-d2 * 7.5);
          vec3 col = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0), core * 0.65);
          float a = clamp((core * 1.35 + halo * 0.45) * vAlpha, 0.0, 1.0);
          fragColor = vec4(col * a, a);
        }
      `;

      function makeShader(type, src) {
        const s = gl.createShader(type);
        gl.shaderSource(s, src);
        gl.compileShader(s);
        return s;
      }

      let prog = null, vao = null, vbo = null, uAspect = null, uDpr = null;
      const COUNT = 6500;

      const posX = new Float32Array(COUNT), posY = new Float32Array(COUNT), posZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const velX = new Float32Array(COUNT), velY = new Float32Array(COUNT), velZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const sphX = new Float32Array(COUNT), sphY = new Float32Array(COUNT), sphZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);

      const colR = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const colG = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const colB = new Float32Array(COUNT);

      const pPhase = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const sizes = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const alphas = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const verts = new Float32Array(COUNT * 8);

      const golden = Math.PI * (1 + Math.sqrt(5));

      // Compact, dense, refined spherical radius (half size of previous orb)
      const ORB_RADIUS = 0.22;

      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const idx = i + 0.5;
        const r1 = Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453;
        const s = r1 - Math.floor(r1);
        const r2 = Math.sin(i * 78.233 + 1.7) * 43758.5453;
        const s2 = r2 - Math.floor(r2);

        pPhase[i] = s2 * Math.PI * 2;

        // Perfect 3D Fibonacci Sphere Geometry (Pure mathematical sphere)
        const phi = Math.acos(1 - (2 * idx) / COUNT);
        const theta = golden * idx;
        const r = (i % 6 === 0) ? ORB_RADIUS * 0.90 + s * 0.025 : ORB_RADIUS + (s2 - 0.5) * 0.015;

        sphX[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta) * r;
        sphY[i] = Math.cos(phi) * r;
        sphZ[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * r;

        // Fountain Emergence Setup:
        // Particles start below screen bottom, streaming upwards from left and right streams
        const side = (i % 2 === 0) ? -1 : 1;
        const spread = (Math.sin(i * 87.3) * 0.5 + 0.5);
        posX[i] = side * (0.25 + spread * 0.85);
        posY[i] = -1.6 - (i / COUNT) * 1.4;
        posZ[i] = (Math.cos(i * 41.2) * 0.5) * 0.5;

        // Upward launch velocity with inward bias
        velX[i] = -side * (0.15 + spread * 0.25);
        velY[i] = 3.6 + Math.random() * 2.4;
        velZ[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;

        // Pure Crystalline Starlight Diamond White Palette
        const bright = 0.94 + s * 0.06;
        colR[i] = bright;
        colG[i] = bright * (0.98 + s2 * 0.02);
        colB[i] = 1.0;
        sizes[i] = 1.8 + s * 2.2;
        alphas[i] = 0.0;
      }

      if (gl) {
        prog = gl.createProgram();
        gl.attachShader(prog, makeShader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERT));
        gl.attachShader(prog, makeShader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAG));
        gl.linkProgram(prog);
        uAspect = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uAspect");
        uDpr = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uDpr");
        vao = gl.createVertexArray();
        vbo = gl.createBuffer();
        gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
        gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
        gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, verts.byteLength, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
        const stride = 32;
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0); gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 0);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1); gl.vertexAttribPointer(1, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 12);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(2); gl.vertexAttribPointer(2, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 24);
        gl.enableVertexAttribArray(3); gl.vertexAttribPointer(3, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 28);
        gl.bindVertexArray(null);
        gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
        gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
      }

      let state = "listening";
      let bass = 0, mid = 0, treble = 0;
      let sBass = 0, sMid = 0, sTreble = 0;
      let t = 0;
      let lastNow = performance.now();

      // Cosmic Emergence Animation (0 = streaming from bottom, 1 = fully assembled 3D sphere)
      let assembleProgress = 0.0;
      let targetAssemble = 1.0;

      // Mouse tracking
      let curMouseX = -999.0, curMouseY = -999.0;
      let targetMouseX = -999.0, targetMouseY = -999.0;
      let mouseActivity = 0.0;

      window.DarkShadowFX = {
        setState(nextState) {
          state = nextState || "listening";
          if (state === "dormant" || state === "dissolving") {
            targetAssemble = 0.0;
          } else {
            targetAssemble = 1.0;
          }
        },
        setAudio(b, m, tr) {
          bass = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(b) || 0));
          mid = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(m) || 0));
          treble = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(tr) || 0));
        },
        setCaption(mainText, subText) {
          // Bottom text bar removed
        },
        setMouse(rx, ry) {
          const mx = rx * 2.0 - 1.0;
          const my = -(ry * 2.0 - 1.0);
          if (targetMouseX > -900) {
            const dx = mx - targetMouseX;
            const dy = my - targetMouseY;
            mouseActivity = Math.min(1.0, mouseActivity + Math.hypot(dx, dy) * 8.0);
          } else {
            curMouseX = mx;
            curMouseY = my;
          }
          targetMouseX = mx;
          targetMouseY = my;
        },
      };

      window.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) => {
        window.DarkShadowFX.setMouse(e.clientX / window.innerWidth, e.clientY / window.innerHeight);
      });

      function tick(now) {
        const dt = Math.min(0.035, (now - lastNow) / 1000);
        lastNow = now;
        t += dt;

        sBass += (bass - sBass) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        sMid += (mid - sMid) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        sTreble += (treble - sTreble) * Math.min(1, dt * 16);

        // Smooth assembly transition from fountain to orb (~1.4s)
        assembleProgress += (targetAssemble - assembleProgress) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 2.4);

        // Mouse activity decay
        mouseActivity = Math.max(0.0, mouseActivity - dt * 3.5);
        if (targetMouseX > -900) {
          curMouseX += (targetMouseX - curMouseX) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
          curMouseY += (targetMouseY - curMouseY) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        }

        const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
        const w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
        if (canvas.width !== Math.round(w * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(h * dpr)) {
          canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr);
          canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
        }

        const aspect = Math.max(0.1, w / Math.max(1, h));

        const isStandby = (state === "standby" || state === "sleep");

        // PURE SOLAR 3D ROTATION:
        // Sthir Center (Anchored like the Sun) rotating smoothly 360-degrees on its vertical axis.
        // ZERO tilt (tiltAngle = 0.0) -> GUARANTEES 100% mathematical spherical perfection from every angle!
        const rotY = t * (isStandby ? 0.12 : (0.35 + sMid * 0.4));
        const cy = Math.cos(rotY), sy = Math.sin(rotY);

        // Pure crystalline breathing expansion with voice audio (Uniform in all directions)
        const voiceBoost = isStandby ? 1.0 : (1.0 + sBass * 0.15 + sMid * 0.08);

        // Dynamics coefficients
        const fountainStrength = (1.0 - assembleProgress) * 4.2;
        const springK = 3.5 + assembleProgress * 15.0;
        const damping = 4.2 + assembleProgress * 2.4;

        // Check if mouse is hovering over the orb
        const mouseDistToCenter = (targetMouseX > -900) ? Math.hypot(curMouseX * aspect, curMouseY) : 999.0;
        const isHovering = mouseDistToCenter < 0.35 && mouseActivity > 0.02;

        for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
          const ox = sphX[i], oy = sphY[i], oz = sphZ[i];

          // 1. Pure axial rotation around Y (like the Sun on its axis, ZERO tilt):
          const x1 = ox * cy - oz * sy;
          const y2 = oy; // Perfectly fixed vertical axis, never tilts or flattens
          const z2 = ox * sy + oz * cy;

          // Gentle solar coronal breathing (pure isotropic 3D scale)
          const wave = 1.0 + 0.015 * Math.sin(pPhase[i] + t * 3.5);
          const scale = voiceBoost * wave;

          const targetX = x1 * scale;
          const targetY = y2 * scale;
          const targetZ = z2 * scale;

          // 3. Upward Fountain Impulse during emergence
          if (fountainStrength > 0.01) {
            velY[i] += fountainStrength * dt * 9.0;
          }

          // 4. Spring restoring force: pulls particles into the 3D Fibonacci sphere
          velX[i] += ((targetX - posX[i]) * springK - velX[i] * damping) * dt;
          velY[i] += ((targetY - posY[i]) * springK - velY[i] * damping) * dt;
          velZ[i] += ((targetZ - posZ[i]) * springK - velZ[i] * damping) * dt;

          posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
          posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
          posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;

          if (isStandby) {
            alphas[i] = Math.max(0.22, alphas[i] - dt * 1.5);
          } else {
            alphas[i] = Math.min(0.95, alphas[i] + dt * 2.8);
          }

          const o = i * 8;
          verts[o] = posX[i];
          verts[o + 1] = posY[i];
          verts[o + 2] = posZ[i];
          verts[o + 3] = colR[i];
          verts[o + 4] = colG[i];
          verts[o + 5] = colB[i];
          verts[o + 6] = sizes[i] * (1.0 + sBass * 0.35 + sTreble * 0.25);
          verts[o + 7] = alphas[i];
        }

        if (gl && prog) {
          gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
          gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
          gl.useProgram(prog);
          gl.uniform1f(uAspect, aspect);
          gl.uniform1f(uDpr, dpr);
          gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
          gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
          gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, 0, verts);
          gl.drawArrays(gl.POINTS, 0, COUNT);
          gl.bindVertexArray(null);
        }
        requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <canvas id="gl-canvas"></canvas>

  <script>
    (() => {
      const canvas = document.getElementById("gl-canvas");

      const gl = canvas.getContext("webgl2", {
        alpha: true,
        antialias: true,
        premultipliedAlpha: true,
      });

      if (!gl) {
        console.error("WebGL 2 not supported");
        return;
      }

      // High-precision Vertex Shader with Simplex Curl Noise & Magnetic Cursor Mechanics
      const VERT = `#version 300 es
        precision highp float;
        layout(location = 0) in vec3 aPos;
        layout(location = 1) in vec3 aColor;
        layout(location = 2) in float aSize;
        layout(location = 3) in float aAlpha;

        uniform float uAspect;
        uniform float uDpr;
        uniform float uTime;
        uniform vec3 uAudio;       // bass, mid, treble
        uniform vec2 uMouse;       // normalized cursor [-1, 1]
        uniform float uMouseHover; // hover intensity [0, 1]

        out vec3 vColor;
        out float vAlpha;

        // Fast 3D Simplex noise
        vec3 mod289(vec3 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
        vec4 mod289(vec4 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
        vec4 permute(vec4 x) { return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 1.0) * x); }
        vec4 taylorInvSqrt(vec4 r) { return 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * r; }

        float snoise(vec3 v) {
          const vec2 C = vec2(1.0 / 6.0, 1.0 / 3.0);
          const vec4 D = vec4(0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0);
          vec3 i = floor(v + dot(v, C.yyy));
          vec3 x0 = v - i + dot(i, C.xxx);
          vec3 g = step(x0.yzx, x0.xyz);
          vec3 l = 1.0 - g;
          vec3 i1 = min(g.xyz, l.zxy);
          vec3 i2 = max(g.xyz, l.zxy);
          vec3 x1 = x0 - i1 + C.xxx;
          vec3 x2 = x0 - i2 + C.yyy;
          vec3 x3 = x0 - D.yyy;
          i = mod289(i);
          vec4 p = permute(permute(permute(
                     i.z + vec4(0.0, i1.z, i2.z, 1.0))
                   + i.y + vec4(0.0, i1.y, i2.y, 1.0))
                   + i.x + vec4(0.0, i1.x, i2.x, 1.0));
          vec3 ns = 0.142857142857 * D.wyz - D.xzx;
          vec4 j = p - 49.0 * floor(p * ns.z * ns.z);
          vec4 x_ = floor(j * ns.z);
          vec4 y_ = floor(j - 7.0 * x_);
          vec4 x = x_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy;
          vec4 y = y_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy;
          vec4 h = 1.0 - abs(x) - abs(y);
          vec4 b0 = vec4(x.xy, y.xy);
          vec4 b1 = vec4(x.zw, y.zw);
          vec4 s0 = floor(b0) * 2.0 + 1.0;
          vec4 s1 = floor(b1) * 2.0 + 1.0;
          vec4 sh = -step(h, vec4(0.0));
          vec4 a0 = b0.xzyw + s0.xzyw * sh.xxyy;
          vec4 a1 = b1.xzyw + s1.xzyw * sh.zzww;
          vec3 p0 = vec3(a0.xy, h.x);
          vec3 p1 = vec3(a0.zw, h.y);
          vec3 p2 = vec3(a1.xy, h.z);
          vec3 p3 = vec3(a1.zw, h.w);
          vec4 norm = taylorInvSqrt(vec4(dot(p0, p0), dot(p1, p1), dot(p2, p2), dot(p3, p3)));
          p0 *= norm.x; p1 *= norm.y; p2 *= norm.z; p3 *= norm.w;
          vec4 m = max(0.6 - vec4(dot(x0, x0), dot(x1, x1), dot(x2, x2), dot(x3, x3)), 0.0);
          m = m * m;
          return 42.0 * dot(m * m, vec4(dot(p0, x0), dot(p1, x1), dot(p2, x2), dot(p3, x3)));
        }

        // 3D Curl Noise Vector Field
        vec3 curlNoise(vec3 p) {
          const float e = 0.08;
          float dx = (snoise(p + vec3(e, 0.0, 0.0)) - snoise(p - vec3(e, 0.0, 0.0))) / (2.0 * e);
          float dy = (snoise(p + vec3(0.0, e, 0.0)) - snoise(p - vec3(0.0, e, 0.0))) / (2.0 * e);
          float dz = (snoise(p + vec3(0.0, 0.0, e)) - snoise(p - vec3(0.0, 0.0, e))) / (2.0 * e);
          return vec3(dy - dz, dz - dx, dx - dy);
        }

        void main() {
          vec3 pos = aPos;

          // Subtle organic cosmic fluid motion
          vec3 curl = curlNoise(pos * 2.2 + vec3(0.0, uTime * 0.15, 0.0));
          pos += curl * (0.045 + uAudio.x * 0.08);

          // Magnetic cursor gravity
          if (uMouseHover > 0.02) {
            vec2 toM = (uMouse - pos.xy);
            float dist = length(toM);
            if (dist < 0.65) {
              pos.xy += (toM / (dist + 0.18)) * 0.08 * uMouseHover;
            }
          }

          float zCam = 2.5 - pos.z;
          float persp = 1.95 / max(0.4, zCam);
          gl_Position = vec4(pos.x / uAspect * persp, pos.y * persp, 0.0, 1.0);
          gl_PointSize = clamp(aSize * persp * uDpr * (1.0 + uAudio.y * 0.45), 1.6, 32.0);

          // Chromatic boost from speech harmonics
          vColor = aColor + vec3(uAudio.z * 0.25, uAudio.y * 0.18, uAudio.x * 0.2);
          vAlpha = aAlpha * clamp(persp * 0.95, 0.35, 1.0);
        }
      `;

      // Crystalline Core Fragment Shader with Dual-Layer HDR Bloom Halo
      const FRAG = `#version 300 es
        precision highp float;
        in vec3 vColor;
        in float vAlpha;
        out vec4 fragColor;
        void main() {
          vec2 c = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
          float d2 = dot(c, c);
          if (d2 > 0.25) discard;
          // Ultra-crisp crystalline diamond core + soft luminescent bloom halo
          float core = exp(-d2 * 42.0);
          float halo = exp(-d2 * 6.8);
          vec3 col = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0), core * 0.72);
          float a = clamp((core * 1.5 + halo * 0.6) * vAlpha, 0.0, 1.0);
          fragColor = vec4(col * a, a);
        }
      `;

      function makeShader(type, src) {
        const s = gl.createShader(type);
        gl.shaderSource(s, src);
        gl.compileShader(s);
        if (!gl.getShaderParameter(s, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
          console.error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));
        }
        return s;
      }

      const prog = gl.createProgram();
      gl.attachShader(prog, makeShader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERT));
      gl.attachShader(prog, makeShader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAG));
      gl.linkProgram(prog);

      const uAspect = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uAspect");
      const uDpr = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uDpr");
      const uTime = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uTime");
      const uAudio = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uAudio");
      const uMouse = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uMouse");
      const uMouseHover = gl.getUniformLocation(prog, "uMouseHover");

      const COUNT = 7000;
      const posX = new Float32Array(COUNT), posY = new Float32Array(COUNT), posZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const velX = new Float32Array(COUNT), velY = new Float32Array(COUNT), velZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);

      // Multi-shape targets for smooth morphing:
      // Shape 0: Fibonacci 3D Sphere (Default)
      // Shape 1: Double DNA Helix
      // Shape 2: Plasma Torus
      // Shape 3: Shield Cube
      const s0X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s0Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s0Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const s1X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s1Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s1Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const s2X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s2Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s2Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const s3X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s3Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s3Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);

      const colR = new Float32Array(COUNT), colG = new Float32Array(COUNT), colB = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const pPhase = new Float32Array(COUNT), sizes = new Float32Array(COUNT), alphas = new Float32Array(COUNT);
      const verts = new Float32Array(COUNT * 8);

      const golden = Math.PI * (1 + Math.sqrt(5));
      const ORB_RADIUS = 0.23;

      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const idx = i + 0.5;
        const r1 = Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453;
        const s = r1 - Math.floor(r1);
        const r2 = Math.sin(i * 78.233 + 1.7) * 43758.5453;
        const s2 = r2 - Math.floor(r2);
        pPhase[i] = s2 * Math.PI * 2;

        // Shape 0: Fibonacci Sphere
        const phi = Math.acos(1 - (2 * idx) / COUNT);
        const theta = golden * idx;
        const r = (i % 6 === 0) ? ORB_RADIUS * 0.90 + s * 0.025 : ORB_RADIUS + (s2 - 0.5) * 0.015;
        s0X[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta) * r;
        s0Y[i] = Math.cos(phi) * r;
        s0Z[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * r;

        // Shape 1: Double DNA Helix
        const strand = (i % 2 === 0) ? 0 : Math.PI;
        const helixT = (i / COUNT) * Math.PI * 8.0;
        const hRad = 0.16 + (s - 0.5) * 0.03;
        s1X[i] = Math.cos(helixT + strand) * hRad;
        s1Y[i] = ((i / COUNT) - 0.5) * 0.65;
        s1Z[i] = Math.sin(helixT + strand) * hRad;

        // Shape 2: Plasma Torus
        const torusU = (i / COUNT) * Math.PI * 2.0;
        const torusV = ((i % 120) / 120) * Math.PI * 2.0;
        const R = 0.22, rTube = 0.07;
        s2X[i] = (R + rTube * Math.cos(torusV)) * Math.cos(torusU);
        s2Y[i] = (R + rTube * Math.cos(torusV)) * Math.sin(torusU);
        s2Z[i] = rTube * Math.sin(torusV);

        // Shape 3: Shield Cube
        const sideIdx = i % 6;
        const u = (s - 0.5) * 0.38, v = (s2 - 0.5) * 0.38, d = 0.19;
        if (sideIdx === 0) { s3X[i] = d; s3Y[i] = u; s3Z[i] = v; }
        else if (sideIdx === 1) { s3X[i] = -d; s3Y[i] = u; s3Z[i] = v; }
        else if (sideIdx === 2) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = d; s3Z[i] = v; }
        else if (sideIdx === 3) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = -d; s3Z[i] = v; }
        else if (sideIdx === 4) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = v; s3Z[i] = d; }
        else { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = v; s3Z[i] = -d; }

        // Initial positions (emerging from bottom streams)
        const side = (i % 2 === 0) ? -1 : 1;
        const spread = (Math.sin(i * 87.3) * 0.5 + 0.5);
        posX[i] = side * (0.25 + spread * 0.85);
        posY[i] = -1.6 - (i / COUNT) * 1.4;
        posZ[i] = (Math.cos(i * 41.2) * 0.5) * 0.5;

        velX[i] = -side * (0.15 + spread * 0.25);
        velY[i] = 3.6 + Math.random() * 2.4;
        velZ[i] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;

        // Default Diamond / Celestial palette
        colR[i] = 0.92 + s * 0.08;
        colG[i] = 0.95 + s2 * 0.05;
        colB[i] = 1.0;
        sizes[i] = 1.8 + s * 2.4;
        alphas[i] = 0.0;
      }

      const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
      const vbo = gl.createBuffer();
      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
      gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, verts.byteLength, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
      const stride = 32;
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0); gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 0);
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1); gl.vertexAttribPointer(1, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 12);
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(2); gl.vertexAttribPointer(2, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 24);
      gl.enableVertexAttribArray(3); gl.vertexAttribPointer(3, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 28);
      gl.bindVertexArray(null);

      gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
      // High-radiance additive blending for HDR luminescence
      gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);

      let state = "listening";
      let activeShape = 0; // 0=sphere, 1=helix, 2=torus, 3=shield
      let morphWeight = 0.0;
      let targetShape = 0;

      let bass = 0, mid = 0, treble = 0;
      let sBass = 0, sMid = 0, sTreble = 0;
      let t = 0;
      let lastNow = performance.now();
      let assembleProgress = 0.0;
      let targetAssemble = 1.0;

      let curMouseX = -999.0, curMouseY = -999.0;
      let targetMouseX = -999.0, targetMouseY = -999.0;
      let mouseActivity = 0.0;

      // Color themes per state
      const PALETTES = {
        listening: { r: 0.88, g: 0.96, b: 1.00 }, // Diamond celestial white/cyan
        speaking:  { r: 0.35, g: 0.85, b: 1.00 }, // Electric cyan-gold
        processing:{ r: 0.78, g: 0.45, b: 1.00 }, // Starlight violet plasma
        alert:     { r: 1.00, g: 0.35, b: 0.20 }, // Solar flare amber
        dormant:   { r: 0.40, g: 0.50, b: 0.65 }, // Dim moonlight
      };

      window.DarkShadowFX = {
        setState(nextState) {
          state = nextState || "listening";
          if (state === "dormant" || state === "dissolving") {
            targetAssemble = 0.0;
          } else {
            targetAssemble = 1.0;
          }
          if (state === "processing") targetShape = 1; // Helix for thinking
          else if (state === "alert") targetShape = 3;  // Shield cube for alert
          else if (state === "speaking") targetShape = 2; // Torus for speaking
          else targetShape = 0; // Sphere for listening
        },
        setShape(shapeIdx) {
          targetShape = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, shapeIdx | 0));
        },
        setAudio(b, m, tr) {
          bass = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(b) || 0));
          mid = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(m) || 0));
          treble = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(tr) || 0));
        },
        setMouse(rx, ry) {
          const mx = rx * 2.0 - 1.0;
          const my = -(ry * 2.0 - 1.0);
          if (targetMouseX > -900) {
            const dx = mx - targetMouseX;
            const dy = my - targetMouseY;
            mouseActivity = Math.min(1.0, mouseActivity + Math.hypot(dx, dy) * 8.0);
          } else {
            curMouseX = mx;
            curMouseY = my;
          }
          targetMouseX = mx;
          targetMouseY = my;
        },
      };

      window.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) => {
        window.DarkShadowFX.setMouse(e.clientX / window.innerWidth, e.clientY / window.innerHeight);
      });

      function tick(now) {
        const dt = Math.min(0.035, (now - lastNow) / 1000);
        lastNow = now;
        t += dt;

        sBass += (bass - sBass) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        sMid += (mid - sMid) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        sTreble += (treble - sTreble) * Math.min(1, dt * 16);

        assembleProgress += (targetAssemble - assembleProgress) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 2.4);
        mouseActivity = Math.max(0.0, mouseActivity - dt * 3.5);

        if (targetMouseX > -900) {
          curMouseX += (targetMouseX - curMouseX) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
          curMouseY += (targetMouseY - curMouseY) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
        }

        const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
        const w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
        if (canvas.width !== Math.round(w * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(h * dpr)) {
          canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr);
          canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
        }
        const aspect = Math.max(0.1, w / Math.max(1, h));

        // PURE SOLAR 3D ROTATION: Sthir Center rotating 360-degrees on its axis (ZERO tilt, ZERO flattening)
        const rotY = t * (0.32 + sMid * 0.4);
        const cy = Math.cos(rotY), sy = Math.sin(rotY);

        const voiceBoost = 1.0 + sBass * 0.16 + sMid * 0.08;
        const fountainStrength = (1.0 - assembleProgress) * 4.2;
        const springK = 3.5 + assembleProgress * 15.0;
        const damping = 4.2 + assembleProgress * 2.4;

        const pal = PALETTES[state] || PALETTES.listening;

        for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
          // Dynamic target shape selection
          let tx0 = s0X[i], ty0 = s0Y[i], tz0 = s0Z[i];
          if (targetShape === 1) { tx0 = s1X[i]; ty0 = s1Y[i]; tz0 = s1Z[i]; }
          else if (targetShape === 2) { tx0 = s2X[i]; ty0 = s2Y[i]; tz0 = s2Z[i]; }
          else if (targetShape === 3) { tx0 = s3X[i]; ty0 = s3Y[i]; tz0 = s3Z[i]; }

          // Pure 3D axial rotation around Y (like the Sun, ZERO vertical tilt):
          const x1 = tx0 * cy - tz0 * sy;
          const y2 = ty0; // Perfectly anchored vertical axis
          const z2 = tx0 * sy + tz0 * cy;

          const wave = 1.0 + (0.015 + sMid * 0.05) * Math.sin(pPhase[i] + t * 4.0);
          const scale = voiceBoost * wave;

          const targetX = x1 * scale;
          const targetY = y2 * scale;
          const targetZ = z2 * scale;

          if (fountainStrength > 0.01) {
            velY[i] += fountainStrength * dt * 9.0;
          }

          velX[i] += ((targetX - posX[i]) * springK - velX[i] * damping) * dt;
          velY[i] += ((targetY - posY[i]) * springK - velY[i] * damping) * dt;
          velZ[i] += ((targetZ - posZ[i]) * springK - velZ[i] * damping) * dt;

          posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
          posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
          posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;

          alphas[i] = Math.min(0.95, alphas[i] + dt * 2.8);

          // Smooth color interpolation towards state palette
          colR[i] += (pal.r - colR[i]) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 4.0);
          colG[i] += (pal.g - colG[i]) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 4.0);
          colB[i] += (pal.b - colB[i]) * Math.min(1.0, dt * 4.0);

          const o = i * 8;
          verts[o] = posX[i];
          verts[o + 1] = posY[i];
          verts[o + 2] = posZ[i];
          verts[o + 3] = colR[i];
          verts[o + 4] = colG[i];
          verts[o + 5] = colB[i];
          verts[o + 6] = sizes[i] * (1.0 + sBass * 0.40 + sTreble * 0.28);
          verts[o + 7] = alphas[i];
        }

        gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
        gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);

        gl.useProgram(prog);
        gl.uniform1f(uAspect, aspect);
        gl.uniform1f(uDpr, dpr);
        gl.uniform1f(uTime, t);
        gl.uniform3f(uAudio, sBass, sMid, sTreble);
        gl.uniform2f(uMouse, curMouseX, curMouseY);
        gl.uniform1f(uMouseHover, Math.min(1.0, mouseActivity));

        gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
        gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
        gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, 0, verts);
        gl.drawArrays(gl.POINTS, 0, COUNT);
        gl.bindVertexArray(null);

        requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }

      requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
