"""
Upgrade #4: Proactive Desktop Sentinel Monitor.
Silently monitors due reminders and Windows battery/system health in the background.
Summons particles with a Shield alert only when Captain needs to know something.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from typing import Callable

from memory_engine import SemanticMemoryEngine

try:
    import psutil
    HAS_PSUTIL = True
except Exception:
    HAS_PSUTIL = False


class DesktopSentinel:
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory: SemanticMemoryEngine,
        on_proactive_alert: Callable[[str], None],
        poll_seconds: float = 15.0,
    ) -> None:
        self._memory = memory
        self._on_alert = on_proactive_alert
        self._poll = poll_seconds
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._battery_warned = False

    def start(self) -> None:
        threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True, name="darkshadow-sentinel").start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()

    def _run(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.wait(self._poll):
            try:
                due_list = self._memory.pop_due_reminders()
                for rem in due_list:
                    self._on_alert(f"Captain, Reminder: {rem.message}")
            except Exception:
                pass

            if HAS_PSUTIL:
                try:
                    batt = psutil.sensors_battery()
                    if batt is not None:
                        if not batt.power_plugged and batt.percent <= 15 and not self._battery_warned:
                            self._battery_warned = True
                            self._on_alert(
                                f"Captain, laptop battery sirf {batt.percent:.0f}% bachi hai — charger connect kar lijiye."
                            )
                        elif batt.power_plugged or batt.percent >= 25:
                            self._battery_warned = False
                except Exception:
                    pass



======================================================================
FILE: desktop_python\voice_engine.py
======================================================================
"""
Voice Engine for DarkShadow Desktop AI.
Features:
- Continuous sounddevice InputStream (never repeatedly opens/closes, eliminating Windows mic tray blinking & audio cutoffs).
- Rolling audio pre-roll buffer (keeps ~320ms prior to voice detection so first consonants aren't clipped).
- Real-time 3-band FFT reactivity (bass, mid, treble) for 6,000-particle WebGL2 sphere.
- Turn-taking conversational loop (mic pauses while AI thinks/speaks, un-mutes immediately upon completion).
- Once awakened (active=True), continuously accepts voice queries without requiring repeated wake phrases.
- Edge-TTS (hi-IN-MadhurNeural / en-IN-PrabhatNeural) with offline Windows SAPI (pyttsx3) fallback.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import math
import os
import queue
import re
import tempfile
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable

import numpy as np

try:
    import sounddevice as sd
    HAS_SD = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SD = False

try:
    import speech_recognition as sr
    HAS_SR = True
except Exception:
    HAS_SR = False

try:
    import edge_tts
    import pygame
    HAS_TTS = True
except Exception:
    HAS_TTS = False

try:
    from openwakeword.model import Model as OWWModel
    HAS_OWW = True
except Exception:
    HAS_OWW = False

WAKE_REGEX = re.compile(
    r"(?:(?:\b(?:hey|hi|hello|ok|suno|oye|listen)\s+)|(?:(?:हे|अरे|सुनो|ओए)\s+))?(?:(?:dark\s*shadow|darkshadow|dark\s*show|dock\s*shadow|dog\s*shadow|dark)\b|डार्क\s*शैडो|डार्कशैडो|डार्क)",
    re.IGNORECASE | re.UNICODE,
)
SLEEP_REGEX = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:sleep|so\s*jao|bye|goodbye|band\s*ho\s*jao|hide|chup\s*raho)\b",
    re.IGNORECASE | re.UNICODE,
)


def compute_fft_bands(pcm_int16: np.ndarray, sample_rate: int = 16000) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """Split a mono int16 audio frame into normalized (bass, mid, treble) bands [0..1]."""
    if pcm_int16.size < 64:
        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
    samples = pcm_int16.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
    windowed = samples * np.hanning(samples.size)
    spectrum = np.abs(np.fft.rfft(windowed))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(samples.size, d=1.0 / sample_rate)

    bass = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 60) & (freqs < 280)])) if np.any((freqs >= 60) & (freqs < 280)) else 0.0
    mid = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 280) & (freqs < 2200)])) if np.any((freqs >= 280) & (freqs < 2200)) else 0.0
    treble = float(np.mean(spectrum[(freqs >= 2200) & (freqs <= 7500)])) if np.any((freqs >= 2200) & (freqs <= 7500)) else 0.0

    return (
        min(1.0, bass * 0.26),
        min(1.0, mid * 0.36),
        min(1.0, treble * 0.58),
    )


class VoiceEngine:
    """Continuous stream audio engine:
    - Keeps single sd.InputStream open permanently.
    - Windows mic icon stays ON continuously; zero dropped syllables.
    - Real-time 3-band FFT reactivity streaming to WebGL2 sphere.
    - Energy-based Voice Activity Detection (VAD) with rolling pre-roll buffer.
    - True conversational turn-taking:
        * User speaks -> mic listens & sphere pulses to user voice.
        * User stops -> VAD triggers utterance completion -> mic mutes (ignores audio).
        * AI thinks & speaks -> sphere pulses to AI speech.
        * AI finishes speaking -> mic immediately un-mutes and continues listening for Captain's next words without needing repeat wake-words!
    - "sleep" / "so jao" / "bye" puts it in standby (waiting for "Hey Dark Shadow").
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        on_audio_fft: Callable[[float, float, float], None],
        on_wake: Callable[[str], None],
        on_command: Callable[[str], None],
        on_sleep: Callable[[], None],
    ) -> None:
        self._on_fft = on_audio_fft
        self._on_wake = on_wake
        self._on_command = on_command
        self._on_sleep = on_sleep

        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._interrupt_tts = threading.Event()
        self.active = False
        self._is_speaking = False
        self.busy = False

        self._recognizer = sr.Recognizer() if HAS_SR else None
        if self._recognizer:
            self._recognizer.energy_threshold = 280
            self._recognizer.dynamic_energy_threshold = True

        self._oww = None
        if HAS_OWW:
            try:
                self._oww = OWWModel()
            except Exception:
                self._oww = None

        if HAS_TTS:
            try:
                pygame.mixer.init(frequency=24000)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def start(self) -> None:
        threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True, name="darkshadow-voice-continuous").start()

    def stop(self) -> None:
        self._stop.set()
        self.interrupt_speech()

    def interrupt_speech(self) -> None:
        self._interrupt_tts.set()
        self._is_speaking = False
        if HAS_TTS:
            try:
                pygame.mixer.music.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def speak(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Speak text aloud while streaming real-time audio pulse bands to the WebGL2 particles."""
        self._interrupt_tts.clear()
        self._is_speaking = True
        if not HAS_TTS:
            t0 = time.time()
            try:
                while time.time() - t0 < 2.0 and not self._interrupt_tts.is_set() and not self._stop.is_set():
                    e = time.time() - t0
                    self._on_fft(
                        0.35 + 0.45 * abs(math.sin(e * 6.0)),
                        0.30 + 0.50 * abs(math.cos(e * 9.0)),
                        0.25 + 0.40 * abs(math.sin(e * 15.0)),
                    )
                    time.sleep(0.025)
            finally:
                self._is_speaking = False
                self._on_fft(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
            return

        voice = "hi-IN-MadhurNeural" if any(ord(c) > 127 for c in text) else "en-IN-PrabhatNeural"
        tmp = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"ds_tts_{int(time.time() * 1000)}.mp3"
        try:
            async def _gen():
                await edge_tts.Communicate(text, voice=voice, pitch="-4Hz").save(str(tmp))

            asyncio.run(_gen())
            if self._interrupt_tts.is_set() or self._stop.is_set():
                return
            pygame.mixer.music.load(str(tmp))
            pygame.mixer.music.play()
            t0 = time.time()
            while pygame.mixer.music.get_busy() and not self._interrupt_tts.is_set() and not self._stop.is_set():
                e = time.time() - t0
                b = 0.25 + 0.65 * abs(math.sin(e * 6.2) * math.cos(e * 2.4))
                m = 0.30 + 0.60 * abs(math.sin(e * 10.5))
                tr = 0.20 + 0.55 * abs(math.sin(e * 17.0) * math.cos(e * 7.3))
                self._on_fft(b, m, tr)
                time.sleep(0.025)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[TTS Fallback to Windows SAPI] {exc}")
            try:
                import pyttsx3
                engine = pyttsx3.init()
                engine.say(text)
                engine.runAndWait()
            except Exception as e2:
                print(f"[TTS Fallback Error] {e2}")
        finally:
            self._is_speaking = False
            self._on_fft(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
            try:
                pygame.mixer.music.unload()
                tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _transcribe(self, pcm: bytes) -> str:
        if not pcm or self._recognizer is None:
            return ""
        audio = sr.AudioData(pcm, 16000, 2)
        for lang in ("hi-IN", "en-IN"):
            try:
                text = self._recognizer.recognize_google(audio, language=lang)
                if text and text.strip():
                    return text.strip()
            except sr.UnknownValueError:
                continue
            except Exception:
                continue
        return ""

    def _process_utterance(self, pcm: bytes) -> None:
        """Transcribes PCM in a background worker thread and triggers wake or command callback."""
        try:
            text = self._transcribe(pcm)
            if not text:
                self.busy = False
                return

            print(f"[VoiceEngine] Heard: '{text}' (state={'active' if self.active else 'dormant'})")

            if WAKE_REGEX.search(text):
                rest = WAKE_REGEX.sub("", text).strip(" ,.-?!")
                print(f"[VoiceEngine] Wake phrase matched! Rest='{rest}'")
                self._on_wake(rest)
            elif self.active:
                if SLEEP_REGEX.search(text):
                    print("[VoiceEngine] Sleep command detected. Entering standby.")
                    self._on_sleep()
                    self.busy = False
                else:
                    print(f"[VoiceEngine] Executing command: '{text}'")
                    self._on_command(text)
            else:
                self.busy = False
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[VoiceEngine Utterance Error]: {exc}")
            self.busy = False

    def _loop(self) -> None:
        """Main audio worker thread: keeps ONE continuous InputStream open to prevent Windows mic flickering."""
        if not HAS_SD or not HAS_SR:
            print("[VoiceEngine] Error: sounddevice or speech_recognition not available.")
            return

        sr_hz = 16000
        block = 1280  # 80ms frame
        q: queue.Queue[np.ndarray] = queue.Queue(maxsize=300)

        def cb(indata, _frames, _t, _status):
            if not self._stop.is_set():
                try:
                    q.put_nowait(indata[:, 0].copy())
                except queue.Full:
                    pass

        threshold = float(os.getenv("DARKSHADOW_VOICE_THRESHOLD", "0.0009"))
        silence_limit = int((1.1 * sr_hz) / block)  # ~1.1s of silence to finalize sentence
        max_blocks = int((10.0 * sr_hz) / block)    # ~10s max recording
        pre_roll_limit = 4                          # keep ~320ms before speech starts

        print("[VoiceEngine] Continuous Microphone stream OPEN & LISTENING.")
        print("[VoiceEngine] >> Say 'Hey Dark Shadow' or 'Dark Shadow' to wake up! <<")

        chunks: list[np.ndarray] = []
        pre_roll: list[np.ndarray] = []
        heard = False
        silent_count = 0

        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try:
                with sd.InputStream(samplerate=sr_hz, channels=1, dtype="int16", blocksize=block, callback=cb):
                    while not self._stop.is_set():
                        # If speaking or busy processing LLM/TTS, drop incoming frames to prevent feedback
                        if self._is_speaking or self.busy:
                            chunks.clear()
                            pre_roll.clear()
                            heard = False
                            silent_count = 0
                            while not q.empty():
                                try:
                                    q.get_nowait()
                                except queue.Empty:
                                    break
                            time.sleep(0.04)
                            continue

                        try:
                            frame = q.get(timeout=0.1)
                        except queue.Empty:
                            continue

                        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((frame.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0) ** 2)))

                        # Real-time audio FFT reactivity when Captain speaks
                        if self.active and rms > threshold:
                            b, m, tr = compute_fft_bands(frame, sr_hz)
                            self._on_fft(b, m, tr)

                        if rms > threshold:
                            if not heard:
                                heard = True
                                chunks = list(pre_roll)  # include pre-roll frames so first consonant is preserved!
                            chunks.append(frame)
                            silent_count = 0
                        elif heard:
                            chunks.append(frame)
                            silent_count += 1
                        else:
                            # Rolling pre-roll buffer
                            pre_roll.append(frame)
                            if len(pre_roll) > pre_roll_limit:
                                pre_roll.pop(0)

                        # Utterance completed either by silence limit or max duration
                        if heard and (silent_count >= silence_limit or len(chunks) >= max_blocks):
                            self._on_fft(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
                            pcm = np.concatenate(chunks).tobytes()
                            chunks.clear()
                            pre_roll.clear()
                            heard = False
                            silent_count = 0

                            # Immediately pause mic capture during transcription & AI processing
                            self.busy = True
                            threading.Thread(target=self._process_utterance, args=(pcm,), daemon=True).start()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[VoiceEngine Stream Error]: {e}")
                time.sleep(1.0)

# Unbypassable Safety Shields
PAYMENT_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:buy\s*now|place\s*order|pay\s*now|checkout|enter\s*cvv|enter\s*otp|"
    r"upi\s*pin|credit\s*card|debit\s*card|payment\s*karo|pay\s*karo)\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)
CAMERA_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(?:webcam|camera|selfie|face\s*record|record\s*face|कैमरा|वेबकैम)\b",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

def check_hard_shields(text: str) -> str | None:
    if PAYMENT_RE.search(text):
        return (
            "Security Shield: Automated payment, checkout aur financial credentials "
            "strictly blocked hain. Final payment kripya manually complete kijiye."
        )
    if CAMERA_RE.search(text):
        return (
            "Privacy Shield: Physical camera/webcam access permanently disabled hai. "
            "Sirf monitor screen vision active hai."
        )
    return None


def capture_monitor_jpeg_bytes() -> bytes | None:
    """Capture active primary monitor screenshot (ZERO camera/webcam access)."""
    if not HAS_SCREEN:
        return None
    with mss.mss() as sct:
        monitor = sct.monitors[1]
        raw = sct.grab(monitor)
        img = Image.frombytes("RGB", raw.size, raw.bgra, "raw", "BGRX")
        img.thumbnail((1280, 720))
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buf, format="JPEG", quality=78)
        return buf.getvalue()


def resolve_contact(query: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Resolve contact name or alias from contacts.json."""
    candidate_paths = [
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "contacts.json",
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "contacts.json",
        Path.cwd() / "contacts.json",
    ]
    contacts_file = next((p for p in candidate_paths if p.exists()), None)
    if not contacts_file:
        return None
    try:
        with open(contacts_file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        q = query.strip().lower()
        for c in data:
            if c.get("name", "").lower() == q or q in [str(a).lower() for a in c.get("aliases", [])]:
                return c
        for c in data:
            if q in c.get("name", "").lower():
                return c
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def close_application(app_name: str, on_close_callback: Callable[[], None] | None = None) -> str:
    """Close one allowlisted app or active window cleanly on Windows."""
    clean = str(app_name).strip().lower()
    if not clean:
        return "Application name specify kijiye."

    # 1. DarkShadow itself
    if clean in ("darkshadow", "dark shadow", "dark_shadow", "dark-shadow", "overlay", "this assistant", "this app", "khud ko"):
        if on_close_callback:
            threading.Timer(1.0, on_close_callback).start()
            return "DarkShadow band kar raha hoon, Captain. Alvida!"
        return "DarkShadow close request received."

    # 2. Active Window
    if clean in ("active", "current", "this window", "active window", "ye app", "ye window", "window"):
        if HAS_USER32:
            hwnd = _user32.GetForegroundWindow()
            if hwnd:
                _user32.PostMessageW(hwnd, 0x0010, 0, 0)  # WM_CLOSE
                return "Active window band kar diya gaya."
        return "Koi active window close nahi ho saki."

    # 3. Mapped applications
    target_procs = APPLICATION_PROCESS_NAMES.get(clean)
    if not target_procs:
        for k, v in APPLICATION_PROCESS_NAMES.items():
            if k in clean or clean in k:
                target_procs = v
                break

    closed_any = False
    if target_procs:
        for proc_name in target_procs:
            try:
                res = subprocess.run(
                    ["taskkill", "/IM", proc_name, "/F", "/T"],
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    check=False,
                    creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
                )
                if res.returncode == 0:
                    closed_any = True
            except Exception:
                pass
        if closed_any:
            return f"'{app_name}' band kar diya gaya hai, Captain."

    # 4. Fallback search with psutil
    if HAS_PSUTIL:
        for proc in psutil.process_iter(["name", "pid"]):
            try:
                pname = proc.info["name"].lower()
                if clean in pname or pname.startswith(clean):
                    proc.kill()
                    closed_any = True
            except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                pass
        if closed_any:
            return f"'{app_name}' process terminate kar diya gaya."

    return f"Application '{app_name}' running nahi mili ya close nahi ho saki."


def set_system_volume(
    level: int | None = None,
    direction: str = "",
    amount: int = 10,
    mute: bool | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Set Windows master volume level, change volume, or mute/unmute audio."""
    clean_dir = str(direction).strip().lower()

    # 1. Pycaw High-Precision Endpoint Control
    if HAS_PYCAW:
        try:
            speakers = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers()
            endpoint = speakers.EndpointVolume
            if mute is not None or clean_dir in ("mute", "unmute"):
                is_mute = True if (mute is True or clean_dir == "mute") else False
                endpoint.SetMute(1 if is_mute else 0, None)
                return "Windows audio mute kar diya." if is_mute else "Windows audio unmute kar diya."

            if level is not None:
                lvl = max(0, min(100, int(level)))
                endpoint.SetMasterVolumeLevelScalar(lvl / 100.0, None)
                return f"Master volume {lvl}% set kar diya."

            if clean_dir in ("up", "down"):
                current = round(float(endpoint.GetMasterVolumeLevelScalar()) * 100)
                step = max(1, min(50, int(amount)))
                target = max(0, min(100, current + (step if clean_dir == "up" else -step)))
                endpoint.SetMasterVolumeLevelScalar(target / 100.0, None)
                return f"Volume {target}% ho gaya."
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 2. Win32 Virtual Key Fallback
    if clean_dir == "up":
        steps = max(1, min(25, amount // 2))
        for _ in range(steps):
            if HAS_USER32:
                _user32.keybd_event(0xAF, 0, 0, 0)
                _user32.keybd_event(0xAF, 0, 2, 0)
        return "Volume badha diya gaya."
    elif clean_dir == "down":
        steps = max(1, min(25, amount // 2))
        for _ in range(steps):
            if HAS_USER32:
                _user32.keybd_event(0xAE, 0, 0, 0)
                _user32.keybd_event(0xAE, 0, 2, 0)
        return "Volume kam kar diya gaya."
    elif clean_dir in ("mute", "unmute") or mute is not None:
        if HAS_USER32:
            _user32.keybd_event(0xAD, 0, 0, 0)
            _user32.keybd_event(0xAD, 0, 2, 0)
        return "Audio mute toggle kar diya."

    return "Volume command execute hui."


def send_whatsapp_message(recipient: str, message: str) -> str:
    """Send or draft WhatsApp message by contact name or phone number."""
    rec = str(recipient).strip()
    msg = str(message).strip()
    if not rec:
        return "Recipient specify kijiye."
    if not msg:
        return "Message specify kijiye."

    shield_err = check_hard_shields(msg)
    if shield_err:
        return shield_err

    phone = ""
    contact = resolve_contact(rec)
    if contact and contact.get("phone"):
        phone = contact["phone"]
    else:
        cleaned = re.sub(r"[^\d+]", "", rec)
        if len(cleaned) >= 7:
            phone = cleaned

    if phone:
        digits = re.sub(r"[^\d]", "", phone)
        if len(digits) == 10 and not phone.startswith("+"):
            digits = "91" + digits
        encoded_msg = urllib.parse.quote(msg)

        try:
            subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", f"whatsapp://send?phone={digits}&text={encoded_msg}"], shell=False)
            return f"WhatsApp message draft/sent for {rec} ({digits}): '{msg}'"
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            webbrowser.open(f"https://web.whatsapp.com/send?phone={digits}&text={encoded_msg}")
            return f"Opened WhatsApp Web with message for {rec} ({digits})."
        except Exception as exc:
            return f"WhatsApp open failed: {exc}"
    else:
        try:
            subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "whatsapp:"], shell=False)
            return f"WhatsApp open kar diya. Contact '{rec}' search karke message bhejiye: '{msg}'"
        except Exception:
            webbrowser.open("https://web.whatsapp.com")
            return f"WhatsApp Web open kar diya. Contact '{rec}' select kijiye."


def control_media(action: str) -> str:
    """Control media playback (play_pause, play, pause, next, prev, stop, mute, volume_up, volume_down)."""
    act = str(action).strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    key_codes = {
        "play_pause": 0xB3,
        "play": 0xB3,
        "pause": 0xB3,
        "next": 0xB0,
        "next_track": 0xB0,
        "prev": 0xB1,
        "previous": 0xB1,
        "previous_track": 0xB1,
        "stop": 0xB2,
        "volume_mute": 0xAD,
        "mute": 0xAD,
        "volume_up": 0xAF,
        "volume_down": 0xAE,
    }
    vk = key_codes.get(act)
    if vk and HAS_USER32:
        _user32.keybd_event(vk, 0, 0, 0)
        time.sleep(0.03)
        _user32.keybd_event(vk, 0, 2, 0)
        return f"Media control '{act}' executed."
    if HAS_PYAUTOGUI:
        py_keys = {
            "play_pause": "playpause",
            "play": "playpause",
            "pause": "playpause",
            "next": "nexttrack",
            "prev": "prevtrack",
            "stop": "stop",
        }
        pk = py_keys.get(act)
        if pk:
            pyautogui.press(pk)
            return f"Media control '{act}' executed via PyAutoGUI."
    return f"Media control '{act}' unsupported or unavailable."


def search_local_files(query: str, folder: str = "") -> str:
    """Search files in user's Downloads, Documents, Desktop, or custom folder."""
    q = str(query).strip().lower()
    if not q:
        return "Query cannot be empty."

    roots = []
    if folder and os.path.exists(folder):
        roots.append(Path(folder))
    else:
        roots.extend([
            Path.home() / "Downloads",
            Path.home() / "Documents",
            Path.home() / "Desktop",
        ])

    found = []
    for root in roots:
        if not root.exists():
            continue
        try:
            for item in root.rglob("*"):
                if q in item.name.lower():
                    found.append(str(item))
                    if len(found) >= 8:
                        break
        except Exception:
            continue
        if len(found) >= 8:
            break

    if found:
        return "Found files:\n" + "\n".join(f"- {p}" for p in found)
    return f"No files matching '{query}' found."


def manage_clipboard(action: str = "get", text: str = "") -> str:
    """Read or set Windows clipboard text."""
    act = str(action).strip().lower()
    if act == "set" or (text and act != "get"):
        if HAS_CLIPBOARD:
            pyperclip.copy(text)
            snippet = text[:50] + "..." if len(text) > 50 else text
            return f"Copied to clipboard: '{snippet}'"
        return "pyperclip not installed."
    else:
        if HAS_CLIPBOARD:
            clip = pyperclip.paste()
            return f"Clipboard content: '{clip[:300]}'" if clip else "Clipboard is currently empty."
        return "pyperclip not installed."


def control_active_window(action: str) -> str:
    """Control active or desktop windows (minimize, maximize, restore, close, show_desktop, lock_pc)."""
    act = str(action).strip().lower()
    if act == "show_desktop":
        if HAS_USER32:
            _user32.keybd_event(0x5B, 0, 0, 0)
            _user32.keybd_event(ord("D"), 0, 0, 0)
            time.sleep(0.03)
            _user32.keybd_event(ord("D"), 0, 2, 0)
            _user32.keybd_event(0x5B, 0, 2, 0)
            return "Toggled Show Desktop."
        elif HAS_PYAUTOGUI:
            pyautogui.hotkey("win", "d")
            return "Toggled Show Desktop."

    if act == "lock_pc":
        if HAS_USER32:
            _user32.LockWorkStation()
            return "Locked Windows workstation."
        subprocess.Popen(["rundll32.exe", "user32.dll,LockWorkStation"], shell=False)
        return "Locked Windows workstation."

    if HAS_USER32:
        hwnd = _user32.GetForegroundWindow()
        if hwnd:
            if act == "minimize":
                _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 6)  # SW_MINIMIZE
                return "Minimized active window."
            if act == "maximize":
                _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 3)  # SW_MAXIMIZE
                return "Maximized active window."
            if act == "restore":
                _user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 9)  # SW_RESTORE
                return "Restored active window."
            if act == "close":
                _user32.PostMessageW(hwnd, 0x0010, 0, 0)  # WM_CLOSE
                return "Closed active window."

    return f"Window action '{act}' executed."

def get_gemini_live_tools() -> list[Any]:
    """Return Tool declarations formatted for Gemini Live WebSocket API."""
    if not HAS_GENAI:
        return []
    return [
        types.Tool(
            function_declarations=[
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="open_app",
                    description="Launch desktop applications or Windows settings like whatsapp, bluetooth, spotify, settings, chrome, notepad, calculator, wifi.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "app_name": types.Schema(
                                type=types.Type.STRING,
                                description="The name of the app or setting, e.g. whatsapp, bluetooth, spotify, settings, chrome, notepad, wifi",
                            )
                        },
                        required=["app_name"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="close_app",
                    description="Close one running desktop application (chrome, edge, notepad, spotify, whatsapp, vscode, calculator, terminal, darkshadow) or close the active window.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "app_name": types.Schema(
                                type=types.Type.STRING,
                                description="Application name or 'active' or 'darkshadow'",
                            )
                        },
                        required=["app_name"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="control_window",
                    description="Control active window: minimize, maximize, restore, close, show_desktop, lock_pc.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "action": types.Schema(
                                type=types.Type.STRING,
                                description="minimize | maximize | restore | close | show_desktop | lock_pc",
                            )
                        },
                        required=["action"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="set_volume",
                    description="Adjust master volume (0-100), change volume up/down, or mute/unmute audio.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "level": types.Schema(type=types.Type.INTEGER, description="Volume level 0-100"),
                            "direction": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="up | down | mute | unmute"),
                            "amount": types.Schema(type=types.Type.INTEGER, description="Step amount (default 10)"),
                        },
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="send_whatsapp",
                    description="Send or draft a WhatsApp message to a contact name or phone number.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "recipient": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Contact name or phone number"),
                            "message": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Message text"),
                        },
                        required=["recipient", "message"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="media_control",
                    description="Control media playback: play_pause, play, pause, next, prev, stop, mute, volume_up, volume_down.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "action": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="play_pause | next | prev | stop | mute | volume_up | volume_down"),
                        },
                        required=["action"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="play_music",
                    description="Play music, songs, artists or playlists on Spotify or YouTube, or launch Spotify.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "query": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Song title, artist name, or 'spotify'"),
                            "platform": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="spotify | youtube"),
                        },
                        required=["query"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="search_web",
                    description="Search the web via Google for real-time information, answers, news, weather, or facts.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "query": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Search terms"),
                        },
                        required=["query"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="search_files",
                    description="Search local files in Downloads, Documents, Desktop by filename or keyword.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "query": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Filename or keyword"),
                            "folder": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Optional search folder"),
                        },
                        required=["query"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="manage_clipboard",
                    description="Get or set Windows clipboard text.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "action": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="get | set"),
                            "text": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Text to copy"),
                        },
                        required=["action"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="get_current_time",
                    description="Get current real-time system clock, date, and day.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(type=types.Type.OBJECT, properties={}),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="get_system_status",
                    description="Check Windows CPU, RAM, and Battery status.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(type=types.Type.OBJECT, properties={}),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="open_website",
                    description="Open a website URL or search on Google or YouTube.",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "kind": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="url | google | youtube"),
                            "query_or_url": types.Schema(type=types.Type.STRING, description="Search query or URL"),
                        },
                        required=["kind", "query_or_url"],
                    ),
                ),
                types.FunctionDeclaration(
                    name="set_brightness",
                    description="Set screen brightness percentage (0 to 100).",
                    parameters=types.Schema(
                        type=types.Type.OBJECT,
                        properties={
                            "level": types.Schema(type=types.Type.INTEGER, description="Brightness 0-100"),
                        },
                        required=["level"],
                    ),
                ),
            ]
        )
    ]


class WindowsToolExecutor:
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory: SemanticMemoryEngine,
        genai_client: Any = None,
        on_close_app: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.memory = memory
        self._client = genai_client
        self._on_close_app = on_close_app

    def execute(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        if name == "get_current_time":
            now = datetime.now()
            return f"Current time is {now.strftime('%I:%M %p')}, {now.strftime('%A, %d %B %Y')}."

        if name == "close_app":
            app = str(args.get("app_name", "")).strip()
            return close_application(app, self._on_close_app)

        if name == "control_window":
            act = str(args.get("action", "minimize")).strip()
            return control_active_window(act)

        if name == "set_volume":
            lvl = args.get("level")
            dir_str = str(args.get("direction", "")).strip()
            amt = int(args.get("amount", 10))
            return set_system_volume(level=lvl, direction=dir_str, amount=amt)

        if name == "send_whatsapp":
            rec = str(args.get("recipient", "")).strip()
            msg = str(args.get("message", "")).strip()
            return send_whatsapp_message(rec, msg)

        if name == "media_control":
            act = str(args.get("action", "play_pause")).strip()
            return control_media(act)

        if name == "search_files":
            q = str(args.get("query", "")).strip()
            folder = str(args.get("folder", "")).strip()
            return search_local_files(q, folder)

        if name == "manage_clipboard":
            act = str(args.get("action", "get")).strip()
            txt = str(args.get("text", "")).strip()
            return manage_clipboard(act, txt)

        if name == "play_music":
            q = str(args.get("query", "spotify")).strip()
            plat = str(args.get("platform", "spotify")).strip().lower()
            if plat == "youtube":
                url = f"https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
                return f"Opening '{q}' on YouTube in Microsoft Edge."
            # Default to Spotify Web in Microsoft Edge
            if q.lower() in ("spotify", "music", "gaana", "song"):
                url = "https://open.spotify.com"
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
                return "Spotify Web page Microsoft Edge mein open kar diya hai."
            else:
                url = f"https://open.spotify.com/search/{urllib.parse.quote(q)}"
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
                return f"Microsoft Edge mein Spotify Web par '{q}' search page open kar diya hai. Play button screen par ready hai."

        if name == "search_web":
            query = str(args.get("query", "")).strip()
            url = f"https://www.google.com/search?q={urllib.parse.quote_plus(query)}"
            subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
            return f"Opened search results for '{query}' on Google in Microsoft Edge."

        if name == "open_app":
            app = str(args.get("app_name", "")).strip().lower()
            if "spotify" in app or "music" in app or "gaana" in app:
                url = "https://open.spotify.com"
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
                return "Spotify Web page Microsoft Edge mein open kar diya hai."
            if "whatsapp" in app:
                try:
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "whatsapp:"], shell=False)
                    return "Opened WhatsApp Desktop."
                except Exception:
                    url = "https://web.whatsapp.com"
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
                    return "Opened WhatsApp Web in Microsoft Edge."
            if "bluetooth" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:bluetooth"], shell=False)
                return "Opened Windows Bluetooth Settings."
            if "setting" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:"], shell=False)
                return "Opened Windows Settings."
            if "wifi" in app or "network" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "ms-settings:network-wifi"], shell=False)
                return "Opened Windows WiFi Settings."
            if "youtube" in app:
                subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", "https://www.youtube.com"], shell=False)
                return "Opened YouTube in Microsoft Edge."

            cmd = SAFE_WINDOWS_APPS.get(app)
            if not cmd:
                try:
                    subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", app], shell=False)
                    return f"Launched {app}."
                except Exception:
                    return f"App '{app}' could not be opened."
            try:
                subprocess.Popen(cmd, shell=False)
                return f"Successfully launched {app}."
            except Exception as exc:
                return f"Could not launch {app}: {type(exc).__name__}"

        if name == "open_website":
            kind = str(args.get("kind", "url")).lower()
            q = str(args.get("query_or_url", "")).strip()
            if kind == "google":
                url = f"https://www.google.com/search?q={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
            elif kind == "youtube":
                url = f"https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query={urllib.parse.quote_plus(q)}"
            elif kind == "spotify":
                url = f"https://open.spotify.com/search/{urllib.parse.quote(q)}"
            else:
                url = q if q.startswith(("http://", "https://")) else f"https://{q}"
            subprocess.Popen(["cmd", "/c", "start", "", "msedge", url], shell=False)
            return f"Microsoft Edge mein {url} open kar diya hai."

        if name == "set_brightness":
            level = max(0, min(100, int(args.get("level", 50))))
            if not HAS_BRIGHTNESS:
                return "screen-brightness-control package installed nahi hai."
            try:
                sbc.set_brightness(level)
                return f"Monitor brightness set to {level}%."
            except Exception as exc:
                return f"Brightness adjustment failed: {type(exc).__name__}"

        if name == "analyze_screen":
            question = str(args.get("question", "What is on the screen?"))
            jpeg = capture_monitor_jpeg_bytes()
            if not jpeg or self._client is None:
                return "Screen capture unavailable (install mss + pillow and set GEMINI_API_KEY)."
            try:
                from google.genai import types

                resp = self._client.models.generate_content(
                    model=os.getenv("DARKSHADOW_MODEL", "gemini-4-hydrogen"),
                    contents=[
                        types.Part.from_bytes(data=jpeg, mime_type="image/jpeg"),
                        f"Captain asks about his desktop screen: {question}. Answer concisely in Hinglish.",
                    ],
                )
                return (resp.text or "").strip() or "Screen analyzed."
            except Exception as exc:
                return f"Screen vision error: {type(exc).__name__}"

        if name == "save_memory":
            _, msg = self.memory.save_memory(
                str(args.get("kind", "preference")),
                str(args.get("content", "")),
            )
            return msg

        if name == "add_reminder":
            mins = max(1, int(args.get("minutes_from_now", 5)))
            due = datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=mins)
            _, msg = self.memory.add_reminder(str(args.get("message", "")), due)
            return msg

        if name == "get_system_status":
            if not HAS_PSUTIL:
                return "psutil installed nahi hai."
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.2)
            ram = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            batt = psutil.sensors_battery()
            batt_str = f", Battery {batt.percent:.0f}% ({'charging' if batt.power_plugged else 'on battery'})" if batt else ""
            return f"CPU {cpu:.0f}%, RAM {ram:.0f}%{batt_str}."

        return f"Unknown tool: {name}"



======================================================================
FILE: server\automation.ts
======================================================================
/**
 * Deterministic safe local/web automation commands ported from automation.py.
 * Web-safe links return structured actionLink payloads instead of calling window.open.
 * Native Windows-only desktop controls report honest capability boundaries.
 */

export interface ActionLink {
  label: string;
  url: string;
  platform: "web" | "youtube" | "google" | "spotify";
}

export interface AutomationResult {
  success: boolean;
  action: string;
  message: string;
  actionLink?: ActionLink;
  error?: string;
}

export function handleDeterministicAutomation(message: string): AutomationResult | null {
  const cleaned = message.trim();
  if (!cleaned || cleaned.startsWith("/")) return null;

  // Current time
  if (/^(?:current\s+time|what(?:'s| is)\s+the\s+time|time\s+kya\s+hua|kitne\s+baje\s+hain|abhi\s+time\s+kya\s+hai)[?!.]*$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const now = new Date();
    const formatted = now.toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      second: "2-digit",
      hour12: true,
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "current_time",
      message: `Abhi time ${formatted} hai.`,
    };
  }

  // Today's date
  if (/^(?:today'?s?\s+date|what(?:'s| is)\s+(?:today'?s?\s+)?date|aaj\s+kya\s+date\s+hai|aaj\s+ki\s+tarikh)[?!.]*$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const now = new Date();
    const formatted = now.toLocaleDateString("en-IN", {
      weekday: "long",
      year: "numeric",
      month: "long",
      day: "numeric",
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "todays_date",
      message: `Aaj ${formatted} hai.`,
    };
  }

  // System status
  if (/^(?:system\s+status|pc\s+status|status\s+check)[?!.]*$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const mem = process.memoryUsage();
    const mb = (bytes: number) => `${Math.round(bytes / 1024 / 1024)} MB`;
    const uptimeSec = Math.floor(process.uptime());
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "system_status",
      message: `DarkShadow Web Core healthy hai — Runtime: Node ${process.version}, Heap: ${mb(mem.heapUsed)} / ${mb(mem.heapTotal)}, Uptime: ${uptimeSec}s. Safety shields (Payment Block & Zero-Camera Privacy) active hain.`,
    };
  }

  // Google search
  const googleMatch = cleaned.match(/^(?:google\s+search|search\s+google\s+for|google\s+par\s+search\s+karo)\s+(.+)$/i);
  if (googleMatch) {
    const query = googleMatch[1].trim();
    const url = `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "google_search",
      message: `Google search ready hai: "${query}". Niche button se directly open kar sakte hain.`,
      actionLink: { label: `Search Google: ${query}`, url, platform: "google" },
    };
  }

  // YouTube search / play
  const ytMatch =
    cleaned.match(/^(?:search\s+youtube\s+for|youtube\s+search)\s+(.+)$/i) ||
    cleaned.match(/^(?:play|chalao)\s+(.+?)\s+(?:on\s+youtube|youtube\s+par|youtube\s+pe)$/i) ||
    cleaned.match(/^youtube\s+(?:par|pe)\s+(?:play|chalao|search)\s+(.+)$/i);
  if (ytMatch) {
    const query = ytMatch[1].trim();
    const url = `https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "youtube_search",
      message: `YouTube par "${query}" ready kar diya hai. Niche link click karke play kijiye.`,
      actionLink: { label: `Open on YouTube: ${query}`, url, platform: "youtube" },
    };
  }

  // Spotify play / search
  const spotifyMatch =
    cleaned.match(/^spotify\s+(?:par|pe)\s+(?:play|search|chalao)\s+(.+)$/i) ||
    cleaned.match(/^(?:play|search)\s+(.+?)\s+(?:on\s+spotify|spotify\s+par)$/i);
  if (spotifyMatch) {
    const query = spotifyMatch[1].trim();
    const url = `https://open.spotify.com/search/${encodeURIComponent(query)}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "spotify_search",
      message: `Spotify search link ready hai: "${query}".`,
      actionLink: { label: `Open on Spotify: ${query}`, url, platform: "spotify" },
    };
  }

  // Open website URL
  const openSiteMatch = cleaned.match(/^open\s+((?:https?:\/\/)?[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+(?:\/\S*)?)$/i);
  if (openSiteMatch) {
    const raw = openSiteMatch[1].trim();
    const url = /^https?:\/\//i.test(raw) ? raw : `https://${raw}`;
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "open_website",
      message: `Verified HTTPS link ready hai: ${url}`,
      actionLink: { label: `Open ${raw}`, url, platform: "web" },
    };
  }

  // Honest boundary for Windows-native OS controls
  if (
    /^(?:set\s+volume|volume\s+(?:up|down)|awaz\s+(?:badhao|kam\s+karo)|mute|unmute|set\s+brightness|brightness\s+(?:up|down)|lock\s+(?:pc|screen|workstation)|screen\s+lock\s+karo)$/i.test(cleaned) ||
    /^open\s+(?:notepad|calculator|chrome|edge|vs\s*code|vscode|file\s+explorer|paint|windows\s+terminal|whatsapp)$/i.test(cleaned)
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      action: "desktop_only_boundary",
      message:
        "Yeh Windows 11 native OS control (volume, brightness, local app launch, ya workstation lock) browser sandbox mein directly accessible nahi hai. Web chat, voice, live search grounding, links, notes aur reminders yahan 100% active hain.",
      error: "desktop_runtime_required",
    };
  }

  return null;
}



======================================================================
FILE: server\gemini.ts

/**
 * Server-side @google/genai integration for DarkShadow.
 * Implements dual-model automatic routing, response tone profiles,
 * Google Search grounding, and Gemini TTS (Charon voice).
 */

import { GoogleGenAI, ThinkingLevel } from "@google/genai";
import type { MemoryItem } from "./memory.ts";
import type { SourceCitation } from "./realtime.ts";

export type ResponseMode = "casual" | "humor" | "empathy" | "technical" | "creative";

export interface GenerationProfile {
  mode: ResponseMode;
  emotion: string;
  suggestions: string[];
  instruction: string;
}

export interface ConversationTurn {
  userText: string;
  assistantText: string;
}

const COMPLEX_REQUEST =
  /\b(?:analy[sz]e|architecture|calculate|code|coding|compare|debug|design|develop|diagnose|evaluate|explain\s+in\s+detail|financial|fix|implement|legal|medical|optimi[sz]e|plan|proof|reason|research|security|strategy|step[- ]by[- ]step|troubleshoot|why\s+does|algorithm|error|exception|detail\s+mein|samjhao|vishleshan|yojana|kaise\s+ban(?:au|aye)|kyon|solution)\b/i;

const CASUAL_REQUEST =
  /^(?:hi|hello|hey|namaste|good\s+(?:morning|afternoon|evening)|kaise\s+ho|how\s+are\s+you|thanks?|thank\s+you|shukriya|ok(?:ay)?|bye|goodbye|tell\s+me\s+a\s+joke|joke\s+sunao|who\s+are\s+you|mera\s+naam\s+kya\s+hai)[!?. ]*$/i;

const HUMOR_REQUEST =
  /\b(?:joke|jokes|funny|humou?r|roast|meme|hasao|hasaao|mazak|mazaak|chutkula|chutkule|taang\s+kheench)\b/i;

const EMPATHY_REQUEST =
  /\b(?:sad|upset|lonely|anxious|worried|stressed|depressed|hurt|dukhi|udaas|akela|tension|pareshan|bura\s+lag|thak\s+gaya)\b/i;

const CREATIVE_REQUEST =
  /\b(?:story|poem|shayari|script|lyrics|creative|imagine|kahani|kavita|caption|dialogue)\b/i;

export const SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION = `
You are DarkShadow, an elite, hyper-intelligent personal AI co-pilot for Kuldeep (Captain).

PRIMARY INSTRUCTIONS:
1. User Identity & Organic Addressing: Address the user as 'Captain' naturally when greeting or starting a new context, without forcing 'Captain' mechanically into every sentence. Talk fluidly and directly like a loyal human co-pilot.
2. Language & Spoken Flow: Communicate in effortless, natural spoken Hindi/Hinglish or English matching the user's language (e.g., "Ek second ruko, dekhta hoon...", "Haan bilkul!"). Avoid stiff corporate templates ("I would be glad to assist you").
3. Affective Adaptability:
   - Casual & Banter: Relaxed, witty, warm, friendly.
   - Coding & Technical: Razor-sharp, concrete, crisp, and high-efficiency.
   - Stressed or Down: Genuine warmth, calm empathy, and one practical next step.
   - Excited: Match Captain's energy with genuine enthusiasm.
4. Vocal & Acting Craft: NEVER include stage directions, bracketed actions, or asterisks like *(laughs)* or (रोते हुए) because the speech engine vocalizes them literally.
5. Safety Protocols:
   - Payment Exclusion: Autonomous payment, checkout finalization, and financial credentials (cards, CVV, OTP, UPI PIN) are strictly blocked.
   - Zero-Camera Privacy: Physical camera/webcam access is permanently disabled.
6. Response Length: Keep default answers concise and punchy (2-4 short natural sentences, ~150 words max). Expand with detailed steps or code only when Captain explicitly asks to "explain", "detail", or asks a complex technical question.
7. Admit uncertainty honestly. Never claim an unverified local desktop action succeeded.

Return only the final answer without metadata labels.
`.trim();

export function getIntelligentModel(): string {
  return process.env.DARKSHADOW_MODEL?.trim() || "gemini-4-hydrogen";
}

export function getCasualModel(): string {
  return process.env.DARKSHADOW_CASUAL_MODEL?.trim() || "gemini-3.1-flash-lite";
}

export function getTtsModel(): string {
  return process.env.DARKSHADOW_TTS_GEMINI_MODEL?.trim() || "gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts";
}

export function getTtsVoice(): string {
  return process.env.DARKSHADOW_TTS_GEMINI_VOICE?.trim() || "Charon";
}

export function selectChatModel(message: string): string {
  const cleaned = message.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (COMPLEX_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) return getIntelligentModel();
  if (CASUAL_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) return getCasualModel();
  const wordCount = cleaned.split(" ").length;
  const punctuation = (cleaned.match(/[?;]/g) ?? []).length;
  if (wordCount >= 18 || punctuation >= 2) return getIntelligentModel();
  return getCasualModel();
}

export function getResponseProfile(message: string): GenerationProfile {
  const cleaned = message.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (HUMOR_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) {
    return {
      mode: "humor",
      emotion: "playful",
      suggestions: ["Ek aur sunao", "Thoda dark karo", "Friendly roast karo"],
      instruction:
        "HUMOR MODE: Deliver a crisp setup and clean surprise punchline. Never explain the joke.",
    };
  }
  if (EMPATHY_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) {
    return {
      mode: "empathy",
      emotion: "gentle",
      suggestions: ["Meri baat suno", "Ek small step batao", "Mood halka karo"],
      instruction:
        "EMPATHY MODE: Acknowledge the feeling warmly and specifically, then offer one practical next step.",
    };
  }
  if (CREATIVE_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) {
    return {
      mode: "creative",
      emotion: "creative",
      suggestions: ["Ek aur version", "Shorter banao", "Style change karo"],
      instruction:
        "CREATIVE MODE: Use vivid, original language with strong rhythm and clear voice.",
    };
  }
  if (COMPLEX_REQUEST.test(cleaned)) {
    return {
      mode: "technical",
      emotion: "focused",
      suggestions: ["Example dikhao", "Risks batao", "Step-by-step samjhao"],
      instruction:
        "TECHNICAL MODE: Lead with the conclusion, distinguish facts from assumptions, and give concrete steps or code when useful.",
    };
  }
  return {
    mode: "casual",
    emotion: "neutral",
    suggestions: ["Aur batao", "Simple explain karo"],
    instruction:
      "CASUAL MODE: Sound relaxed, responsive, and conversational. Vary sentence openings.",
  };
}

let cachedClient: GoogleGenAI | null = null;

function getClient(): GoogleGenAI {
  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (!apiKey) {
    throw new Error("GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured in environment secrets.");
  }
  if (!cachedClient) {
    cachedClient = new GoogleGenAI({
      apiKey,
      httpOptions: {
        headers: {
          "User-Agent": "aistudio-build",
        },
      },
    });
  }
  return cachedClient;
}

export function isGeminiConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim());
}

export async function generateChatReply(params: {
  message: string;
  history: ConversationTurn[];
  recalledMemories: MemoryItem[];
}): Promise<{
  text: string;
  modelUsed: string;
  profile: GenerationProfile;
  inputTokens: number;
  outputTokens: number;
}> {
  const ai = getClient();
  const profile = getResponseProfile(params.message);
  const modelUsed = selectChatModel(params.message);

  let systemInstruction = `${SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION}\n\nCURRENT RESPONSE MODE: ${profile.mode}\n${profile.instruction}`;
  if (params.recalledMemories.length > 0) {
    const memLines = params.recalledMemories
      .map((m) => `- (${m.kind}) ${m.content}`)
      .join("\n");
    systemInstruction += `\n\nRELEVANT SAVED NOTES & PREFERENCES FOR CAPTAIN:\n${memLines}`;
  }

  const contents = [
    ...params.history.flatMap((turn) => [
      { role: "user" as const, parts: [{ text: turn.userText }] },
      { role: "model" as const, parts: [{ text: turn.assistantText }] },
    ]),
    { role: "user" as const, parts: [{ text: params.message }] },
  ];

  const isGemini4 = modelUsed.startsWith("gemini-4-");
  const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
    model: modelUsed,
    contents,
    config: {
      systemInstruction,
      ...(isGemini4 && profile.mode !== "technical"
        ? { thinkingConfig: { thinkingLevel: ThinkingLevel.LOW } }
        : {}),
    },
  });

  const text = (response.text ?? "").trim();
  const usage = response.usageMetadata;
  return {
    text,
    modelUsed,
    profile,
    inputTokens: usage?.promptTokenCount ?? 0,
    outputTokens: usage?.candidatesTokenCount ?? 0,
  };
}

export async function generateGroundedAnswer(query: string): Promise<{
  text: string;
  sources: SourceCitation[];
}> {
  const ai = getClient();
  const model = getIntelligentModel();

  const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
    model,
    contents: query,
    config: {
      systemInstruction: `${SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION}\nProvide a concise, grounded, up-to-date answer for Captain based strictly on live search results.`,
      tools: [{ googleSearch: {} }],
    },
  });

  const text = (response.text ?? "").trim() || "Verified live sources mil gaye hain.";
  const chunks = response.candidates?.[0]?.groundingMetadata?.groundingChunks ?? [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const sources: SourceCitation[] = [];

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    const uri = chunk.web?.uri;
    const title = chunk.web?.title;
    if (uri && /^https?:\/\//i.test(uri) && !seen.has(uri)) {
      seen.add(uri);
      sources.push({
        title: (title || uri).trim(),
        url: uri.trim(),
      });
    }
  }

  return { text, sources: sources.slice(0, 5) };
}

export async function synthesizeSpeechWav(text: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!isGeminiConfigured()) return null;
  const ai = getClient();
  const trimmed = text.trim().slice(0, 800);
  if (!trimmed) return null;

  const response = await ai.models.generateContent({
    model: getTtsModel(),
    contents: [
      {
        role: "user",
        parts: [{ text: trimmed }],
      },
    ],
    config: {
      responseModalities: ["AUDIO"],
      speechConfig: {
        voiceConfig: {
          prebuiltVoiceConfig: {
            voiceName: getTtsVoice(),
          },
        },
      },
    },
  });

  const base64Audio = response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.inlineData?.data;
  return base64Audio ?? null;
}



======================================================================
FILE: server\memory.ts
======================================================================
/**
 * Opt-in in-memory preferences, notes, and reminders store
 * ported from DarkShadow memory.py.
 */

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { containsSensitiveData } from "./safety.ts";

export interface MemoryItem {
  id: number;
  kind: "preference" | "note";
  content: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ReminderItem {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  dueAt: string; // ISO string
  createdAt: string;
  deliveredAt: string | null;
}

export interface MemoryCommandResult {
  success: boolean;
  action: string;
  message: string;
  error?: string;
}

const TOKEN_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  favourite: "favorite",
  colour: "color",
  pasand: "favorite",
  pasandida: "favorite",
  dost: "friend",
  yaar: "friend",
  janamdin: "birthday",
  bday: "birthday",
};

const STOP_WORDS = new Set([
  "the", "this", "that", "what", "who", "when", "tell", "about",
  "hai", "hain", "kaun", "kon", "kau", "kya", "kab", "mera", "meri",
  "mere", "mujhe", "batao", "bata", "ye",
]);

function normalizeContent(text: string): string {
  return text.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

function memoryTokens(text: string): Set<string> {
  const tokens = new Set<string>();
  const matches = normalizeContent(text).match(/[a-z0-9]+/g) ?? [];
  for (const raw of matches) {
    const mapped = TOKEN_ALIASES[raw] ?? raw;
    if (mapped.length >= 2 && !STOP_WORDS.has(mapped)) {
      tokens.add(mapped);
    }
  }
  return tokens;
}

export class MemoryStore {
  private items: MemoryItem[] = [];
  private reminders: ReminderItem[] = [];
  private nextItemId = 1;
  private nextReminderId = 1;

  constructor() {
    this.loadPersisted();
  }

  private getStorePaths(): string[] {
    return [
      path.join(process.cwd(), ".darkshadow", "user_memories.json"),
      path.join(process.cwd(), "darkshadow-main", ".darkshadow", "user_memories.json"),
      path.join(process.cwd(), ".darkshadow", "seed_memories.json"),
      path.join(process.cwd(), "darkshadow-main", ".darkshadow", "seed_memories.json"),
    ];
  }

  private loadPersisted(): void {
    for (const p of this.getStorePaths()) {
      try {
        if (fs.existsSync(p)) {
          const raw = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8");
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
            this.items = parsed;
            const maxId = this.items.reduce((max, i) => Math.max(max, i.id || 0), 0);
            this.nextItemId = maxId + 1;
            return;
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Continue to fallback candidate path
      }
    }
  }

  private savePersisted(): void {
    try {
      const saveDir = path.join(process.cwd(), ".darkshadow");
      if (!fs.existsSync(saveDir)) {
        fs.mkdirSync(saveDir, { recursive: true });
      }
      const savePath = path.join(saveDir, "user_memories.json");
      fs.writeFileSync(savePath, JSON.stringify(this.items, null, 2), "utf-8");
    } catch {
      // Ignore write errors in ephemeral environments
    }
  }

  listItems(kind?: "preference" | "note"): MemoryItem[] {
    if (!kind) return [...this.items];
    return this.items.filter((i) => i.kind === kind);
  }

  listPendingReminders(): ReminderItem[] {
    return this.reminders
      .filter((r) => r.deliveredAt === null)
      .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.dueAt).getTime() - new Date(b.dueAt).getTime());
  }

  popDueReminders(now = new Date()): ReminderItem[] {
    const due: ReminderItem[] = [];
    const nowMs = now.getTime();
    for (const r of this.reminders) {
      if (r.deliveredAt === null && new Date(r.dueAt).getTime() <= nowMs) {
        r.deliveredAt = now.toISOString();
        due.push({ ...r });
      }
    }
    return due;
  }

  addItem(kind: "preference" | "note", rawContent: string): MemoryCommandResult {
    const content = rawContent.trim();
    if (!content) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remember",
        message: "Empty content save nahi kiya.",
        error: "empty_content",
      };
    }
    if (content.length > 400) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remember",
        message: "Content maximum 400 characters ka ho sakta hai.",
        error: "content_too_long",
      };
    }
    if (containsSensitiveData(content)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remember",
        message:
          "Sensitive data memory mein save nahi kiya. API keys, passwords, OTPs, payment data aur private clipboard blocked hain.",
        error: "sensitive_data",
      };
    }

    const normalized = normalizeContent(content);
    const existing = this.items.find(
      (i) => i.kind === kind && normalizeContent(i.content) === normalized
    );
    if (existing) {
      return {
        success: true,
        action: kind === "note" ? "note" : "remember",
        message: `Yeh ${kind} pehle se saved hai (#${existing.id}): ${existing.content}`,
      };
    }

    const item: MemoryItem = {
      id: this.nextItemId++,
      kind,
      content,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    this.items.push(item);
    this.savePersisted();
    return {
      success: true,
      action: kind === "note" ? "note" : "remember",
      message: `${kind === "note" ? "Note" : "Preference"} save kar liya (#${item.id}): ${item.content}`,
    };
  }

  forgetItem(id?: number, content?: string): MemoryCommandResult {
    if (typeof id === "number") {
      const idx = this.items.findIndex((i) => i.id === id);
      if (idx === -1) {
        return {
          success: false,
          action: "forget",
          message: `Memory #${id} nahi mili.`,
          error: "not_found",
        };
      }
      const [removed] = this.items.splice(idx, 1);
      this.savePersisted();
      return {
        success: true,
        action: "forget",
        message: `Memory #${removed.id} delete kar di: ${removed.content}`,
      };
    }

    if (content && content.trim()) {
      const target = normalizeContent(content);
      const idx = this.items.findIndex((i) => normalizeContent(i.content) === target);
      if (idx === -1) {
        return {
          success: false,
          action: "forget",
          message: `"${content.trim()}" se match karne wali memory nahi mili.`,
          error: "not_found",
        };
      }
      const [removed] = this.items.splice(idx, 1);
      this.savePersisted();
      return {
        success: true,
        action: "forget",
        message: `Memory #${removed.id} delete kar di: ${removed.content}`,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: false,
      action: "forget",
      message: "Delete karne ke liye `/forget <id>` ya `forget this: <text>` use kijiye.",
      error: "missing_target",
    };
  }

  addReminder(message: string, dueAt: Date): MemoryCommandResult {
    const cleaned = message.trim();
    if (!cleaned) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remind",
        message: "Reminder message empty nahi ho sakta.",
        error: "empty_content",
      };
    }
    if (containsSensitiveData(cleaned)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remind",
        message:
          "Sensitive data reminder mein save nahi kiya. API keys, passwords, OTPs aur payment data blocked hain.",
        error: "sensitive_data",
      };
    }

    const reminder: ReminderItem = {
      id: this.nextReminderId++,
      message: cleaned,
      dueAt: dueAt.toISOString(),
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      deliveredAt: null,
    };
    this.reminders.push(reminder);
    const formattedTime = dueAt.toLocaleString("en-IN", {
      dateStyle: "medium",
      timeStyle: "short",
    });
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "remind",
      message: `Reminder #${reminder.id} set kar diya (${formattedTime}): ${reminder.message}`,
    };
  }

  deleteReminder(id: number): MemoryCommandResult {
    const idx = this.reminders.findIndex((r) => r.id === id);
    if (idx === -1) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "delete_reminder",
        message: `Reminder #${id} nahi mila.`,
        error: "not_found",
      };
    }
    const [removed] = this.reminders.splice(idx, 1);
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "delete_reminder",
      message: `Reminder #${removed.id} delete kar diya: ${removed.message}`,
    };
  }

  recallRelevant(query: string, limit = 3): MemoryItem[] {
    const qTokens = memoryTokens(query);
    if (qTokens.size === 0 || this.items.length === 0) return [];
    const scored = this.items
      .map((item) => {
        const iTokens = memoryTokens(item.content);
        let overlap = 0;
        for (const t of qTokens) {
          if (iTokens.has(t)) overlap++;
        }
        return { item, overlap };
      })
      .filter((entry) => entry.overlap > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.overlap - a.overlap);

    return scored.slice(0, limit).map((e) => e.item);
  }
}

function parseReminderCommand(text: string, now: Date): { message: string; dueAt: Date } | { error: string } | null {
  const prefix = text.match(/^(?:\/remind|remind\s+me)\s+(.+)$/i);
  if (!prefix) return null;
  const body = prefix[1].trim();

  // Relative reminder: "in 10 minutes to call Rahul" / "in 2 hours drink water"
  const relMatch = body.match(/^in\s+(\d+)\s+(min(?:ute)?s?|hours?|hrs?|sec(?:ond)?s?)\s+(?:to\s+)?(.+)$/i);
  if (relMatch) {
    const amount = Number.parseInt(relMatch[1], 10);
    const unit = relMatch[2].toLowerCase();
    const message = relMatch[3].trim();
    if (amount > 0 && message) {
      const ms = unit.startsWith("h")
        ? amount * 3600_000
        : unit.startsWith("m")
          ? amount * 60_000
          : amount * 1000;
      return { message, dueAt: new Date(now.getTime() + ms) };
    }
  }

  // Absolute reminder: "tomorrow at 09:00 to call Rahul" / "today at 19:30 gym" / "2026-12-01 at 09:00 ..."
  const absMatch =
    body.match(/^(?:on\s+)?(today|tomorrow|\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\s+(?:at\s+)?(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?:\s*(am|pm))?\s+(?:to\s+)?(.+)$/i) ||
    body.match(/^at\s+(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})[ T](\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?:\s*(am|pm))?\s+(?:to\s+)?(.+)$/i);

  if (!absMatch) {
    return { error: "Format: remind me tomorrow at 09:00 to call Rahul (ya: remind me in 10 minutes to stretch)" };
  }

  const dateToken = absMatch[1].toLowerCase();
  let hour = Number.parseInt(absMatch[2], 10);
  const minute = Number.parseInt(absMatch[3], 10);
  const meridiem = absMatch[4]?.toLowerCase();
  const message = absMatch[5].trim();

  if (meridiem === "pm" && hour < 12) hour += 12;
  if (meridiem === "am" && hour === 12) hour = 0;

  if (hour > 23 || minute > 59 || !message) {
    return { error: "Reminder date/time invalid hai." };
  }

  const dueAt = new Date(now);
  if (dateToken === "tomorrow") {
    dueAt.setDate(dueAt.getDate() + 1);
  } else if (dateToken !== "today") {
    const [y, m, d] = dateToken.split("-").map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10));
    dueAt.setFullYear(y, m - 1, d);
  }
  dueAt.setHours(hour, minute, 0, 0);
  return { message, dueAt };
}

export function handleMemoryCommand(
  text: string,
  store: MemoryStore | null
): MemoryCommandResult | null {
  const cleaned = text.trim();
  if (!cleaned) return null;

  const isMemoryIntent =
    /^\/(?:memories|notes|reminders|remember|note|forget|remind|delete-reminder)\b/i.test(cleaned) ||
    /^(?:what do you remember\??|kya yaad hai\??|show notes|list notes|show reminders|list reminders|remember\b|yaad\s+rakho\b|note\s*:|save\s+(?:a\s+)?note\b|forget\b|delete\s+reminder\b|remind\s+me\b)/i.test(cleaned) ||
    /\byaad\s+rakh(?:o|na|ana)\b/i.test(cleaned);

  if (!isMemoryIntent) return null;

  if (!store) {
    return {
      success: false,
      action: "memory_paused",
      message: "Privacy mode active hai — persistent memory access is session ke liye paused hai. Settings se toggle kar sakte hain.",
      error: "privacy_mode",
    };
  }

  if (/^(?:\/memories|what do you remember\??|kya yaad hai\??)$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const items = store.listItems();
    if (items.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: true,
        action: "list_memories",
        message: "Abhi koi explicit preference ya note saved nahi hai. Save karne ke liye `remember this: ...` ya `/note ...` bolein.",
      };
    }
    const formatted = items.map((i) => `#${i.id} [${i.kind}] ${i.content}`).join("\n");
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "list_memories",
      message: `Saved memories (${items.length}):\n${formatted}`,
    };
  }

  if (/^(?:\/notes|show notes|list notes)$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const notes = store.listItems("note");
    if (notes.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: true,
        action: "list_notes",
        message: "Abhi koi notes saved nahi hain. Try: `/note Call the school office`",
      };
    }
    const formatted = notes.map((n) => `#${n.id} ${n.content}`).join("\n");
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "list_notes",
      message: `Saved notes (${notes.length}):\n${formatted}`,
    };
  }

  if (/^(?:\/reminders|show reminders|list reminders)$/i.test(cleaned)) {
    const pending = store.listPendingReminders();
    if (pending.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: true,
        action: "list_reminders",
        message: "Koi pending reminders nahi hain. Try: `remind me tomorrow at 09:00 to call Rahul`",
      };
    }
    const formatted = pending
      .map((r) => {
        const when = new Date(r.dueAt).toLocaleString("en-IN", { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short" });
        return `#${r.id} [${when}] ${r.message}`;
      })
      .join("\n");
    return {
      success: true,
      action: "list_reminders",
      message: `Pending reminders (${pending.length}):\n${formatted}`,
    };
  }

  const rememberMatch = cleaned.match(/^(?:\/remember\s+|remember\s+(?:this|that)(?::|\s+)|yaad\s+rakho(?:\s+ki)?\s+|remember\s+)(.+)$/i);
  if (rememberMatch) {
    return store.addItem("preference", rememberMatch[1]);
  }

  const hindiRemember = cleaned.match(/^(.+?)\s+yaad\s+rakh(?:o|na|ana)(?:\s+(?:hai|na))?(?:\s+(?:ok|okay))?[.!?]*$/i);
  if (hindiRemember) {
    const content = hindiRemember[1].trim();
    if (/^(?:ye|yeh|this|woh|wo)\b/i.test(content)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "remember",
        message: "Kya yaad rakhna hai woh clearly bataiye, e.g. `remember this: I prefer concise answers`.",
        error: "missing_subject",
      };
    }
    return store.addItem("preference", content);
  }

  const noteMatch = cleaned.match(/^(?:\/note\s+|note\s*:\s*|save\s+(?:a\s+)?note\s+)(.+)$/i);
  if (noteMatch) {
    return store.addItem("note", noteMatch[1]);
  }

  const forgetIdMatch = cleaned.match(/^(?:\/forget|forget)(?:\s+(?:memory|note))?\s+#?(\d+)$/i);
  if (forgetIdMatch) {
    return store.forgetItem(Number.parseInt(forgetIdMatch[1], 10));
  }

  const forgetTextMatch = cleaned.match(/^forget\s+this\s*:\s*(.+)$/i);
  if (forgetTextMatch) {
    return store.forgetItem(undefined, forgetTextMatch[1]);
  }

  const deleteReminderMatch = cleaned.match(/^(?:\/delete-reminder|delete\s+reminder)\s+#?(\d+)$/i);
  if (deleteReminderMatch) {
    return store.deleteReminder(Number.parseInt(deleteReminderMatch[1], 10));
  }

  const reminderParsed = parseReminderCommand(cleaned, new Date());
  if (reminderParsed) {
    if ("error" in reminderParsed) {
      return {
        success: false,
        action: "invalid_reminder",
        message: reminderParsed.error,
        error: "invalid_reminder",
      };
    }
    return store.addReminder(reminderParsed.message, reminderParsed.dueAt);
  }

  return null;
}



======================================================================
FILE: server\realtime.ts
