/**
 * Fresh-information routing backed by Tavily Search (when TAVILY_API_KEY is set)
 * with automatic fallback to Gemini Google Search Grounding (gemini-4-hydrogen).
 * Ported from DarkShadow realtimeinfo.py.
 */

import { generateGroundedAnswer } from "./gemini.ts";
import { ensureCaptainAddress, speechFriendlyText } from "./safety.ts";

export interface SourceCitation {
  title: string;
  url: string;
}

export interface RealtimeAnswer {
  displayText: string;
  speechText: string;
  intent: string;
  sources: SourceCitation[];
  provider: "tavily" | "gemini_grounding";
  error?: string;
}

const FRESH_TERMS = [
  "latest", "today", "tonight", "current", "currently", "recent",
  "right now", "this week", "this month", "tomorrow", "yesterday",
  "abhi", "aaj", "kal", "taaza", "taza", "आज", "अभी", "कल",
  "ताज़ा", "ताजा", "news", "headline", "breaking", "khabar",
  "samachar", "खबर", "समाचार", "politics", "political", "election",
  "president", "prime minister", "chief minister", "government",
  "minister", "chunav", "चुनाव", "राष्ट्रपति", "प्रधानमंत्री",
  "मुख्यमंत्री", "weather", "forecast", "temperature", "rainfall",
  "mausam", "baarish", "barish", "मौसम", "बारिश", "price", "cost",
  "exchange rate", "stock", "share market", "crypto", "bitcoin",
  "gold rate", "silver rate", "petrol", "diesel", "daam", "bhaav",
  "कीमत", "दाम", "भाव", "score", "fixture", "schedule", "standings",
  "points table", "match result", "ipl", "cricket match",
  "football match", "स्कोर", "मैच", "कार्यक्रम", "version",
  "release date", "software update",
];

const MAPS_TERMS = [
  "near me", "nearby", "paas", "hotel", "hotels", "restaurant", "restaurants",
  "cafe", "cafes", "hospital", "hospitals", "petrol pump", "ev charging",
  "gas station", "route", "direction", "directions", "rashta", "rasta",
  "distance", "kahan hai", "kidhar hai", "location", "map", "maps",
  "dhaba", "cinema", "theatre", "theater", "mall", "railway station",
  "bus stand", "airport", "navigate", "traffic",
];

function containsTerm(text: string, term: string): boolean {
  if (/[^\u0000-\u007f]/.test(term)) {
    return text.includes(term);
  }
  const escaped = term.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  return new RegExp(`(?<!\\w)${escaped}(?!\\w)`, "i").test(text);
}

export function requiresFreshInformation(query: string): boolean {
  const normalized = query.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!normalized) return false;
  if (
    /\b(what|which|kaun|konsa|kaunsa)\b.*\b(model|api|assistant)\b|\b(model|api)\b.*\b(use|using|chala|kar rahe|status)\b|\b(tum|aap|you)\b.*\b(kaun|who)\b/i.test(
      normalized
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  return (
    FRESH_TERMS.some((term) => containsTerm(normalized, term)) ||
    MAPS_TERMS.some((term) => containsTerm(normalized, term))
  );
}

function topicForQuery(query: string): "finance" | "news" | "general" {
  const lower = query.toLowerCase();
  if (/(stock|share market|crypto|bitcoin|gold rate|finance)/.test(lower)) return "finance";
  if (/(news|headline|breaking|khabar|samachar|election)/.test(lower)) return "news";
  return "general";
}

const cache = new Map<string, { expiresAt: number; value: RealtimeAnswer }>();

export async function answerFreshQuery(query: string): Promise<RealtimeAnswer> {
  const cacheKey = query.toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  const ttlMs = Number(process.env.DARKSHADOW_REALTIME_CACHE_SECONDS || 300) * 1000;
  const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached && cached.expiresAt > Date.now()) {
    return cached.value;
  }

  const tavilyKey = process.env.TAVILY_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (tavilyKey) {
    try {
      const response = await fetch("https://api.tavily.com/search", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${tavilyKey}`,
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          query,
          search_depth: process.env.DARKSHADOW_TAVILY_SEARCH_DEPTH || "basic",
          max_results: Number(process.env.DARKSHADOW_TAVILY_MAX_RESULTS || 5),
          topic: topicForQuery(query),
          include_answer: "basic",
          include_raw_content: false,
          include_images: false,
        }),
      });
      if (response.ok) {
        const data = (await response.json()) as {
          answer?: string;
          results?: Array<{ title?: string; url?: string; content?: string }>;
        };
        const sources: SourceCitation[] = (data.results ?? [])
          .filter((r) => typeof r.url === "string" && /^https?:\/\//i.test(r.url))
          .slice(0, 5)
          .map((r) => ({
            title: (r.title || r.url || "Source").trim(),
            url: r.url!.trim(),
          }));

        if (sources.length > 0) {
          const rawText =
            data.answer?.trim() ||
            data.results?.[0]?.content?.trim() ||
            "Verified live sources mil gaye hain.";
          const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(rawText);
          const result: RealtimeAnswer = {
            displayText,
            speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
            intent: "realtime_info",
            sources,
            provider: "tavily",
          };
          cache.set(cacheKey, { expiresAt: Date.now() + ttlMs, value: result });
          return result;
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to Gemini Search Grounding below
    }
  }

  // Fallback to Gemini Google Search Grounding so live info works out-of-the-box
  const grounded = await generateGroundedAnswer(query);
  const displayText = ensureCaptainAddress(grounded.text);
  const result: RealtimeAnswer = {
    displayText,
    speechText: speechFriendlyText(displayText),
    intent: "realtime_info",
    sources: grounded.sources,
    provider: "gemini_grounding",
  };
  if (grounded.sources.length > 0) {
    cache.set(cacheKey, { expiresAt: Date.now() + ttlMs, value: result });
  }
  return result;
}

/**
 * Safety shields, Captain addressing, and speech-friendly normalization
 * ported from DarkShadow safety.py and memory.py.
 */

const CAPTAIN_PATTERN = /\b(?:captain|कैप्टन)\b/i;

export function ensureCaptainAddress(text: string): string {
  const cleaned = text.trim();
  if (!cleaned) {
    return "Captain, mujhe khaali response mila. Please dobara try kijiye.";
  }
  if (CAPTAIN_PATTERN.test(cleaned)) {
    return cleaned;
  }
  return `Captain, ${cleaned}`;
}

export function speechFriendlyText(displayText: string): string {
  const replacements: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
    [/\bCaptain\b/g, "कैप्टन"],
    [/\bcaptain\b/g, "कैप्टन"],
    [/\bNamaste\b/g, "नमस्ते"],
    [/\bnamaste\b/g, "नमस्ते"],
    [/\baap\b/g, "आप"],
    [/\bkijiye\b/g, "कीजिए"],
    [/\bdobara\b/g, "दोबारा"],
    [/\bsamajh\b/g, "समझ"],
    [/\bnahi\b/g, "नहीं"],
    [/\bhaan\b/g, "हाँ"],
    [/\btheek\b/g, "ठीक"],
  ];

  // Strip markdown/links so TTS reads cleanly
  let result = displayText
    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, " code block ")
    .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, "$1")
    .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "$1")
    .replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g, "$1")
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/g, "$1")
    .replace(/https?:\/\/\S+/g, "");

  for (const [pattern, target] of replacements) {
    result = result.replace(pattern, target);
  }
  return result.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
}

const PAYMENT_PATTERNS =
  /(?:\b(?:buy[-_ ]*now|place[-_ ]*order|proceed[-_ ]*to[-_ ]*pay|pay[-_ ]*now|complete[-_ ]*payment|payment\s*karo|pay\s*karo|kharido|kharid\s*lo|order\s*place\s*karo|checkout|enter\s*cvv|enter\s*otp|upi[-_ ]*pin|atm[-_ ]*pin|credit[-_ ]*card|debit[-_ ]*card|card\s*details|bank\s*transfer|net\s*banking|भुगतान|खरीदो|पेमेंट|आर्डर\s*प्लेस)\b|pay[-_]now|payment|checkout)/i;

export const PAYMENT_BLOCKED_MESSAGE =
  "Security protocol: Automated payment, checkout aur financial credentials execution strictly blocked hai. Comparison available hai, par final payment kripya manually complete kijiye.";

export function isPaymentOrCheckoutIntent(text: string): boolean {
  if (!text) return false;
  return PAYMENT_PATTERNS.test(text);
}

const PHYSICAL_CAMERA_PATTERNS =
  /\b(?:webcam|camera|cam|video[-_ ]*call|face[-_ ]*record(?:ing)?|record[-_ ]*face|camera\s*(?:on|kholo|chalao|start|open)|turn\s*on\s*camera|open\s*camera|start\s*camera|webcam\s*(?:on|kholo|chalao|start|open)|turn\s*on\s*webcam|open\s*webcam|start\s*webcam|chehra\s*(?:dekho|dikhao|record)|face\s*(?:dekho|dikhao|record)|capture\s*(?:video|selfie|face)|take\s*selfie|selfie\s*lo|photo\s*(?:khincho|lo)|कैमरा|वेबकैम|चेहरा\s*देखो)\b/i;

export const CAMERA_BLOCKED_MESSAGE =
  "Privacy Protocol: Captain ki privacy ke mutabiq physical camera/webcam hardware access permanently disabled hai. Face ya room recording strictly blocked hai.";

export function isPhysicalCameraIntent(text: string): boolean {
  if (!text) return false;
  const cleaned = text.replace(/[-_]+/g, " ").trim();
  return PHYSICAL_CAMERA_PATTERNS.test(cleaned);
}

const DANGEROUS_TERMINAL_RULES: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
  [/\bformat\s+[a-z]:/i, "Disk formatting is strictly blocked to protect your drives."],
  [/\b(?:diskpart|bootrec|bcdedit)\b/i, "Partition and boot record modification commands are strictly blocked."],
  [/\bcipher\s+\/[wW]/i, "Drive data wiping is blocked."],
  [/\b(?:del|erase|rmdir|rd|Remove-Item)\b.*?(?:[a-z]:\\|\\windows|\\system32|\bwindows\b|\bsystem32\b)/i, "System and root directory deletion is strictly blocked."],
  [/\brm\s+-[rf]+\s+.*?(?:\/|[a-z]:\\|\\windows|\\system32)/i, "Recursive force removal of system/root paths is blocked."],
  [/\breg\s+delete\s+.*(?:hklm|hkcr|hku)\b/i, "Deleting critical system registry hives is strictly blocked."],
  [/\bshutdown\s+\/[sSrR]/i, "Unconfirmed system shutdown/restart commands are blocked."],
  [/\b(?:iex|invoke-expression)\s*[({]|(?:downloadstring|downloadfile)\b/i, "Executing untrusted remote download scripts is blocked."],
  [/\bpowershell(?:\.exe)?\s+.*-(?:enc|encodedcommand)\b/i, "Obfuscated encoded PowerShell execution is blocked."],
  [/\|\s*(?:bash|powershell|cmd|iex)\b/i, "Piping untrusted network output into shell interpreters is blocked."],
  [/\bdrop\s+(?:database|schema)\b/i, "Database drop operations are blocked."],
];

export function isDangerousTerminalCommand(command: string): { blocked: boolean; reason: string } {
  const cleaned = command.trim();
  if (!cleaned) return { blocked: false, reason: "" };
  for (const [pattern, reason] of DANGEROUS_TERMINAL_RULES) {
    if (pattern.test(cleaned)) {
      return { blocked: true, reason: `Security Shield Blocked: ${reason}` };
    }
  }
  return { blocked: false, reason: "" };
}

const SENSITIVE_LABELS =
  /\b(?:api\s*key|password|passcode|passwd|otp|one[- ]time password|cvv|card\s*(?:number|no)|credit\s*card|debit\s*card|bank\s*account|upi\s*pin|private\s*key|secret\s*key|access\s*token|refresh\s*token|private\s*clipboard|clipboard\s*content)\b/i;

const SECRET_SHAPES: RegExp[] = [
  /\bAIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{20,}\b/,
  /\bAQ[0-9A-Za-z_-]{20,}\b/,
  /\bsk-[0-9A-Za-z_-]{16,}\b/i,
  /-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----/,
  /\b(?:\d[ -]*?){13,19}\b/,
];

export function containsSensitiveData(content: string): boolean {
  const cleaned = content.trim();
  if (SENSITIVE_LABELS.test(cleaned)) return true;
  return SECRET_SHAPES.some((pattern) => pattern.test(cleaned));
}

/**
 * Lightweight, zero-dependency ZIP archive builder (STORE method, CRC-32)
 * compatible with Windows 11 File Explorer "Extract All".
 */

const CRC_TABLE = new Uint32Array(256);
for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
  let c = i;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
    c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
  }
  CRC_TABLE[i] = c >>> 0;
}

function crc32(buf: Buffer): number {
  let crc = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) {
    crc = CRC_TABLE[(crc ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (crc >>> 8);
  }
  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

export interface ZipEntry {
  name: string;
  data: Buffer;
}

export function buildZipBuffer(entries: ZipEntry[]): Buffer {
  const localParts: Buffer[] = [];
  const centralParts: Buffer[] = [];
  let offset = 0;

  const now = new Date();
  const dosYear = Math.max(0, now.getFullYear() - 1980);
  const dosDate = (dosYear << 9) | ((now.getMonth() + 1) << 5) | now.getDate();
  const dosTime =
    (now.getHours() << 11) | (now.getMinutes() << 5) | Math.floor(now.getSeconds() / 2);

  for (const entry of entries) {
    const nameBuf = Buffer.from(entry.name, "utf-8");
    const crc = crc32(entry.data);
    const size = entry.data.length;

    const localHeader = Buffer.alloc(30);
    localHeader.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0);
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 6); // UTF-8 filename encoding
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 8); // STORE
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(dosTime, 10);
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(dosDate, 12);
    localHeader.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14);
    localHeader.writeUInt32LE(size, 18);
    localHeader.writeUInt32LE(size, 22);
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 26);
    localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 28);

    localParts.push(localHeader, nameBuf, entry.data);

    const centralHeader = Buffer.alloc(46);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 6);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 8); // UTF-8 filename encoding
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 10);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(dosTime, 12);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(dosDate, 14);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(size, 20);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(size, 24);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 28);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 30);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 32);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 34);
    centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 36);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 38);
    centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(offset, 42);

    centralParts.push(centralHeader, nameBuf);
    offset += localHeader.length + nameBuf.length + size;
  }

  const centralDir = Buffer.concat(centralParts);
  const eocd = Buffer.alloc(22);
  eocd.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0);
  eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 4);
  eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 6);
  eocd.writeUInt16LE(entries.length, 8);
  eocd.writeUInt16LE(entries.length, 10);
  eocd.writeUInt32LE(centralDir.length, 12);
  eocd.writeUInt32LE(offset, 16);
  eocd.writeUInt16LE(0, 20);

  return Buffer.concat([...localParts, centralDir, eocd]);
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

interface BrowserSpeechRecognitionEvent {
  results: {
    length: number;
    [index: number]: {
      isFinal: boolean;
      [altIndex: number]: { transcript: string };
    };
  };
}

interface BrowserSpeechRecognition {
  lang: string;
  interimResults: boolean;
  maxAlternatives: number;
  continuous: boolean;
  onresult: ((ev: BrowserSpeechRecognitionEvent) => void) | null;
  onerror: ((ev: { error: string }) => void) | null;
  onend: (() => void) | null;
  start: () => void;
  stop: () => void;
  abort: () => void;
}

declare global {
  interface Window {
    SpeechRecognition?: new () => BrowserSpeechRecognition;
    webkitSpeechRecognition?: new () => BrowserSpeechRecognition;
  }
}

const WAKE_REGEX = /\b(?:hey\s+|hello\s+|ok\s+|suno\s+)?(?:dark\s*shadow|darkshadow|डार्क\s*शैडो)\b/i;
const SLEEP_REGEX = /\b(?:sleep|so\s*jao|bye|goodbye|band\s*ho\s*jao|hide)\b/i;

const SHAPE_OPTIONS: Array<{ id: ParticleShape; label: string }> = [

export default function App() {
  const [particlePhase, setParticlePhase] = useState<ParticlePhase>("rising");

  const [assistantState, setAssistantState] = useState<AssistantState>("idle");
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<string>("Summoning 6,000 particles…");
  const [audioBands, setAudioBands] = useState<AudioBands>({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });

  const [commandText, setCommandText] = useState("");
  const [currentPrompt, setCurrentPrompt] = useState("");
  const [currentResponse, setCurrentResponse] = useState(
    'Captain, Option B + 5 Upgrades ready hain! Neeche Sphere / DNA Helix / Plasma Torus / Shield Cube morph test kijiye, aur upar "Windows Python (.py)" se poora Desktop ZIP download kijiye.'
  );
  const [sources, setSources] = useState<SourceCitation[]>([]);
  const [actionLink, setActionLink] = useState<ActionLink | null>(null);
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<string[]>([
    "Namaste DarkShadow",
    "Delhi weather today",
    "/help",
  ]);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<ChatTurn[]>([]);

  const [voiceEnabled, setVoiceEnabled] = useState(true);
  const [reducedMotion, setReducedMotion] = useState(false);
  const [privacyMode, setPrivacyMode] = useState(false);
  const [sttLanguage, setSttLanguage] = useState<"hi-IN" | "en-IN">("hi-IN");

  const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [remindersOpen, setRemindersOpen] = useState(false);
  const [desktopModalOpen, setDesktopModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [runtimeStatus, setRuntimeStatus] = useState<RuntimeStatus | null>(null);
  const [reminders, setReminders] = useState<ReminderItem[]>([]);
  const [memories, setMemories] = useState<MemoryItem[]>([]);
  const [copiedNotice, setCopiedNotice] = useState(false);

  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const returnTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const audioElRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null);
  const speechAnimRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<BrowserSpeechRecognition | null>(null);
  const micStreamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const micMeterRafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const isListeningRef = useRef(false);
  const voiceEnabledRef = useRef(voiceEnabled);
  const phaseRef = useRef<ParticlePhase>(particlePhase);
  voiceEnabledRef.current = voiceEnabled;
  phaseRef.current = particlePhase;

  const setHudState = useCallback((next: AssistantState, customMessage?: string) => {
    if (returnTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(returnTimerRef.current);
      returnTimerRef.current = null;
    }
    setAssistantState(next);
    setStatusMessage(customMessage?.trim() || STATE_TEXT[next]);

    if (next === "listening") {
      setParticlePhase("listening");
      setParticleShape("sphere");
    } else if (next === "processing") {
      setParticlePhase("processing");
      setParticleShape("helix");
    } else if (next === "speaking") {
      setParticlePhase("speaking");
      setParticleShape("torus");
    } else if (next === "error") {
      setParticlePhase("alert");
      setParticleShape("shield");
    } else if (next === "idle" || next === "success") {
      setParticlePhase((prev) =>
        prev === "dormant" || prev === "dissolving" || prev === "rising" ? prev : "listening"
      );
      setParticleShape("sphere");
    }

    if (next === "success") {
      returnTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        setAssistantState("idle");
        setStatusMessage(STATE_TEXT.idle);
      }, 1800);
    } else if (next === "error") {
      returnTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        setAssistantState("idle");
        setStatusMessage(STATE_TEXT.idle);
        setParticlePhase("listening");
        setParticleShape("sphere");
      }, 4200);
    }
  }, []);

  const stopSpeech = useCallback(() => {
    if (speechAnimRef.current !== null) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(speechAnimRef.current);
      speechAnimRef.current = null;
    }
    if (audioElRef.current) {
      audioElRef.current.pause();
      audioElRef.current.src = "";
      audioElRef.current = null;
    }
    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && "speechSynthesis" in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
    setAudioBands({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });
    setAssistantState((prev) => {
      if (prev === "speaking") {
        setStatusMessage(STATE_TEXT.idle);
        setParticlePhase((p) => (p === "speaking" ? "listening" : p));
        setParticleShape("sphere");
        return "idle";
      }
      return prev;
    });
  }, []);

  const stopMicMeter = useCallback(() => {
    if (micMeterRafRef.current !== null) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(micMeterRafRef.current);
      micMeterRafRef.current = null;
    }
    if (micStreamRef.current) {
      micStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
      micStreamRef.current = null;
    }
    setAudioBands({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });
  }, []);

  const summonParticles = useCallback(() => {
    stopSpeech();
    setParticleShape("sphere");
    setParticlePhase("rising");
    setAssistantState("idle");
    setStatusMessage("Hey Dark Shadow — Forming sphere…");
  }, [stopSpeech]);

  const dismissParticles = useCallback(() => {
    stopSpeech();
    if (isListeningRef.current) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      isListeningRef.current = false;
      stopMicMeter();
    }
    setParticlePhase("dissolving");
    setAssistantState("idle");
    setStatusMessage("Dissolving to standby…");
  }, [stopMicMeter, stopSpeech]);

  const speakWithBrowserFallback = useCallback(
    (text: string) => {
      if (typeof window === "undefined" || !("speechSynthesis" in window)) {
        setHudState("success");
        return;
      }
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      const hasDevanagari = /[\u0900-\u097F]/.test(text);
      const targetLang = hasDevanagari ? "hi-IN" : sttLanguage;
      utterance.lang = targetLang;
      utterance.rate = 1.02;
      utterance.pitch = 0.92;

      const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
      const match =
        voices.find((v) => v.lang.toLowerCase() === targetLang.toLowerCase()) ||
        voices.find((v) => v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(hasDevanagari ? "hi" : "en"));
      if (match) utterance.voice = match;

      let t = 0;
      const pulse = () => {
        t += 0.16;
        setAudioBands({
          bass: 0.25 + 0.62 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 1.1) * Math.cos(t * 0.45)),
          mid: 0.3 + 0.58 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 1.9)),
          treble: 0.2 + 0.55 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 3.1) * Math.cos(t * 1.3)),
        });
        speechAnimRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(pulse);
      };

      utterance.onstart = () => {
        setHudState("speaking");
        pulse();
      };
      utterance.onend = () => {
        if (speechAnimRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(speechAnimRef.current);
        speechAnimRef.current = null;
        setAudioBands({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });
        setHudState("idle");
      };
      utterance.onerror = () => {
        if (speechAnimRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(speechAnimRef.current);
        speechAnimRef.current = null;
        setAudioBands({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });
        setHudState("idle");
      };
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    },
    [setHudState, sttLanguage]
  );

  const speakResponse = useCallback(
    async (speechText: string) => {
      if (!voiceEnabledRef.current || !speechText.trim()) return;
      stopSpeech();
      setHudState("speaking");

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/tts", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text: speechText }),
        });
        if (!res.ok) {
          speakWithBrowserFallback(speechText);
          return;
        }
        const data = (await res.json()) as { audioBase64?: string; mimeType?: string };
        if (!data.audioBase64) {
          speakWithBrowserFallback(speechText);
          return;
        }

        const audio = new Audio(`data:${data.mimeType || "audio/wav"};base64,${data.audioBase64}`);
        audioElRef.current = audio;

        let t = 0;
        const animateSpeech = () => {
          if (!audioElRef.current || audioElRef.current.paused) return;
          t += 0.2;
          setAudioBands({
            bass: 0.28 + 0.64 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 1.0) * Math.cos(t * 0.4)),
            mid: 0.32 + 0.58 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 1.85)),
            treble: 0.22 + 0.52 * Math.abs(Math.sin(t * 3.0) * Math.cos(t * 1.2)),
          });
          speechAnimRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animateSpeech);
        };

        audio.onplay = () => animateSpeech();
        audio.onended = () => {
          if (speechAnimRef.current !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(speechAnimRef.current);
          speechAnimRef.current = null;
          audioElRef.current = null;
          setAudioBands({ bass: 0, mid: 0, treble: 0 });
          setHudState("idle");
        };
        audio.onerror = () => speakWithBrowserFallback(speechText);

        await audio.play();
      } catch {
        speakWithBrowserFallback(speechText);
      }
    },
    [setHudState, speakWithBrowserFallback, stopSpeech]
  );

  const refreshStatusAndStores = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [statusRes, remRes, memRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch("/api/status"),
        fetch("/api/reminders"),
        fetch("/api/memories"),
      ]);
      if (statusRes.ok) setRuntimeStatus((await statusRes.json()) as RuntimeStatus);
      if (remRes.ok) {
        const remData = (await remRes.json()) as {
          pending: ReminderItem[];
          due: ReminderItem[];
        };
        setReminders(remData.pending ?? []);
        if (remData.due && remData.due.length > 0) {
          const msg = remData.due.map((r) => `Captain, Reminder: ${r.message}`).join("\n");
          setParticleShape("shield");
          setParticlePhase(phaseRef.current === "dormant" ? "rising" : "alert");
          setCurrentResponse(msg);
          setStatusMessage("Sentinel • Reminder Due");
          void speakResponse(msg);
        }
      }
      if (memRes.ok) {
        const memData = (await memRes.json()) as { items: MemoryItem[] };
        setMemories(memData.items ?? []);
      }
    } catch {
      // Retry next poll
    }
  }, [speakResponse]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void refreshStatusAndStores();
    const id = window.setInterval(() => void refreshStatusAndStores(), 10000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [refreshStatusAndStores]);

  const submitCommand = useCallback(
    async (rawText: string) => {
      const cleaned = rawText.trim();
      if (!cleaned) return;

      if (WAKE_REGEX.test(cleaned)) {
        const rest = cleaned.replace(WAKE_REGEX, "").replace(/^[,.\-?!\s]+/, "").trim();
        summonParticles();
        setCommandText("");
        setCurrentPrompt(cleaned);
        if (!rest) {
          const greet = "Haanji Captain! DarkShadow active hai, bataiye kya hukum hai?";
          setCurrentResponse(greet);
          window.setTimeout(() => void speakResponse(greet), 1100);
          return;
        }
      }

      if (SLEEP_REGEX.test(cleaned)) {
        setCommandText("");
        setCurrentPrompt(cleaned);
        setCurrentResponse("Theek hai Captain, standby par ja raha hoon.");
        dismissParticles();
        return;
      }

      if (phaseRef.current === "dormant") setParticlePhase("rising");

      stopSpeech();
      setCommandText("");
      setCurrentPrompt(cleaned);
      setSources([]);
      setActionLink(null);
      setSuggestions([]);
      setHudState("processing", "Thinking • DNA Helix");

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/chat", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ message: cleaned, privacyMode }),
        });
        const data = (await res.json()) as ChatApiResponse;

        setCurrentResponse(data.displayText || "Captain, response empty mila.");
        setSources(data.sources ?? []);
        setActionLink(data.actionLink ?? null);
        setSuggestions(data.suggestions ?? []);

        if (data.clearHistory) {
          setHistory([]);
        } else {
          setHistory((prev) => [
            {
              id: `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
              prompt: cleaned,
              response: data.displayText,
              intent: data.intent,
              sources: data.sources ?? [],
              actionLink: data.actionLink,
              modelUsed: data.modelUsed,
              timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString("en-IN", {
                hour: "2-digit",
                minute: "2-digit",
              }),
            },
            ...prev.slice(0, 15),
          ]);
        }

        if (data.reminders) setReminders(data.reminders);

        if (!res.ok || data.error) {
          setHudState("error", "Shield • Alert");
        } else if (voiceEnabledRef.current && data.speechText) {
          void speakResponse(data.speechText);
        } else {
          setHudState("success");
        }

        void refreshStatusAndStores();
      } catch {
        setCurrentResponse(
          "Captain, DarkShadow server se connection nahi ban paya. Kripya dobara try kijiye."
        );
        setHudState("error", "Connection issue");
      }
    },
    [
      dismissParticles,
      privacyMode,
      refreshStatusAndStores,
      setHudState,
      speakResponse,
      stopSpeech,
      summonParticles,
    ]
  );

  const toggleListening = useCallback(async () => {
    if (phaseRef.current === "dormant") summonParticles();

    if (isListeningRef.current) {
      recognitionRef.current?.stop();
      isListeningRef.current = false;
      stopMicMeter();
      setHudState("idle");
      return;
    }

    stopSpeech();

    const SpeechRecCtor = window.SpeechRecognition || window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
    if (!SpeechRecCtor) {
      setCurrentResponse(
        "Captain, is browser mein Web Speech Recognition available nahi hai. Typed mode 100% active hai — niche command type kijiye."
      );
      setHudState("error", "Mic unsupported");
      inputRef.current?.focus();
      return;
    }

    // Real-Time 3-Band FFT Microphone Metering (Bass / Mid / Treble)
    try {
      if (navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
        const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
        micStreamRef.current = stream;
        const audioCtx = new AudioContext();
        const source = audioCtx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
        const analyser = audioCtx.createAnalyser();
        analyser.fftSize = 256;
        source.connect(analyser);
        const bins = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);
        const updateFFT = () => {
          if (!isListeningRef.current) {
            void audioCtx.close();
            return;
          }
          analyser.getByteFrequencyData(bins);
          let bSum = 0, mSum = 0, tSum = 0;
          for (let i = 1; i < 8; i++) bSum += bins[i];
          for (let i = 8; i < 36; i++) mSum += bins[i];
          for (let i = 36; i < 96; i++) tSum += bins[i];
          setAudioBands({
            bass: Math.min(1, bSum / (7 * 150)),
            mid: Math.min(1, mSum / (28 * 130)),
            treble: Math.min(1, tSum / (60 * 95)),
          });
          micMeterRafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(updateFFT);
        };
        micMeterRafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(updateFFT);
      }
    } catch {
      // Proceed even if analyser fails
    }

    const recognition = new SpeechRecCtor();
    recognition.lang = sttLanguage;
    recognition.interimResults = true;
    recognition.maxAlternatives = 1;
    recognition.continuous = false;

    let finalTranscript = "";
    isListeningRef.current = true;
    recognitionRef.current = recognition;
    setHudState("listening", "Listening • 3-Band FFT Sphere");

    recognition.onresult = (event) => {
      let interim = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < event.results.length; i++) {
        const result = event.results[i];
        const text = result[0]?.transcript ?? "";
        if (result.isFinal) finalTranscript += text;
        else interim += text;
      }
      const preview = (finalTranscript || interim).trim();
      if (preview) setCurrentPrompt(preview);
    };

    recognition.onerror = (ev) => {
      isListeningRef.current = false;
      stopMicMeter();
      if (ev.error === "not-allowed") {
        setCurrentResponse(
          "Captain, microphone permission deny hui hai. Browser permissions allow kijiye ya typed mode use kijiye."
        );
        setHudState("error", "Mic blocked");
      } else if (ev.error === "no-speech") {
        setHudState("idle", "No speech detected");
      } else {
        setHudState("error", "Voice capture issue");
      }
    };

    recognition.onend = () => {
      isListeningRef.current = false;
      stopMicMeter();
      const spoken = finalTranscript.trim();
      if (spoken) {
        void submitCommand(spoken);
      } else {
        setAssistantState((prev) => (prev === "listening" ? "idle" : prev));
        setStatusMessage((prev) => (prev.startsWith("Listening") ? STATE_TEXT.idle : prev));
      }
    };

    try {
      recognition.start();
    } catch {
      isListeningRef.current = false;
      stopMicMeter();
      setHudState("error", "Mic start failed");
    }
  }, [setHudState, stopMicMeter, stopSpeech, sttLanguage, submitCommand, summonParticles]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      const isInputFocused =
        target?.tagName === "INPUT" ||
        target?.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
        target?.tagName === "SELECT";

      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "l") {
        e.preventDefault();
        inputRef.current?.focus();
        return;
      }

      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        if (desktopModalOpen) setDesktopModalOpen(false);
        else if (settingsOpen) setSettingsOpen(false);
        else if (remindersOpen) setRemindersOpen(false);
        else if (assistantState === "speaking") stopSpeech();
        else if (isListeningRef.current) void toggleListening();
        else if (phaseRef.current !== "dormant") dismissParticles();
        return;
      }

      if (isInputFocused || settingsOpen || remindersOpen || desktopModalOpen) return;

      if (e.code === "Space") {
        e.preventDefault();
        void toggleListening();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
  }, [
    assistantState,
    desktopModalOpen,
    dismissParticles,
    remindersOpen,
    settingsOpen,
    stopSpeech,
    toggleListening,
  ]);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    if (!currentResponse.trim()) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(currentResponse);
      setCopiedNotice(true);
      setStatusMessage("Copied to clipboard");
      window.setTimeout(() => {
        setCopiedNotice(false);
        setStatusMessage(STATE_TEXT[assistantState]);
      }, 1600);
    } catch {
      // Ignore clipboard failures
    }
  };

  const handleClearChat = async () => {
    stopSpeech();
    await fetch("/api/clear", { method: "POST" });
    setHistory([]);
    setCurrentPrompt("");
    setSources([]);
    setActionLink(null);
    setCurrentResponse("Captain, in-memory conversation context clear kar diya hai.");
    setHudState("idle");
    void refreshStatusAndStores();
  };

  const handleDeleteReminder = async (id: number) => {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/reminders/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = (await res.json()) as { pending: ReminderItem[] };
      setReminders(data.pending ?? []);
      void refreshStatusAndStores();
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteMemory = async (id: number) => {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/memories/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = (await res.json()) as { items: MemoryItem[] };
      setMemories(data.items ?? []);
      void refreshStatusAndStores();
    }
  };

  const combinedAudioLevel = Math.min(1, audioBands.bass * 0.4 + audioBands.mid * 0.4 + audioBands.treble * 0.3);

  return (
    <div className="relative h-screen w-screen bg-[#000000] text-[#F8F9FD] flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
      {/* Full-Viewport GPU WebGL2 6,000-Particle Layer (Option B Exact Engine) */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
        <ParticleOrbWebGL
          phase={particlePhase}
          shape={particleShape}
          audioBands={audioBands}
          reducedMotion={reducedMotion}
          onRiseComplete={() => {
            setParticlePhase("listening");
            setStatusMessage("Ready • 6,000 WebGL2 Particles");
          }}
          onDissolveComplete={() => {
            setParticlePhase("dormant");
            setStatusMessage('Standby — Say "Hey Dark Shadow" or Summon');
          }}
          onClick={() => {
            if (particlePhase === "dormant") summonParticles();
            else void toggleListening();
          }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Top Custom TitleBar */}
      <TitleBar
        pendingReminders={reminders.length}
        privacyMode={privacyMode}
        onOpenReminders={() => {
          void refreshStatusAndStores();
          setRemindersOpen(true);
        }}
        onOpenSettings={() => {
          void refreshStatusAndStores();
          setSettingsOpen(true);
        }}
        onOpenDesktopDownload={() => setDesktopModalOpen(true)}
      />

      {/* 3-Column Panoramic Cyber HUD */}
      <main className="relative z-10 pointer-events-none flex-1 min-h-0 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[3fr_4fr_3fr] gap-4 lg:gap-6 px-5 sm:px-8 py-4 overflow-y-auto lg:overflow-hidden">
        {/* Left Column: Captain's Prompt */}
        <section className="flex flex-col min-h-0 py-2 pointer-events-auto">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[1.5px] uppercase text-[#80D8FF] mb-3 shrink-0">
            <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Captain&apos;s Prompt</span>
          </div>

          <div className="text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-[#80D8FF] whitespace-pre-wrap break-words min-h-[48px] shrink-0">
            {currentPrompt || (
              <span className="text-[#80D8FF]/45 italic text-[13px]">
                Say &ldquo;Hey Dark Shadow&rdquo; or morph shapes below…
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          {history.length > 1 && (
            <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-[#181E2C]/60 flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 pr-1">
              <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[1.4px] text-[#8A90A4]/80 font-semibold">
                Recent Session
              </div>
              {history.slice(1, 8).map((turn) => (
                <button
                  key={turn.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setCurrentPrompt(turn.prompt);
                    setCurrentResponse(turn.response);
                    setSources(turn.sources);
                    setActionLink(turn.actionLink ?? null);
                  }}
                  className="w-full text-left group rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 hover:bg-[#070A10]/80 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-[#80D8FF]/75 group-hover:text-[#80D8FF] truncate">
                    <span className="truncate">{turn.prompt}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-[#8A90A4] shrink-0">
                      {turn.timestamp}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] truncate mt-0.5">
                    {turn.response}
                  </div>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* Center Column: Controls floating beneath the 3D Morphing Particle Sphere */}
        <section className="flex flex-col items-center justify-end pb-2 shrink-0 min-h-[260px]">
          <div className="pointer-events-auto flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div
              className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold tracking-[1px] min-h-[22px] transition-colors duration-200 text-center"
              style={{ color: STATE_LABEL_COLOR[assistantState] }}
            >
              {statusMessage}
            </div>

            <AudioWaveform
              state={assistantState}
              audioLevel={combinedAudioLevel}
              reducedMotion={reducedMotion}
            />

            {/* Upgrade #5: Live 3D Shape Morphing Selector */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
              {SHAPE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <button
                  key={opt.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (particlePhase === "dormant") setParticlePhase("rising");
                    setParticleShape(opt.id);
                    setStatusMessage(`Morphed • ${opt.label}`);
                  }}
                  className={`h-[26px] px-2.5 rounded-md text-[10.5px] font-medium border transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                    particleShape === opt.id && particlePhase !== "dormant"
                      ? "bg-[#765CFF]/25 border-[#765CFF] text-white"
                      : "bg-[#070A10]/80 border-[#181E2C] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white hover:border-[#765CFF]/60"
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Summon / Sleep Controls */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={summonParticles}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 h-[30px] px-3.5 rounded-full bg-[#070A10]/90 border border-[#00D2FF]/50 hover:border-[#00D2FF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] text-[11px] font-medium text-[#80D8FF] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Summon (&ldquo;Hey Dark Shadow&rdquo;)</span>
              </button>

              {particlePhase !== "dormant" && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={dismissParticles}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 h-[30px] px-3 rounded-full bg-[#070A10]/90 border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Moon className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Sleep</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* Right Column: DarkShadow's Response & Actions */}
        <section className="flex flex-col min-h-0 py-2 pointer-events-auto">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 shrink-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-[1.5px] uppercase text-[#FFFFFF]">
              <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00D2FF]" />
              <span>DarkShadow AI</span>
            </div>
            {history[0]?.modelUsed && (
              <span className="text-[10.5px] text-[#8A90A4] font-mono">
                {history[0].modelUsed}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-3">
            <div className="text-[14px] leading-[1.55] text-[#FFFFFF] whitespace-pre-wrap break-words select-text">
              {currentResponse}
            </div>

            {sources.length > 0 && (
              <div className="pt-2 space-y-1.5">
                <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#80D8FF]">
                  Live • {sources.length} verified{" "}
                  {sources.length === 1 ? "source" : "sources"}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {sources.map((src) => (
                    <a
                      key={src.url}
                      href={src.url}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#00D2FF] transition-colors truncate"
                    >
                      <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                      <span className="truncate">{src.title}</span>
                    </a>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {actionLink && (
              <div className="pt-1">
                <a
                  href={actionLink.url}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#070A10]/90 border border-[#765CFF]/60 hover:border-[#00D2FF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] text-[12px] font-medium text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors"
                >
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00D2FF]" />
                  <span>{actionLink.label}</span>
                </a>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="pt-3 mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            {assistantState === "speaking" ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={stopSpeech}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 h-[32px] px-4 rounded-full bg-[#070A10] border border-[#5A2630] hover:bg-[#3A1720] hover:border-[#FF4560] text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#FF4560] hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Square className="w-2.5 h-2.5 fill-current" />
                <span>Stop speaking</span>
              </button>
            ) : (
              <>
                {suggestions.slice(0, 3).map((item) => (
                  <button
                    key={item}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void submitCommand(item)}
                    className="h-[30px] px-3 rounded-full bg-[#070A10]/90 border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {item}
                  </button>
                ))}

                {currentResponse.trim() && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void handleCopy()}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 h-[30px] px-3 rounded-full bg-black/50 border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Copy className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>{copiedNotice ? "Copied" : "Copy"}</span>
                  </button>
                )}

                {currentPrompt.trim() && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => void submitCommand(currentPrompt)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 h-[30px] px-3 rounded-full bg-black/50 border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Regenerate</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>

      {/* Bottom Command Input */}
      <footer className="relative z-10 shrink-0 px-4 pb-5 pt-2 flex justify-center">
        <form
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            void submitCommand(commandText);
          }}
          className="w-full max-w-[620px] h-[54px] flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 rounded-[17px] bg-[#0B0F19]/90 border border-[#181E2C] focus-within:border-[#765CFF]/80 backdrop-blur-xs transition-colors"
        >
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            type="text"
            value={commandText}
            onChange={(e) => setCommandText(e.target.value)}
            placeholder='Type a command or "Hey Dark Shadow"…'
            aria-label="Command"
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent border-none text-[13.5px] text-[#F8F9FD] placeholder:text-[#8A90A4]/75 focus:outline-none px-1"
          />

          {commandText.trim() && (
            <button
              type="submit"
              aria-label="Send command"
              className="flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-full text-[#00D2FF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void toggleListening()}
            aria-label="Use microphone"
            title="Use microphone (Space)"
            className={`flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-10 rounded-full transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              assistantState === "listening"
                ? "bg-[#765CFF]/25 text-[#00D2FF]"
                : "text-[#A9B0C5] hover:bg-[#0F1422] hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {assistantState === "listening" ? (
              <MicOff className="w-[18px] h-[18px]" />
            ) : (
              <Mic className="w-[18px] h-[18px]" />
            )}
          </button>
        </form>
      </footer>

      <DesktopExportModal
        open={desktopModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setDesktopModalOpen(false)}
      />

      <ControlCenterModal
        open={settingsOpen}
        voiceEnabled={voiceEnabled}
        reducedMotion={reducedMotion}
        privacyMode={privacyMode}
        sttLanguage={sttLanguage}
        status={runtimeStatus}
        onClose={() => setSettingsOpen(false)}
        onApply={(prefs) => {
          setVoiceEnabled(prefs.voiceEnabled);
          setReducedMotion(prefs.reducedMotion);
          setPrivacyMode(prefs.privacyMode);
          setSttLanguage(prefs.sttLanguage);
          if (!prefs.voiceEnabled) stopSpeech();
        }}
        onClearChat={() => void handleClearChat()}
      />

      <RemindersModal
        open={remindersOpen}
        reminders={reminders}
        memories={memories}
        privacyMode={privacyMode}
        onClose={() => setRemindersOpen(false)}
        onDeleteReminder={(id) => void handleDeleteReminder(id)}
        onDeleteMemory={(id) => void handleDeleteMemory(id)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

  listening: "Listening…",
  processing: "Thinking…",
  speaking: "Speaking…",
  success: "Done",
  error: "Something went wrong",
};

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { STATE_PALETTES } from "../types.ts";
import type { AssistantState } from "../types.ts";

interface AssistantOrbProps {
  state: AssistantState;
  audioLevel: number;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  onClick: () => void;
}

interface RGB {
  r: number;
  g: number;
  b: number;
}

function hexToRgb(hex: string): RGB {
  const clean = hex.replace("#", "");
  return {
    r: Number.parseInt(clean.slice(0, 2), 16),
    g: Number.parseInt(clean.slice(2, 4), 16),
    b: Number.parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16),
  };
}

function mixRgb(a: RGB, b: RGB, t: number): RGB {
  const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
  return {
    r: Math.round(a.r + (b.r - a.r) * clamped),
    g: Math.round(a.g + (b.g - a.g) * clamped),
    b: Math.round(a.b + (b.b - a.b) * clamped),
  };
}

function rgba(c: RGB, alpha: number): string {
  return `rgba(${c.r}, ${c.g}, ${c.b}, ${alpha.toFixed(3)})`;
}

function lighten(c: RGB, factor: number): RGB {
  return {
    r: Math.min(255, Math.round(c.r * factor)),
    g: Math.min(255, Math.round(c.g * factor)),
    b: Math.min(255, Math.round(c.b * factor)),
  };
}

/**
 * Single-orb hybrid canvas + fluid GIF component ported directly from ui.py's AssistantOrb.
 * Uses DarkShadow's original /assets/darkshadow_core.gif material wrapped with state-aware
 * radial bloom, 76 procedural orbital pin-points, conical ring, processing arc, and glowing eyes.
 */
export function AssistantOrb({
  state,
  audioLevel,
  reducedMotion,
  onClick,
}: AssistantOrbProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const stateRef = useRef(state);
  const audioRef = useRef(audioLevel);
  const reducedRef = useRef(reducedMotion);

  stateRef.current = state;
  audioRef.current = audioLevel;
  reducedRef.current = reducedMotion;

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    let rafId = 0;
    let phase = 0;
    let rotation = 0;
    let displayAudio = 0;
    let blinkTimer = 3.2;
    let blinkProgress = 0;
    let lastTime = performance.now();

    const currentPalette = STATE_PALETTES[stateRef.current].map(hexToRgb);

    const draw = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
      lastTime = now;

      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const size = canvas.clientWidth || 300;
      const targetPx = Math.round(size * dpr);
      if (canvas.width !== targetPx || canvas.height !== targetPx) {
        canvas.width = targetPx;
        canvas.height = targetPx;
      }

      ctx.save();
      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, size, size);

      const st = stateRef.current;
      const reduced = reducedRef.current;
      const targetAudio =
        st === "listening" || st === "speaking" ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, audioRef.current)) : 0;
      displayAudio += (targetAudio - displayAudio) * 0.22;

      const speed =
        st === "processing"
          ? 2.6
          : st === "listening" || st === "speaking"
            ? 1.7 + displayAudio * 1.4
            : 0.9;
      phase += dt * speed * (reduced ? 0.25 : 1);
      rotation += dt * (st === "processing" ? 135 : 32) * (reduced ? 0.2 : 1);

      // Smooth palette interpolation
      const targetPalette = STATE_PALETTES[st].map(hexToRgb);
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        currentPalette[i] = mixRgb(currentPalette[i], targetPalette[i], 0.12);
      }
      const [primary, , accent] = currentPalette;

      // Natural eye blink cadence
      blinkTimer -= dt;
      if (blinkTimer <= 0) {
        blinkProgress += dt * 9.5;
        if (blinkProgress >= Math.PI) {
          blinkProgress = 0;
          blinkTimer = 2.6 + Math.random() * 3.4;
        }
      }
      const blink = blinkProgress > 0 ? Math.max(0.08, 1 - Math.sin(blinkProgress)) : 1;

      const cx = size / 2;
      const cy = size / 2;
      const visualRadius = size * 0.345 * (1 + 0.015 * Math.sin(phase * 1.4) + displayAudio * 0.055);

      if (st === "error" && !reduced) {
        ctx.translate(Math.sin(phase * 16) * 2.8, 0);
      }

      // 1. State radial bloom
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "screen";
      const wash = mixRgb(primary, accent, 0.28);
      const haloGrad = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, visualRadius * 1.48);
      haloGrad.addColorStop(0, rgba(wash, 0.04));
      haloGrad.addColorStop(0.65, rgba(wash, 0.14 + displayAudio * 0.12));
      haloGrad.addColorStop(1, rgba(wash, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = haloGrad;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, visualRadius * 1.48, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // 2. 76 procedural orbital pin-points (matching ui.py)
      for (let idx = 0; idx < 76; idx++) {
        const angle = (Math.PI * 2 * idx) / 76 + phase * 0.12;
        const spread = 1.12 + 0.25 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(idx * 2.17 + phase * 0.75));
        const dotR = visualRadius * spread;
        const dotColor = mixRgb(primary, accent, (idx % 7) / 7);
        const alpha = (24 + ((idx * 19) % 58)) / 255;
        const dotSize = 0.65 + (idx % 3) * 0.35;
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(dotColor, alpha);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx + Math.cos(angle) * dotR, cy + Math.sin(angle) * dotR, dotSize, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      // 3. Outer glowing ring
      const rotRad = (-rotation * Math.PI) / 180;
      const ringGrad =
        typeof ctx.createConicGradient === "function"
          ? ctx.createConicGradient(rotRad, cx, cy)
          : ctx.createLinearGradient(cx - visualRadius, cy - visualRadius, cx + visualRadius, cy + visualRadius);

      ringGrad.addColorStop(0, rgba(lighten(accent, 1.35), 0.95));
      ringGrad.addColorStop(0.3, rgba(primary, 0.92));
      ringGrad.addColorStop(0.62, "rgba(197, 154, 255, 0.92)");
      ringGrad.addColorStop(1, rgba(lighten(accent, 1.35), 0.95));

      ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(primary, 0.18 + displayAudio * 0.22);
      ctx.lineWidth = 9 + displayAudio * 6;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, visualRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.strokeStyle = ringGrad;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2.6 + displayAudio * 1.4;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, visualRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();

      // 4. Spinning processing arc
      if (st === "processing" && !reduced) {
        const arcR = visualRadius * 1.07;
        const startAngle = (rotation * Math.PI) / 180;
        ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(lighten(accent, 1.45), 0.92);
        ctx.lineWidth = 3.2;
        ctx.lineCap = "round";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, arcR, startAngle, startAngle + (76 * Math.PI) / 180);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();

      // 5. Two expressive glowing eyes (matching ui.py)
      const gazeX = reduced ? 0 : Math.sin(phase * 0.55) * visualRadius * 0.032;
      const gazeY = reduced ? 0 : Math.cos(phase * 0.42) * visualRadius * 0.018;
      const eyeY = cy + visualRadius * 0.015 + gazeY;
      const eyeOffset = visualRadius * 0.165;
      const eyeWidth = visualRadius * (0.042 + displayAudio * 0.008);
      const eyeHeight = Math.max(
        visualRadius * 0.008,
        visualRadius * (0.068 + displayAudio * 0.01) * blink
      );

      for (const eyeX of [cx - eyeOffset + gazeX, cx + eyeOffset + gazeX]) {
        const eyeGlow = ctx.createRadialGradient(eyeX, eyeY, 0, eyeX, eyeY, eyeHeight * 2.1);
        eyeGlow.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)");
        eyeGlow.addColorStop(0.45, "rgba(192, 220, 255, 0.42)");
        eyeGlow.addColorStop(1, "rgba(95, 125, 255, 0)");
        ctx.fillStyle = eyeGlow;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(eyeX, eyeY, eyeHeight * 2.1, Math.max(1.5, eyeHeight * 2.1), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(245, 248, 255, 0.95)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(eyeX, eyeY, eyeWidth, eyeHeight, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      ctx.restore();
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
  }, []);

  const gifScale =
    state === "listening" || state === "speaking"
      ? 1 + audioLevel * 0.06
      : state === "success"
        ? 1.03
        : 1;

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label="Activate DarkShadow voice assistant"
      title="Click or press Space to speak"
      className="group relative flex items-center justify-center w-[260px] h-[260px] sm:w-[300px] sm:h-[300px] cursor-pointer focus:outline-none select-none"
    >
      {/* Core Fluid GIF clipped cleanly to the inner dark glass sphere */}
      <div
        className="absolute rounded-full overflow-hidden transition-transform duration-150 ease-out"
        style={{
          width: "69%",
          height: "69%",
          transform: `scale(${gifScale})`,
          boxShadow: "inset 0 0 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.72)",
        }}
      >
        <img
          src="/assets/darkshadow_core.gif"
          alt="DarkShadow Core Orb"
          draggable={false}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover opacity-90"
          style={{
            filter:
              state === "error"
                ? "hue-rotate(140deg) saturate(1.4)"
                : state === "processing"
                  ? "hue-rotate(42deg) saturate(1.25)"
                  : state === "speaking"
                    ? "saturate(1.25) brightness(1.08)"
                    : "none",
          }}
        />
        {/* Subtle dark center vignette so the glowing eyes read with crisp contrast */}
        <div
          className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
          style={{
            background:
              "radial-gradient(circle at center, rgba(4, 6, 18, 0.62) 0%, rgba(4, 6, 18, 0.25) 48%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08) 100%)",
          }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* State-aware Canvas Overlay: bloom, particles, rotating ring, arc & blinking eyes */}
      <canvas
        ref={canvasRef}
        className="relative z-10 w-full h-full pointer-events-none"
      />
    </button>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { AssistantState } from "../types.ts";

interface AudioWaveformProps {
  state: AssistantState;
  audioLevel: number;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
}

/**
 * 25-bar state-aware audio waveform ported from ui.py's AudioWaveform.
 * Visible only during listening and speaking states.
 */
export function AudioWaveform({
  state,
  audioLevel,
  reducedMotion,
}: AudioWaveformProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const stateRef = useRef(state);
  const audioRef = useRef(audioLevel);
  const reducedRef = useRef(reducedMotion);

  stateRef.current = state;
  audioRef.current = audioLevel;
  reducedRef.current = reducedMotion;

  const active = state === "listening" || state === "speaking";

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;

    let rafId = 0;
    let phase = 0;
    let display = 0;

    const draw = () => {
      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      const width = 250;
      const height = 46;
      if (canvas.width !== width * dpr || canvas.height !== height * dpr) {
        canvas.width = width * dpr;
        canvas.height = height * dpr;
      }

      ctx.save();
      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      display += (audioRef.current - display) * 0.24;
      phase += reducedRef.current ? 0.05 : 0.16;

      ctx.strokeStyle = stateRef.current === "speaking" ? "#00D2FF" : "#765CFF";
      ctx.lineWidth = 3.2;
      ctx.lineCap = "round";

      const count = 25;
      const centerY = height / 2;
      const spacing = width / (count + 1);

      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const distance = Math.abs(i - 12) / 12;
        const envelope = Math.pow(1 - distance, 1.55);
        const wave = 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.pow(Math.sin(phase + i * 0.74), 2);
        const barH = 3 + envelope * (9 + display * 22) * wave;
        const x = spacing * (i + 1);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(x, centerY - barH / 2);
        ctx.lineTo(x, centerY + barH / 2);
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      ctx.restore();
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(draw);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
  }, [active]);

  return (
    <div
      className={`h-[46px] flex items-center justify-center transition-opacity duration-200 ${
        active ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0 pointer-events-none"
      }`}
    >
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="w-[250px] h-[46px]" />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { RuntimeStatus } from "../types.ts";

interface ControlCenterModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  voiceEnabled: boolean;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  privacyMode: boolean;
  sttLanguage: "hi-IN" | "en-IN";
  status: RuntimeStatus | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onApply: (prefs: {
    voiceEnabled: boolean;
    reducedMotion: boolean;
    privacyMode: boolean;
    sttLanguage: "hi-IN" | "en-IN";
  }) => void;
  onClearChat: () => void;
}

/**
 * Control Center modal ported from ui.py's ControlCenterDialog.
 * Never displays API keys or raw memory contents.
 */
export function ControlCenterModal({
  open,
  voiceEnabled,
  reducedMotion,
  privacyMode,
  sttLanguage,
  status,
  onClose,
  onApply,
  onClearChat,
}: ControlCenterModalProps) {
  const [localVoice, setLocalVoice] = useState(voiceEnabled);
  const [localMotion, setLocalMotion] = useState(reducedMotion);
  const [localPrivacy, setLocalPrivacy] = useState(privacyMode);
  const [localLang, setLocalLang] = useState<"hi-IN" | "en-IN">(sttLanguage);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      setLocalVoice(voiceEnabled);
      setLocalMotion(reducedMotion);
      setLocalPrivacy(privacyMode);
      setLocalLang(sttLanguage);
    }
  }, [open, voiceEnabled, reducedMotion, privacyMode, sttLanguage]);

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs p-4"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-[440px] rounded-2xl bg-[#000000] border border-[#181E2C] p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-[#F8F9FD]">
            Settings &amp; privacy
          </h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white px-2 py-1 text-lg leading-none cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Close settings"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] p-3 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-[#8A90A4] whitespace-pre-line">
          {status?.summary ?? "Loading DarkShadow runtime status…"}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[13px] text-[#F8F9FD] cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={localVoice}
              onChange={(e) => setLocalVoice(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#765CFF] rounded cursor-pointer"
            />
            <span>Speak assistant replies</span>
          </label>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[13px] text-[#F8F9FD] cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={localMotion}
              onChange={(e) => setLocalMotion(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#765CFF] rounded cursor-pointer"
            />
            <span>Reduce orb motion</span>
          </label>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[13px] text-[#F8F9FD] cursor-pointer">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={localPrivacy}
              onChange={(e) => setLocalPrivacy(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#765CFF] rounded cursor-pointer"
            />
            <span>Privacy mode (pause persistent memory access)</span>
          </label>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
            <span className="text-[12px] text-[#8A90A4]">Voice input locale</span>
            <select
              value={localLang}
              onChange={(e) => setLocalLang(e.target.value as "hi-IN" | "en-IN")}
              className="bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12px] text-[#F8F9FD] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#765CFF]"
            >
              <option value="hi-IN">Hindi / Hinglish (hi-IN)</option>
              <option value="en-IN">English India (en-IN)</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] leading-relaxed">
          Privacy mode affects this app session only. API keys and saved memory
          contents are never shown here.
        </p>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onClearChat();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] text-[12px] text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            Clear chat context
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onApply({
                voiceEnabled: localVoice,
                reducedMotion: localMotion,
                privacyMode: localPrivacy,
                sttLanguage: localLang,
              });
              onClose();
            }}
            className="px-5 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#765CFF] hover:bg-[#8670FF] text-[12px] font-semibold text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            Apply
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



======================================================================
FILE: src\components\DesktopExportModal.tsx
======================================================================
import { Download, Package, Terminal } from "lucide-react";

  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
