import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { AudioBands, ParticlePhase, ParticleShape } from "./ParticleOrbWebGL.tsx";

interface ParticleOrbNextGenProps {
  phase: ParticlePhase;
  shape: ParticleShape;
  audioBands: AudioBands;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  onRiseComplete?: () => void;
  onDissolveComplete?: () => void;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

const VERTEX_SHADER = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 aPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 aColor;
layout(location = 2) in float aSize;
layout(location = 3) in float aAlpha;

uniform float uAspect;
uniform float uDpr;
uniform float uTime;
uniform vec3 uAudio;       // bass, mid, treble
uniform vec2 uMouse;       // normalized pointer [-1, 1]
uniform float uMouseHover; // hover intensity [0, 1]

out vec3 vColor;
out float vAlpha;

// Simplex 3D noise
vec3 mod289(vec3 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
vec4 mod289(vec4 x) { return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0; }
vec4 permute(vec4 x) { return mod289(((x * 34.0) + 1.0) * x); }
vec4 taylorInvSqrt(vec4 r) { return 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * r; }

float snoise(vec3 v) {
  const vec2 C = vec2(1.0 / 6.0, 1.0 / 3.0);
  const vec4 D = vec4(0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0);
  vec3 i = floor(v + dot(v, C.yyy));
  vec3 x0 = v - i + dot(i, C.xxx);
  vec3 g = step(x0.yzx, x0.xyz);
  vec3 l = 1.0 - g;
  vec3 i1 = min(g.xyz, l.zxy);
  vec3 i2 = max(g.xyz, l.zxy);
  vec3 x1 = x0 - i1 + C.xxx;
  vec3 x2 = x0 - i2 + C.yyy;
  vec3 x3 = x0 - D.yyy;
  i = mod289(i);
  vec4 p = permute(permute(permute(
             i.z + vec4(0.0, i1.z, i2.z, 1.0))
           + i.y + vec4(0.0, i1.y, i2.y, 1.0))
           + i.x + vec4(0.0, i1.x, i2.x, 1.0));
  vec3 ns = 0.142857142857 * D.wyz - D.xzx;
  vec4 j = p - 49.0 * floor(p * ns.z * ns.z);
  vec4 x_ = floor(j * ns.z);
  vec4 y_ = floor(j - 7.0 * x_);
  vec4 x = x_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy;
  vec4 y = y_ * ns.x + ns.yyyy;
  vec4 h = 1.0 - abs(x) - abs(y);
  vec4 b0 = vec4(x.xy, y.xy);
  vec4 b1 = vec4(x.zw, y.zw);
  vec4 s0 = floor(b0) * 2.0 + 1.0;
  vec4 s1 = floor(b1) * 2.0 + 1.0;
  vec4 sh = -step(h, vec4(0.0));
  vec4 a0 = b0.xzyw + s0.xzyw * sh.xxyy;
  vec4 a1 = b1.xzyw + s1.xzyw * sh.zzww;
  vec3 p0 = vec3(a0.xy, h.x);
  vec3 p1 = vec3(a0.zw, h.y);
  vec3 p2 = vec3(a1.xy, h.z);
  vec3 p3 = vec3(a1.zw, h.w);
  vec4 norm = taylorInvSqrt(vec4(dot(p0, p0), dot(p1, p1), dot(p2, p2), dot(p3, p3)));
  p0 *= norm.x; p1 *= norm.y; p2 *= norm.z; p3 *= norm.w;
  vec4 m = max(0.6 - vec4(dot(x0, x0), dot(x1, x1), dot(x2, x2), dot(x3, x3)), 0.0);
  m = m * m;
  return 42.0 * dot(m * m, vec4(dot(p0, x0), dot(p1, x1), dot(p2, x2), dot(p3, x3)));
}

vec3 curlNoise(vec3 p) {
  const float e = 0.08;
  float dx = (snoise(p + vec3(e, 0.0, 0.0)) - snoise(p - vec3(e, 0.0, 0.0))) / (2.0 * e);
  float dy = (snoise(p + vec3(0.0, e, 0.0)) - snoise(p - vec3(0.0, e, 0.0))) / (2.0 * e);
  float dz = (snoise(p + vec3(0.0, 0.0, e)) - snoise(p - vec3(0.0, 0.0, e))) / (2.0 * e);
  return vec3(dy - dz, dz - dx, dx - dy);
}

void main() {
  vec3 pos = aPos;

  // Cosmic fluid turbulence
  vec3 curl = curlNoise(pos * 2.0 + vec3(0.0, uTime * 0.16, 0.0));
  pos += curl * (0.04 + uAudio.x * 0.07);

  // Magnetic cursor gravity
  if (uMouseHover > 0.02) {
    vec2 toM = uMouse - pos.xy;
    float dist = length(toM);
    if (dist < 0.7) {
      pos.xy += (toM / (dist + 0.2)) * 0.08 * uMouseHover;
    }
  }

  float zCam = 2.6 - pos.z;
  float persp = 1.9 / max(0.4, zCam);
  gl_Position = vec4(pos.x / uAspect * persp, pos.y * persp, 0.0, 1.0);
  gl_PointSize = clamp(aSize * persp * uDpr * (1.0 + uAudio.y * 0.45), 1.6, 32.0);

  // Chromatic Luminescence
  vColor = aColor + vec3(uAudio.z * 0.25, uAudio.y * 0.18, uAudio.x * 0.20);
  vAlpha = aAlpha * clamp(persp * 0.95, 0.35, 1.0);
}
`;

const FRAGMENT_SHADER = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

in vec3 vColor;
in float vAlpha;
out vec4 fragColor;

void main() {
  vec2 coord = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
  float distSq = dot(coord, coord);
  if (distSq > 0.25) discard;

  float core = exp(-distSq * 42.0);
  float halo = exp(-distSq * 6.8);
  vec3 col = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0), core * 0.72);
  float alpha = clamp((core * 1.5 + halo * 0.6) * vAlpha, 0.0, 1.0);
  fragColor = vec4(col * alpha, alpha);
}
`;

function createShader(gl: WebGL2RenderingContext, type: number, source: string): WebGLShader | null {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (!gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
    console.error("Shader compile error:", gl.getShaderInfoLog(shader));
    gl.deleteShader(shader);
    return null;
  }
  return shader;
}

const COUNT = 6500;

export function ParticleOrbNextGen({
  phase,
  shape,
  audioBands,
  reducedMotion,
  onRiseComplete,
  onDissolveComplete,
  onClick,
}: ParticleOrbNextGenProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const phaseRef = useRef(phase);
  const shapeRef = useRef(shape);
  const audioRef = useRef(audioBands);
  const motionRef = useRef(reducedMotion);
  const onRiseCompleteRef = useRef(onRiseComplete);
  const onDissolveCompleteRef = useRef(onDissolveComplete);

  const mousePosRef = useRef<[number, number]>([-999, -999]);
  const mouseHoverRef = useRef<number>(0);

  useEffect(() => { phaseRef.current = phase; }, [phase]);
  useEffect(() => { shapeRef.current = shape; }, [shape]);
  useEffect(() => { audioRef.current = audioBands; }, [audioBands]);
  useEffect(() => { motionRef.current = reducedMotion; }, [reducedMotion]);
  useEffect(() => { onRiseCompleteRef.current = onRiseComplete; }, [onRiseComplete]);
  useEffect(() => { onDissolveCompleteRef.current = onDissolveComplete; }, [onDissolveComplete]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const gl = canvas.getContext("webgl2", {
      alpha: true,
      antialias: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
    });

    if (!gl) return;

    const vert = createShader(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX_SHADER);
    const frag = createShader(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT_SHADER);
    if (!vert || !frag) return;

    const program = gl.createProgram();
    if (!program) return;
    gl.attachShader(program, vert);
    gl.attachShader(program, frag);
    gl.linkProgram(program);

    const uAspectLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uAspect");
    const uDprLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uDpr");
    const uTimeLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uTime");
    const uAudioLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uAudio");
    const uMouseLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uMouse");
    const uMouseHoverLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uMouseHover");

    const posX = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const posY = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const posZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const velX = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const velY = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const velZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);

    const s0X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s0Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s0Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const s1X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s1Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s1Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const s2X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s2Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s2Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const s3X = new Float32Array(COUNT), s3Y = new Float32Array(COUNT), s3Z = new Float32Array(COUNT);

    const colR = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const colG = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const colB = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const sizes = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const alphas = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const pPhase = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const vertexData = new Float32Array(COUNT * 8);

    const golden = Math.PI * (1 + Math.sqrt(5));
    const ORB_RADIUS = 0.23;

    for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
      const idx = i + 0.5;
      const s = ((Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453) % 1 + 1) % 1;
      const s2 = ((Math.sin(i * 78.233 + 1.7) * 43758.5453) % 1 + 1) % 1;
      pPhase[i] = s2 * Math.PI * 2;

      // Sphere
      const phi = Math.acos(1 - (2 * idx) / COUNT);
      const theta = golden * idx;
      const r = (i % 6 === 0) ? ORB_RADIUS * 0.90 + s * 0.025 : ORB_RADIUS + (s2 - 0.5) * 0.015;
      s0X[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta) * r;
      s0Y[i] = Math.cos(phi) * r;
      s0Z[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * r;

      // Helix
      const strand = (i % 2 === 0) ? 0 : Math.PI;
      const helixT = (i / COUNT) * Math.PI * 8.0;
      const hRad = 0.16 + (s - 0.5) * 0.03;
      s1X[i] = Math.cos(helixT + strand) * hRad;
      s1Y[i] = ((i / COUNT) - 0.5) * 0.65;
      s1Z[i] = Math.sin(helixT + strand) * hRad;

      // Torus
      const torusU = (i / COUNT) * Math.PI * 2.0;
      const torusV = ((i % 120) / 120) * Math.PI * 2.0;
      const R = 0.22, rTube = 0.07;
      s2X[i] = (R + rTube * Math.cos(torusV)) * Math.cos(torusU);
      s2Y[i] = (R + rTube * Math.cos(torusV)) * Math.sin(torusU);
      s2Z[i] = rTube * Math.sin(torusV);

      // Cube
      const sideIdx = i % 6;
      const u = (s - 0.5) * 0.38, v = (s2 - 0.5) * 0.38, d = 0.19;
      if (sideIdx === 0) { s3X[i] = d; s3Y[i] = u; s3Z[i] = v; }
      else if (sideIdx === 1) { s3X[i] = -d; s3Y[i] = u; s3Z[i] = v; }
      else if (sideIdx === 2) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = d; s3Z[i] = v; }
      else if (sideIdx === 3) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = -d; s3Z[i] = v; }
      else if (sideIdx === 4) { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = v; s3Z[i] = d; }
      else { s3X[i] = u; s3Y[i] = v; s3Z[i] = -d; }

      posX[i] = s0X[i];
      posY[i] = s0Y[i];
      posZ[i] = s0Z[i];

      colR[i] = 0.92 + s * 0.08;
      colG[i] = 0.95 + s2 * 0.05;
      colB[i] = 1.0;
      sizes[i] = 1.8 + s * 2.4;
      alphas[i] = 0.85;
    }

    const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
    const vbo = gl.createBuffer();
    gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
    gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
    gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vertexData.byteLength, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
    const stride = 32;
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0); gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 0);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1); gl.vertexAttribPointer(1, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 12);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(2); gl.vertexAttribPointer(2, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 24);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(3); gl.vertexAttribPointer(3, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 28);
    gl.bindVertexArray(null);

    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
    gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);

    let lastTime = performance.now();
    let t = 0;
    let sBass = 0, sMid = 0, sTreble = 0;
    let rafId: number;

    const animate = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(0.035, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
      lastTime = now;
      t += dt;

      const audio = audioRef.current;
      sBass += (audio.bass - sBass) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
      sMid += (audio.mid - sMid) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);
      sTreble += (audio.treble - sTreble) * Math.min(1, dt * 16);

      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      const w = canvas.clientWidth, h = canvas.clientHeight;
      if (canvas.width !== Math.round(w * dpr) || canvas.height !== Math.round(h * dpr)) {
        canvas.width = Math.round(w * dpr);
        canvas.height = Math.round(h * dpr);
      }
      const aspect = Math.max(0.1, w / Math.max(1, h));

      const curShape = shapeRef.current;
      let targetShapeIdx = 0;
      if (curShape === "helix") targetShapeIdx = 1;
      else if (curShape === "torus") targetShapeIdx = 2;
      else if (curShape === "shield") targetShapeIdx = 3;

      // PURE SOLAR 3D ROTATION: Sthir Center rotating 360-degrees on its axis (ZERO tilt, ZERO flattening)
      const rotY = t * (0.32 + sMid * 0.4);
      const cy = Math.cos(rotY), sy = Math.sin(rotY);
      const voiceBoost = 1.0 + sBass * 0.16 + sMid * 0.08;
      const springK = 18.0;
      const damping = 5.0;

      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        let tx0 = s0X[i], ty0 = s0Y[i], tz0 = s0Z[i];
        if (targetShapeIdx === 1) { tx0 = s1X[i]; ty0 = s1Y[i]; tz0 = s1Z[i]; }
        else if (targetShapeIdx === 2) { tx0 = s2X[i]; ty0 = s2Y[i]; tz0 = s2Z[i]; }
        else if (targetShapeIdx === 3) { tx0 = s3X[i]; ty0 = s3Y[i]; tz0 = s3Z[i]; }

        // Pure axial rotation around Y (like the Sun rotating on its axis, ZERO tilt):
        const x1 = tx0 * cy - tz0 * sy;
        const y2 = ty0; // Perfectly anchored vertical axis
        const z2 = tx0 * sy + tz0 * cy;

        const wave = 1.0 + 0.02 * Math.sin(pPhase[i] + t * 4.0);
        const scale = voiceBoost * wave;

        const targetX = x1 * scale;
        const targetY = y2 * scale;
        const targetZ = z2 * scale;

        velX[i] += ((targetX - posX[i]) * springK - velX[i] * damping) * dt;
        velY[i] += ((targetY - posY[i]) * springK - velY[i] * damping) * dt;
        velZ[i] += ((targetZ - posZ[i]) * springK - velZ[i] * damping) * dt;

        posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
        posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
        posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;

        const o = i * 8;
        vertexData[o] = posX[i];
        vertexData[o + 1] = posY[i];
        vertexData[o + 2] = posZ[i];
        vertexData[o + 3] = colR[i];
        vertexData[o + 4] = colG[i];
        vertexData[o + 5] = colB[i];
        vertexData[o + 6] = sizes[i] * (1.0 + sBass * 0.40);
        vertexData[o + 7] = alphas[i];
      }

      gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);

      gl.useProgram(program);
      gl.uniform1f(uAspectLoc, aspect);
      gl.uniform1f(uDprLoc, dpr);
      gl.uniform1f(uTimeLoc, t);
      gl.uniform3f(uAudioLoc, sBass, sMid, sTreble);
      gl.uniform2f(uMouseLoc, mousePosRef.current[0], mousePosRef.current[1]);
      gl.uniform1f(uMouseHoverLoc, mouseHoverRef.current);

      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
      gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, 0, vertexData);
      gl.drawArrays(gl.POINTS, 0, COUNT);
      gl.bindVertexArray(null);

      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      if (gl && program) gl.deleteProgram(program);
      if (gl && vbo) gl.deleteBuffer(vbo);
      if (gl && vao) gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
    };
  }, []);

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLCanvasElement>) => {
    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    const nx = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
    const ny = -(((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 - 1);
    mousePosRef.current = [nx, ny];
    mouseHoverRef.current = 1.0;
  };

  const handlePointerLeave = () => {
    mouseHoverRef.current = 0.0;
  };

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      onClick={onClick}
      onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
      onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}
      aria-label="DarkShadow Next-Gen 3D Particle Simulation"
      className="w-full h-full block cursor-pointer"
    />
  );
}



======================================================================
FILE: src\components\ParticleOrbWebGL.tsx
======================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";

export type ParticlePhase =
  | "dormant"
  | "rising"
  | "listening"
  | "processing"
  | "speaking"
  | "alert"
  | "dissolving";

export type ParticleShape = "sphere" | "helix" | "torus" | "shield";

export interface AudioBands {
  bass: number;
  mid: number;
  treble: number;
}

interface ParticleOrbWebGLProps {
  phase: ParticlePhase;
  shape: ParticleShape;
  audioBands: AudioBands;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  onRiseComplete?: () => void;
  onDissolveComplete?: () => void;
  onClick?: () => void;
}

const VERTEX_SHADER = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 aPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 aColor;
layout(location = 2) in float aSize;
layout(location = 3) in float aAlpha;

uniform float uAspect;
uniform float uDpr;

out vec3 vColor;
out float vAlpha;

void main() {
  float zCam = 2.6 - aPos.z;
  float persp = 1.9 / max(0.4, zCam);
  vec2 projected = vec2(aPos.x / uAspect, aPos.y) * persp;
  gl_Position = vec4(projected, 0.0, 1.0);
  gl_PointSize = clamp(aSize * persp * uDpr, 1.4, 24.0);
  vColor = aColor;
  vAlpha = aAlpha * clamp(persp * 0.9, 0.25, 1.0);
}
`;

const FRAGMENT_SHADER = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

in vec3 vColor;
in float vAlpha;
out vec4 fragColor;

void main() {
  vec2 coord = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
  float distSq = dot(coord, coord);
  if (distSq > 0.25) discard;

  float core = exp(-distSq * 24.0);
  float halo = exp(-distSq * 6.2) * 0.58;
  float intensity = core + halo;
  vec3 finalCol = mix(vColor, vec3(1.0, 1.0, 1.0), core * 0.68);
  fragColor = vec4(finalCol * intensity, clamp(intensity * vAlpha, 0.0, 1.0));
}
`;

function compileShader(gl: WebGL2RenderingContext, type: number, src: string): WebGLShader | null {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, src);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (!gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
    gl.deleteShader(shader);
    return null;
  }
  return shader;
}

/**
 * Upgrade #5 GPU WebGL2 6,000-particle engine matching desktop_python/particle_engine.html.
 * Supports 4 live 3D morph shapes (Fibonacci Sphere, DNA Helix + Atom Rings, Plasma Torus, Shield Cube)
 * and real-time 3-band FFT audio reactivity (bass, mid, treble).
 */
export function ParticleOrbWebGL({
  phase,
  shape,
  audioBands,
  reducedMotion,
  onRiseComplete,
  onDissolveComplete,
  onClick,
}: ParticleOrbWebGLProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);
  const phaseRef = useRef<ParticlePhase>(phase);
  const prevPhaseRef = useRef<ParticlePhase>(phase);
  const shapeRef = useRef<ParticleShape>(shape);
  const bandsRef = useRef<AudioBands>(audioBands);
  const reducedRef = useRef<boolean>(reducedMotion);
  const onRiseCompleteRef = useRef(onRiseComplete);
  const onDissolveCompleteRef = useRef(onDissolveComplete);

  phaseRef.current = phase;
  shapeRef.current = shape;
  bandsRef.current = audioBands;
  reducedRef.current = reducedMotion;
  onRiseCompleteRef.current = onRiseComplete;
  onDissolveCompleteRef.current = onDissolveComplete;

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;

    const gl = canvas.getContext("webgl2", {
      alpha: true,
      antialias: false,
      premultipliedAlpha: false,
    });
    const ctx2d = gl ? null : canvas.getContext("2d");

    let program: WebGLProgram | null = null;
    let vao: WebGLVertexArrayObject | null = null;
    let vbo: WebGLBuffer | null = null;
    let uAspectLoc: WebGLUniformLocation | null = null;
    let uDprLoc: WebGLUniformLocation | null = null;

    const COUNT = 6000;
    const posX = new Float32Array(COUNT), posY = new Float32Array(COUNT), posZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const velX = new Float32Array(COUNT), velY = new Float32Array(COUNT), velZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const botX = new Float32Array(COUNT), botY = new Float32Array(COUNT), botZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);

    const sphX = new Float32Array(COUNT), sphY = new Float32Array(COUNT), sphZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const helX = new Float32Array(COUNT), helY = new Float32Array(COUNT), helZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const torX = new Float32Array(COUNT), torY = new Float32Array(COUNT), torZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const cubX = new Float32Array(COUNT), cubY = new Float32Array(COUNT), cubZ = new Float32Array(COUNT);

    const seed = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const pPhase = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const sizes = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const alphas = new Float32Array(COUNT);
    const vertexData = new Float32Array(COUNT * 8);

    const golden = Math.PI * (1 + Math.sqrt(5));
    for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
      const idx = i + 0.5;
      const r1 = Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453;
      const s = r1 - Math.floor(r1);
      const r2 = Math.sin(i * 78.233 + 1.7) * 43758.5453;
      const s2 = r2 - Math.floor(r2);
      const r3 = Math.sin(i * 39.425 + 4.3) * 43758.5453;
      const s3 = r3 - Math.floor(r3);

      seed[i] = s;
      pPhase[i] = s2 * Math.PI * 2;
      sizes[i] = 1.9 + s3 * 3.1;

      botX[i] = (s2 * 2 - 1) * 1.15;
      botY[i] = -1.18 - s * 0.38;
      botZ[i] = (s3 * 2 - 1) * 0.4;
      posX[i] = botX[i]; posY[i] = botY[i]; posZ[i] = botZ[i];

      // 0: Fibonacci Sphere
      const phi = Math.acos(1 - (2 * idx) / COUNT);
      const theta = golden * idx;
      sphX[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta) * 0.44;
      sphY[i] = Math.cos(phi) * 0.44;
      sphZ[i] = Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * 0.44;

      // 1: DNA Double Helix + Orbital Atom Rings
      if (i < COUNT * 0.65) {
        const tH = (i / (COUNT * 0.65)) * 2 - 1;
        const strand = i % 2 === 0 ? 0 : Math.PI;
        const twist = tH * Math.PI * 3.2 + strand;
        const rH = 0.24 + (s3 - 0.5) * 0.04;
        helX[i] = Math.cos(twist) * rH;
        helY[i] = tH * 0.52;
        helZ[i] = Math.sin(twist) * rH;
      } else {
        const ringIdx = i % 3;
        const a = s2 * Math.PI * 2;
        const rr = 0.48 + (s3 - 0.5) * 0.03;
        const rx = Math.cos(a) * rr;
        const rz = Math.sin(a) * rr;
        const tilt = ringIdx === 0 ? 0.6 : ringIdx === 1 ? -0.6 : 1.5;
        helX[i] = rx;
        helY[i] = -rz * Math.sin(tilt);
        helZ[i] = rz * Math.cos(tilt);
      }

      // 2: Plasma Audio Torus
      const u = s2 * Math.PI * 2;
      const v = s3 * Math.PI * 2;
      const R = 0.36;
      const r = 0.14;
      torX[i] = (R + r * Math.cos(v)) * Math.cos(u);
      torY[i] = r * Math.sin(v) * 1.15;
      torZ[i] = (R + r * Math.cos(v)) * Math.sin(u);

      // 3: Geometric Shield Cube
      const face = i % 6;
      const a1 = (s2 * 2 - 1) * 0.36;
      const a2 = (s3 * 2 - 1) * 0.36;
      if (face === 0) { cubX[i] = 0.36; cubY[i] = a1; cubZ[i] = a2; }
      else if (face === 1) { cubX[i] = -0.36; cubY[i] = a1; cubZ[i] = a2; }
      else if (face === 2) { cubX[i] = a1; cubY[i] = 0.36; cubZ[i] = a2; }
      else if (face === 3) { cubX[i] = a1; cubY[i] = -0.36; cubZ[i] = a2; }
      else if (face === 4) { cubX[i] = a1; cubY[i] = a2; cubZ[i] = 0.36; }
      else { cubX[i] = a1; cubY[i] = a2; cubZ[i] = -0.36; }
    }

    if (gl) {
      const vs = compileShader(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX_SHADER);
      const fs = compileShader(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT_SHADER);
      if (vs && fs) {
        program = gl.createProgram();
        if (program) {
          gl.attachShader(program, vs);
          gl.attachShader(program, fs);
          gl.linkProgram(program);
          if (gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
            uAspectLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uAspect");
            uDprLoc = gl.getUniformLocation(program, "uDpr");
            vao = gl.createVertexArray();
            vbo = gl.createBuffer();
            gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
            gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
            gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vertexData.byteLength, gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
            const stride = 32;
            gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0); gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 0);
            gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1); gl.vertexAttribPointer(1, 3, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 12);
            gl.enableVertexAttribArray(2); gl.vertexAttribPointer(2, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 24);
            gl.enableVertexAttribArray(3); gl.vertexAttribPointer(3, 1, gl.FLOAT, false, stride, 28);
            gl.bindVertexArray(0);
            gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
            gl.blendFunc(gl.SRC_ALPHA, gl.ONE);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    let rafId = 0;
    let t = 0;
    let stateTime = 0;
    let sBass = 0, sMid = 0, sTreble = 0;
    let riseSignaled = false;
    let dissolveSignaled = false;
    let lastNow = performance.now();

    const resetForRising = () => {
      riseSignaled = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        posX[i] = botX[i]; posY[i] = botY[i]; posZ[i] = botZ[i];
        velX[i] = -botX[i] * 0.45;
        velY[i] = 1.75 + seed[i] * 1.45;
        velZ[i] = Math.sin(pPhase[i]) * 0.35;
        alphas[i] = 0;
      }
    };

    if (phaseRef.current === "rising") resetForRising();

    const animate = (now: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(0.04, (now - lastNow) / 1000);
      lastNow = now;

      const curPhase = phaseRef.current;
      const curShape = shapeRef.current;
      if (curPhase !== prevPhaseRef.current) {
        stateTime = 0;
        if (curPhase === "rising") resetForRising();
        else if (curPhase === "dissolving") dissolveSignaled = false;
        prevPhaseRef.current = curPhase;
      }

      const speedScale = reducedRef.current ? 0.3 : 1.0;
      t += dt * speedScale;
      stateTime += dt;

      const audioActive = curPhase === "listening" || curPhase === "speaking";
      const b = audioActive ? bandsRef.current.bass : 0;
      const m = audioActive ? bandsRef.current.mid : 0;
      const tr = audioActive ? bandsRef.current.treble : 0;
      sBass += (b - sBass) * Math.min(1, dt * 15);
      sMid += (m - sMid) * Math.min(1, dt * 15);
      sTreble += (tr - sTreble) * Math.min(1, dt * 18);

      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      const cssW = canvas.clientWidth || 640;
      const cssH = canvas.clientHeight || 480;
      const pxW = Math.round(cssW * dpr);
      const pxH = Math.round(cssH * dpr);
      if (canvas.width !== pxW || canvas.height !== pxH) {
        canvas.width = pxW;
        canvas.height = pxH;
      }

      const txArr = curShape === "helix" ? helX : curShape === "torus" ? torX : curShape === "shield" ? cubX : sphX;
      const tyArr = curShape === "helix" ? helY : curShape === "torus" ? torY : curShape === "shield" ? cubY : sphY;
      const tzArr = curShape === "helix" ? helZ : curShape === "torus" ? torZ : curShape === "shield" ? cubZ : sphZ;

      // PURE SOLAR 3D ROTATION: Sthir Center rotating 360-degrees on its axis (ZERO tilt, ZERO flattening)
      const rotY = t * (curPhase === "processing" ? 0.8 : 0.35 + sMid * 0.4);
      const cy = Math.cos(rotY), sy = Math.sin(rotY);

      let c1r = 0.0, c1g = 0.82, c1b = 1.0;
      let c2r = 0.46, c2g = 0.36, c2b = 1.0;
      if (curPhase === "processing" || curShape === "helix") {
        c1r = 0.84; c1g = 0.34; c1b = 1.0;
        c2r = 0.42; c2g = 0.34; c2b = 1.0;
      } else if (curPhase === "speaking" || curShape === "torus") {
        c1r = 0.0; c1g = 0.9; c1b = 1.0;
        c2r = 0.65; c2g = 0.4; c2b = 1.0;
      } else if (curPhase === "alert" || curShape === "shield") {
        c1r = 1.0; c1g = 0.28; c1b = 0.4;
        c2r = 1.0; c2g = 0.62; c2b = 0.22;
      }

      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const ox = txArr[i], oy = tyArr[i], oz = tzArr[i];
        // Pure axial rotation around Y (like the Sun rotating on its axis, ZERO tilt):
        const x1 = ox * cy - oz * sy;
        const y2 = oy; // Perfectly anchored vertical axis, never tilts or flattens
        const z2 = ox * sy + oz * cy;

        // Pure isotropic 3D solar breathing (Uniform in all 3 axes, 100% spherical roundness):
        const scale = 1.0 + sBass * 0.16 + sMid * 0.08 + 0.015 * Math.sin(pPhase[i] + t * 3.5);

        const gx = x1 * scale;
        const gy = y2 * scale;
        const gz = z2 * scale;

        if (curPhase === "dormant") {
          const driftX = botX[i] * 0.85 + Math.sin(t * 0.8 + pPhase[i]) * 0.05;
          const driftY = -0.96 + seed[i] * 0.09 + Math.sin(t * 1.9 + pPhase[i]) * 0.025;
          posX[i] += (driftX - posX[i]) * Math.min(1, dt * 3.5);
          posY[i] += (driftY - posY[i]) * Math.min(1, dt * 3.5);
          posZ[i] += (botZ[i] * 0.4 - posZ[i]) * Math.min(1, dt * 3.5);
          const targetA = seed[i] < 0.2 ? 0.28 + 0.2 * Math.sin(t * 2.2 + pPhase[i]) : 0;
          alphas[i] += (targetA - alphas[i]) * Math.min(1, dt * 4);
        } else if (curPhase === "rising") {
          const delay = seed[i] * 0.4;
          const progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (stateTime - delay) / 1.15));
          if (progress > 0) {
            const swirl = (1 - progress) * 2.4;
            const k = 8.0 * Math.pow(progress, 1.5);
            velX[i] += ((gx - posX[i]) * k - velX[i] * 3.8 - posZ[i] * swirl) * dt;
            velY[i] += ((gy - posY[i]) * k - velY[i] * 3.8) * dt;
            velZ[i] += ((gz - posZ[i]) * k - velZ[i] * 3.8 + posX[i] * swirl) * dt;
            posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
            posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
            posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;
            alphas[i] = Math.min(0.95, progress * 1.45);
          }
        } else if (curPhase === "dissolving") {
          velY[i] -= (2.4 + seed[i] * 1.8) * dt;
          velX[i] += Math.sin(t * 5.2 + pPhase[i]) * 0.5 * dt;
          posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
          posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
          posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;
          alphas[i] = Math.max(0, 1 - stateTime / 1.15);
        } else {
          const k = curPhase === "speaking" ? 14.5 : 11.5;
          const tang = 0.14 + sMid * 0.9;
          velX[i] += ((gx - posX[i]) * k - velX[i] * 5.0 - z2 * tang) * dt;
          velY[i] += ((gy - posY[i]) * k - velY[i] * 5.0) * dt;
          velZ[i] += ((gz - posZ[i]) * k - velZ[i] * 5.0 + x1 * tang) * dt;
          posX[i] += velX[i] * dt;
          posY[i] += velY[i] * dt;
          posZ[i] += velZ[i] * dt;
          alphas[i] = Math.min(0.96, alphas[i] + dt * 3.2);
        }

        const mix = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(pPhase[i] + t * 1.6 + y2 * 3.0);
        const o = i * 8;
        vertexData[o] = posX[i]; vertexData[o + 1] = posY[i]; vertexData[o + 2] = posZ[i];
        vertexData[o + 3] = c1r * mix + c2r * (1 - mix);
        vertexData[o + 4] = c1g * mix + c2g * (1 - mix);
        vertexData[o + 5] = c1b * mix + c2b * (1 - mix);
        vertexData[o + 6] = sizes[i] * (1.0 + sBass * 0.55 + sTreble * 0.4);
        vertexData[o + 7] = alphas[i];
      }

      if (curPhase === "rising" && stateTime >= 1.6 && !riseSignaled) {
        riseSignaled = true;
        onRiseCompleteRef.current?.();
      }
      if (curPhase === "dissolving" && stateTime >= 1.2 && !dissolveSignaled) {
        dissolveSignaled = true;
        onDissolveCompleteRef.current?.();
      }

      if (gl && program && vao && vbo) {
        gl.viewport(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
        gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
        gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
        gl.useProgram(program);
        gl.uniform1f(uAspectLoc, Math.max(0.1, cssW / Math.max(1, cssH)));
        gl.uniform1f(uDprLoc, dpr);
        gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
        gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
        gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, 0, vertexData);
        gl.drawArrays(gl.POINTS, 0, COUNT);
        gl.bindVertexArray(0);
      } else if (ctx2d) {
        ctx2d.clearRect(0, 0, pxW, pxH);
        const cx2 = pxW * 0.5, cy2 = pxH * 0.5;
        const sc = Math.min(pxW, pxH) * 0.48;
        for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i += 2) {
          const a = alphas[i];
          if (a < 0.03) continue;
          const persp = 1.9 / Math.max(0.4, 2.6 - posZ[i]);
          const o = i * 8;
          ctx2d.fillStyle = `rgba(${Math.round(vertexData[o + 3] * 255)},${Math.round(vertexData[o + 4] * 255)},${Math.round(vertexData[o + 5] * 255)},${a.toFixed(2)})`;
          ctx2d.beginPath();
          ctx2d.arc(cx2 + posX[i] * persp * sc, cy2 - posY[i] * persp * sc, Math.max(1, sizes[i] * persp * 0.4), 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx2d.fill();
        }
      }

      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      if (gl && program) gl.deleteProgram(program);
      if (gl && vbo) gl.deleteBuffer(vbo);
      if (gl && vao) gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-label="DarkShadow 3D Particle Simulation"
      className="w-full h-full block cursor-pointer"
    />
  );
}

FILE: src\components\RemindersModal.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import type { MemoryItem, ReminderItem } from "../types.ts";

interface RemindersModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  reminders: ReminderItem[];
  memories: MemoryItem[];
  privacyMode: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onDeleteReminder: (id: number) => void;
  onDeleteMemory: (id: number) => void;
}

/**
 * Upcoming reminders & explicit memory vault modal ported from ui.py's ReminderDialog.
 */
export function RemindersModal({
  open,
  reminders,
  memories,
  privacyMode,
  onClose,
  onDeleteReminder,
  onDeleteMemory,
}: RemindersModalProps) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"reminders" | "memories">("reminders");

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs p-4"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-[460px] rounded-2xl bg-[#000000] border border-[#181E2C] p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-[18px] font-semibold text-[#F8F9FD]">
            {tab === "reminders" ? "Upcoming reminders" : "Saved memories & notes"}
          </h2>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white px-2 py-1 text-lg leading-none cursor-pointer"
            aria-label="Close modal"
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 border-b border-[#181E2C] pb-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab("reminders")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              tab === "reminders"
                ? "bg-[#765CFF]/20 text-[#F8F9FD] border border-[#765CFF]/50"
                : "text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Reminders ({reminders.length})
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTab("memories")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[12px] font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
              tab === "memories"
                ? "bg-[#765CFF]/20 text-[#F8F9FD] border border-[#765CFF]/50"
                : "text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Memories &amp; Notes ({privacyMode ? "Paused" : memories.length})
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1">
          {tab === "reminders" ? (
            reminders.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#8A90A4] py-4 leading-relaxed">
                No upcoming reminders. Try typing:{" "}
                <span className="text-[#80D8FF]">
                  &ldquo;Remind me tomorrow at 09:00 to call Rahul&rdquo;
                </span>{" "}
                or{" "}
                <span className="text-[#80D8FF]">
                  &ldquo;Remind me in 5 minutes to drink water&rdquo;
                </span>
              </p>
            ) : (
              reminders.map((r) => (
                <div
                  key={r.id}
                  className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] p-3"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-[#00D2FF] font-medium">
                      #{r.id} •{" "}
                      {new Date(r.dueAt).toLocaleString("en-IN", {
                        dateStyle: "medium",
                        timeStyle: "short",
                      })}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[13px] text-[#F8F9FD] mt-0.5 break-words">
                      {r.message}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onDeleteReminder(r.id)}
                    title="Delete reminder"
                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#FF4560] hover:bg-[#1B1018] transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))
            )
          ) : privacyMode ? (
            <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#8A90A4] py-4">
              Privacy mode is active — saved memory access is paused for this session.
            </p>
          ) : memories.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-[12.5px] text-[#8A90A4] py-4 leading-relaxed">
              No explicit memories saved yet. Try:{" "}
              <span className="text-[#80D8FF]">
                &ldquo;remember this: I prefer concise answers&rdquo;
              </span>{" "}
              or{" "}
              <span className="text-[#80D8FF]">&ldquo;/note Call the office&rdquo;</span>
            </p>
          ) : (
            memories.map((m) => (
              <div
                key={m.id}
                className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] p-3"
              >
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-[10.5px] uppercase tracking-wider text-[#765CFF] font-semibold">
                    #{m.id} • {m.kind}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[13px] text-[#F8F9FD] mt-0.5 break-words">
                    {m.content}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onDeleteMemory(m.id)}
                  title="Forget item"
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-[#8A90A4] hover:text-[#FF4560] hover:bg-[#1B1018] transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end pt-1">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-[#070A10] border border-[#181E2C] hover:border-[#765CFF] text-[12px] text-[#F8F9FD] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            Close
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



======================================================================
FILE: src\components\TitleBar.tsx
======================================================================
import { Clock, Download, Settings } from "lucide-react";

interface TitleBarProps {
  pendingReminders: number;
  privacyMode: boolean;
  onOpenReminders: () => void;
  onOpenSettings: () => void;
  onOpenDesktopDownload: () => void;
}

/**
 * Custom TitleBar with AppMark orb glyph matching ui.py + Windows Python Desktop download action.
 */
export function TitleBar({
  pendingReminders,
  privacyMode,
  onOpenReminders,
  onOpenSettings,
  onOpenDesktopDownload,
}: TitleBarProps) {
  return (
    <header className="h-[52px] shrink-0 flex items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-5 border-b border-[#181E2C]/70 select-none z-20 relative bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
        <svg
          width="24"
          height="24"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="ds-mark-grad" x1="2" y1="2" x2="22" y2="22" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#00D2FF" />
              <stop offset="45%" stopColor="#765CFF" />
              <stop offset="80%" stopColor="#A95BFF" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#00D2FF" />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8" fill="#080B16" stroke="url(#ds-mark-grad)" strokeWidth="2.2" />
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="2.2" fill="#7869FF" />
        </svg>
        <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-[#F8F9FD]">
          DarkShadow
        </span>
        {privacyMode && (
          <span className="ml-2 text-[11px] text-[#8A90A4] border border-[#181E2C] bg-[#070A10] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md">
            Privacy Mode
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenDesktopDownload}
          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#070A10] border border-[#00D2FF]/40 hover:border-[#00D2FF] hover:bg-[#0F1422] text-[11.5px] font-medium text-[#80D8FF] transition-colors cursor-pointer mr-1"
        >
          <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">Windows Python (.py)</span>
          <span className="sm:hidden">.py</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenReminders}
          title="Reminders & Memories"
          aria-label="Reminders and memories"
          className="relative flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-9 rounded-lg text-[#AEB4C6] hover:text-white hover:bg-[#0F1422] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          <Clock className="w-[17px] h-[17px]" />
          {pendingReminders > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 min-w-[14px] h-[14px] px-1 rounded-full bg-[#765CFF] text-[9px] font-semibold text-white flex items-center justify-center">
              {pendingReminders}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onOpenSettings}
          title="Settings and privacy"
          aria-label="Settings and privacy"
          className="flex items-center justify-center w-10 h-9 rounded-lg text-[#AEB4C6] hover:text-white hover:bg-[#0F1422] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          <Settings className="w-[17px] h-[17px]" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
